1/-
2# HRS.Dynamics.InitialData — initial modulated energy and initial fluctuation data (APPA-02)
3
4Appendix A, Section "Verification: initial data" of the main manuscript (source lines
511490-11640), together with the static parts of Examples A.16/A.17 (source lines 10450-10483)
6and the elementary scale limits `H:eq:balanced-weights` (source lines 636-646).
7
8* Lemma A.35 `M:B:lem:initial-energy` (lines 11498-11513): (i) first display (uniform form),
9  second display (`B:eq:initial-energy-second-moment`), the "automatic" squared-kernel
10  condition, and the static content of (ii).  The exact expectation identity of (i) is
11  `HRS.expectation_modulatedEnergy_iid` (ENER-01) and is not restated.
12* Lemma A.36 `M:B:lem:iid-init` (lines 11544-11550): (I2) and the finite-family coupling
13  `B:eq:iid-coupling`.
14* Lemma A.38 `M:B:lem:iid-init-field` (lines 11581-11587): the field-level coupling
15  `B:eq:iid-init-field` and the second-moment bound.  (Lemma A.37, Yurinskii, is
16  `HRS.yurinskii_coupling` in `HRS/Prob/Metrics.lean`.)
17* Example A.39 `M:B:ex:lattice` (lines 11608-11612): the perturbed-lattice statistic and field
18  estimates and the (external) lattice energy bound.
19* Example A.17 `M:B:ex:iid` (lines 10469-10483): existence of the Gaussian initial field with
20  the bridge covariance; the central-limit scaling condition `s < d/2`.
21* `H:eq:balanced-weights` and the two degenerate limits (lines 636-646).
22
23Representation conventions.
24* `μ° = ρ dx` with `ρ` a probability density bounded by `Λ` (`μ° ∈ 𝒫 ∩ L^∞`).
25* The Gaussian field `η°` is a law `ν` on `E_m = H^{-m}_0(𝕋^d)` with
26  `IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m ν (iidCov μ°)`.
27* Second moments are `lintegral`s, so a non-integrable integrand cannot make a bound vacuous;
28  couplings are random elements on an explicit probability space with prescribed laws.
29-/

46/-! ### Lemma A.35: initial modulated energy -/
47
48/-- `o'_N = 1_{s=0} log N / N + Λ^{s/d} N^{s/d-1}` (Lemma A.35 (i), source line 11502; the
49constant `C` in front of the second summand is absorbed into the constants of the statements). -/
50def oPrime (d : ℕ) (s Λ : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℝ :=
51  (if s = 0 then Real.log N / N else 0) + Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)
52
53namespace InitialDataAux

55lemma isProb_withDensityReal {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) :
56    IsProbabilityMeasure (withDensityReal ρ) := by
57  have hint : Integrable ρ := Integrable.of_integral_ne_zero (by rw [hρ.integral_one]; exact one_ne_zero)
58  refine ⟨?_⟩
59  rw [withDensityReal, withDensity_apply _ MeasurableSet.univ, Measure.restrict_univ,
60    ← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hρ.nonneg),
61    hρ.integral_one]
62  simp
63
64lemma one_le_of_probDensity {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ)
65    (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) : 1 ≤ Λ := by
66  have hint : Integrable ρ := Integrable.of_integral_ne_zero (by rw [hρ.integral_one]; exact one_ne_zero)
67  have := integral_mono hint (integrable_const Λ) hΛ
68  rw [hρ.integral_one] at this
69  simpa using this

71lemma withDensityReal_ac (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) : withDensityReal ρ ≪ (volume : Measure (Torus d)) :=
72  withDensity_absolutelyContinuous _ _
73
74lemma integrable_energy (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
75    (hint : Integrable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => g (p.1 - p.2)) (μ.prod μ)) (N : ℕ) :
76    Integrable (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergyKernel g X μ)
77      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
78  have hpair : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
79      Integrable (fun X : Config d N => g (X i - X j)) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
80    fun i j hij => integrable_pair_comp μ hij (H := fun p => g (p.1 - p.2)) hint
81  have hconv_int : Integrable (fun x => ∫ y, g (x - y) ∂μ) μ := hint.integral_prod_left
82  have h1 : ∀ i : Fin N, Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∫ y, g (X i - y) ∂μ)
83      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := fun i =>
84    integrable_comp_eval (μ := fun _ : Fin N => μ) (i := i) hconv_int
85  have hT1 : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, g (X i - X j))
86      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
87    integrable_finsetSum _ fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j hj =>
88      hpair i j (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj).symm
89  have hT2 : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∫ y, g (X i - y) ∂μ)
90      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := integrable_finsetSum _ fun i _ => h1 i
91  unfold modulatedEnergyKernel modulatedEnergy
92  exact ((hT1.const_mul _).sub (hT2.const_mul _)).add (integrable_const _)
93
94lemma integrable_conv_shift {g : Torus d → ℝ} (hg : Integrable g) (hgm : AEStronglyMeasurable g volume)
95    {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (x : Torus d) :
96    Integrable (fun y => g (x - y)) (withDensityReal ρ) := by
97  have : (volume : Measure (Torus d)).IsNegInvariant :=
98    MeasureTheory.Measure.pi.isNegInvariant (fun _ : Fin d => (volume : Measure UnitAddCircle))
99  have hqmp : Measure.QuasiMeasurePreserving (fun y : Torus d => x - y) (withDensityReal ρ) volume :=
100    ((volume : Measure (Torus d)).measurePreserving_sub_left x).quasiMeasurePreserving.mono_left
101      (withDensityReal_ac ρ)
102  refine ⟨hgm.comp_quasiMeasurePreserving hqmp, ?_⟩
103  unfold HasFiniteIntegral
104  refine lt_of_le_of_lt (KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ‖g z‖ₑ) x) ?_
105  exact ENNReal.mul_lt_top ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top hg.hasFiniteIntegral

108lemma oN_abs_le {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (C₀ Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : 1 ≤ Λ) (N : ℕ) (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
109    (N : ℝ)⁻¹ ≤ oPrime d s Λ N ∧ |oN d s C₀ N| ≤ (|C₀| + 1) * oPrime d s Λ N ∧ 0 ≤ oPrime d s Λ N := by
110  have hdR : (2 : ℝ) ≤ d := by exact_mod_cast hd
111  have hdpos : (0 : ℝ) < d := by linarith
112  have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
113  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
114  set q : ℝ := (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) with hq
115  have hqpos : 0 < q := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hNpos _
116  have hinv : (N : ℝ)⁻¹ ≤ q := by
117    rw [hq, ← Real.rpow_neg_one]
118    have h0 : 0 ≤ s / d := by positivity
119    exact Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hN1 (by linarith)
120  have hΛq : 1 ≤ Λ ^ (s / (d : ℝ)) := Real.one_le_rpow hΛ (by positivity)
121  set L : ℝ := (if s = 0 then Real.log N / N else 0) with hL
122  have hL0 : 0 ≤ L := by
123    rw [hL]; split_ifs
124    · exact div_nonneg (Real.log_nonneg hN1) hNpos.le
125    · exact le_rfl
126  have hop : oPrime d s Λ N = L + Λ ^ (s / (d : ℝ)) * q := rfl
127  have hop1 : q ≤ Λ ^ (s / (d : ℝ)) * q := by nlinarith
128  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
129  · rw [hop]; linarith
130  · have hoN : oN d s C₀ N = C₀ * q + (if s = 0 then Real.log N / (2 * d * N) else 0) := rfl
131    have h2 : |(if s = 0 then Real.log N / (2 * d * N) else 0)| ≤ L := by
132      rw [hL]; split_ifs
133      · rw [abs_of_nonneg (div_nonneg (Real.log_nonneg hN1) (by positivity))]
134        apply div_le_div_of_nonneg_left (Real.log_nonneg hN1) hNpos
135        nlinarith
136      · simp
137    calc |oN d s C₀ N| ≤ |C₀ * q| + |(if s = 0 then Real.log N / (2 * d * N) else 0)| := by
138          rw [hoN]; exact abs_add_le _ _
139      _ ≤ |C₀| * q + L := by rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos hqpos]; linarith
140      _ ≤ (|C₀| + 1) * (L + Λ ^ (s / (d : ℝ)) * q) := by
141          nlinarith [abs_nonneg C₀, mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg C₀) hL0]
142      _ = _ := by rw [hop]
143  · rw [hop]; positivity
144
145end InitialDataAux

147open InitialDataAux in
148/-- Lemma A.35 (i), first display (source lines 11501-11502): for i.i.d. data
149`X ∼ (μ°)^{⊗N}` with `μ° = ρ dx`, `ρ ≤ Λ`, `∬|g| d(μ°)^{⊗2} < ∞`,
150`𝓔_N^0 = E[F_N(X, μ°) + o_N] ≤ C (N⁻¹ + o_N) ≤ C o'_N`.  The constant depends only on
151`d, s, C₀, Λ` (uniform over the densities), and `F_N` is integrable, so the Bochner
152expectation is the true one. -/
153theorem initial_energy_iid_mean {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (C₀ Λ : ℝ) :
154    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
155      Integrable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2))
156        ((withDensityReal ρ).prod (withDensityReal ρ)) →
157      ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
158        Integrable (fun X : Config d N => rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ))
159            (Measure.pi fun _ => withDensityReal ρ) ∧
160          ∫ X : Config d N, (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oN d s C₀ N)
161              ∂(Measure.pi fun _ => withDensityReal ρ) ≤ C * ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ + |oN d s C₀ N|) ∧
162          ∫ X : Config d N, (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oN d s C₀ N)
163              ∂(Measure.pi fun _ => withDensityReal ρ) ≤ C * oPrime d s Λ N := by
164  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := periodicRiesz_ge (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
165  have hgi := KernelAux.integrable_periodicRiesz (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
166  have hgm := KernelAux.aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
167  refine ⟨|B| / 2 + |C₀| + 1, fun ρ hρ hΛ hint N hN => ?_⟩
168  have hprob := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
169  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
170  have hΛ1 := one_le_of_probDensity hρ Λ hΛ
171  have hF : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => rieszEnergy d s X μ) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
172    integrable_energy (periodicRiesz d s) μ hint N
173  have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
174  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN1
175  have hmean := expectation_modulatedEnergy_iid (periodicRiesz d s) μ (N := N) hN1 hint
176    (integrable_conv_shift hgi hgm Λ hΛ)
177  have hG : -|B| ≤ ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ∂(μ.prod μ) := by
178    have h := integral_mono (integrable_const (-|B|)) hint (f := fun _ => -|B|)
179      (fun p => (neg_le_neg (le_abs_self B)).trans (hB (p.1 - p.2)))
180    simpa using h
181  have hEF : ∫ X : Config d N, rieszEnergy d s X μ ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) ≤
182      |B| / 2 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
183    have : ∫ X : Config d N, rieszEnergy d s X μ ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) =
184        -(1 / (2 * (N : ℝ))) * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ∂(μ.prod μ) :=
185      hmean
186    rw [this]
187    have h1 : 0 < 1 / (2 * (N : ℝ)) := by positivity
188    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (neg_le_neg hG) h1.le
189    calc _ = -(1 / (2 * (N : ℝ))) * ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ∂(μ.prod μ) := rfl
190      _ = (1 / (2 * (N : ℝ))) * (-∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ∂(μ.prod μ)) := by ring
191      _ ≤ (1 / (2 * (N : ℝ))) * (-(-|B|)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (neg_le_neg hG) h1.le
192      _ = |B| / 2 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by field_simp
193  have hint_sum : ∫ X : Config d N, (rieszEnergy d s X μ + oN d s C₀ N) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) =
194      ∫ X : Config d N, rieszEnergy d s X μ ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) + oN d s C₀ N := by
195    rw [integral_add hF (integrable_const _), integral_const]
196    simp
197  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩ := oN_abs_le hd hs0 C₀ Λ hΛ1 N hN1
198  refine ⟨hF, ?_, ?_⟩
199  · rw [hint_sum]
200    have := le_abs_self (oN d s C₀ N)
201    have hB0 := abs_nonneg B
202    have hC0 := abs_nonneg C₀
203    have hinv : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by positivity
204    nlinarith [mul_nonneg hB0 hinv, mul_nonneg hC0 hinv, mul_nonneg hC0 (abs_nonneg (oN d s C₀ N)),
205      mul_nonneg hB0 (abs_nonneg (oN d s C₀ N)), abs_nonneg (oN d s C₀ N)]
206  · rw [hint_sum]
207    have := le_abs_self (oN d s C₀ N)
208    have hB0 := abs_nonneg B
209    have hC0 := abs_nonneg C₀
210    nlinarith [mul_nonneg hB0 h3, mul_nonneg hC0 h3]

216/-- local bound: `|g(x)| ≤ c |x|^{-b} + R` near the origin, `b = (s + d/2)/2`. -/
217lemma riesz_local_bound {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hs : s < (d : ℝ) / 2) :
218    ∃ (δ c R : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ δ ≤ 1 ∧ 0 ≤ c ∧ 0 ≤ R ∧ ∀ x : Euc d, x ≠ 0 → ‖x‖ < δ →
219      |periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk x)| ≤ c * ‖x‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2)) + R := by
220  have hdR : (2 : ℝ) ≤ d := by exact_mod_cast hd
221  set b : ℝ := (s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2 with hb
222  have hbpos : 0 < b := by rw [hb]; positivity
223  rcases hs0.eq_or_lt with h0 | h0
224  · subst h0
225    obtain ⟨r, δ, hδ, hr, hloc⟩ := periodicLog_local_singularity (d := d) hd
226    obtain ⟨R, hR⟩ := (isCompact_closedBall (0 : Euc d) 1).exists_bound_of_continuousOn
227      (f := Torus.lift r) (show ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift r) from hr).continuous.continuousOn
228    refine ⟨min δ 1, 1 / b, |R|, lt_min hδ one_pos, min_le_right _ _, by positivity, abs_nonneg _, ?_⟩
229    intro x hx0 hxn
230    have hxpos : 0 < ‖x‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hx0
231    have hx1 : ‖x‖ < 1 := lt_of_lt_of_le hxn (min_le_right _ _)
232    have hxδ : ‖x‖ < δ := lt_of_lt_of_le hxn (min_le_left _ _)
233    have hg := hloc x hxδ hx0
234    have hrR : |r (Torus.mk x)| ≤ |R| := by
235      have := hR x (by simpa using hx1.le)
236      simp only [Torus.lift, Function.comp, Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
237      exact this.trans (le_abs_self _)
238    have hlog : -Real.log ‖x‖ ≤ ‖x‖ ^ (-b) / b := by
239      have := Real.log_le_rpow_div (inv_nonneg.2 hxpos.le) hbpos
240      rw [Real.log_inv, Real.inv_rpow hxpos.le, ← Real.rpow_neg hxpos.le] at this
241      exact this
242    have hlog0 : 0 ≤ -Real.log ‖x‖ := by
243      have := Real.log_neg hxpos hx1; linarith
244    rw [hg]
245    calc _ ≤ |-Real.log ‖x‖| + |r (Torus.mk x)| := abs_add_le _ _
246      _ ≤ _ := by
247        rw [abs_of_nonneg hlog0]
248        have : 1 / b * ‖x‖ ^ (-b) = ‖x‖ ^ (-b) / b := by ring
249        simp only [zero_add] at *
250        linarith
251  · obtain ⟨r, δ, hδ, hr, hloc⟩ := periodicRiesz_local_singularity (d := d) (s := s) hd h0 (by linarith)
252    obtain ⟨R, hR⟩ := (isCompact_closedBall (0 : Euc d) 1).exists_bound_of_continuousOn
253      (f := Torus.lift r) (show ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift r) from hr).continuous.continuousOn
254    refine ⟨min δ 1, 1, |R|, lt_min hδ one_pos, min_le_right _ _, zero_le_one, abs_nonneg _, ?_⟩
255    intro x hx0 hxn
256    have hxpos : 0 < ‖x‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hx0
257    have hx1 : ‖x‖ < 1 := lt_of_lt_of_le hxn (min_le_right _ _)
258    have hxδ : ‖x‖ < δ := lt_of_lt_of_le hxn (min_le_left _ _)
259    have hg := hloc x hxδ hx0
260    have hrR : |r (Torus.mk x)| ≤ |R| := by
261      have := hR x (by simpa using hx1.le)
262      simp only [Torus.lift, Function.comp, Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
263      exact this.trans (le_abs_self _)
264    have h1 : ‖x‖ ^ (-s) ≤ ‖x‖ ^ (-b) :=
265      Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_ge hxpos hx1.le (by rw [hb]; linarith)
266    rw [hg]
267    calc _ ≤ |‖x‖ ^ (-s)| + |r (Torus.mk x)| := abs_add_le _ _
268      _ ≤ _ := by
269        rw [abs_of_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg hxpos.le _)]
270        linarith

273lemma riesz_sq_box {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hs : s < (d : ℝ) / 2) :
274    ∃ K1 K2 : ℝ, 0 ≤ K1 ∧ 0 ≤ K2 ∧ ∀ y : Fin d → ℝ, y ∈ Box d → y ≠ 0 →
275      periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) ^ 2 ≤ K1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + (d : ℝ) / 2)) + K2 := by
276  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
277  have hdR : (2 : ℝ) ≤ d := by exact_mod_cast hd
278  obtain ⟨δ, c, R, hδ, hδ1, hc, hR, hloc⟩ := riesz_local_bound (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hs
279  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := periodicRiesz_box_bound (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 (by linarith)
280  set θ : ℝ := δ / d with hθ
281  have hθ0 : 0 < θ := by positivity
282  set M : ℝ := C1 * θ ^ (-((s + (d:ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 with hM
283  have hM0 : 0 ≤ M := by positivity
284  refine ⟨2 * c ^ 2, M ^ 2 + 2 * R ^ 2, by positivity, by positivity, fun y hyB hy => ?_⟩
285  have hypos : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy
286  have hrp : 0 ≤ 2 * c ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + (d : ℝ) / 2)) :=
287    mul_nonneg (by positivity) (Real.rpow_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) _)
288  by_cases hcase : θ ≤ ‖y‖
289  · have h1 := hbd y hyB hy
290    have h2 : ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d:ℝ)) / 2)) ≤ θ ^ (-((s + (d:ℝ)) / 2)) :=
291      Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hθ0 hcase (by have : (0:ℝ) ≤ (s + (d:ℝ))/2 := by positivity
292                                                linarith)
293    have h3 : |periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)| ≤ M := by
294      refine h1.trans ?_
295      rw [hM]
296      have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hC1
297      linarith
298    have h4 : periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) ^ 2 ≤ M ^ 2 := by
299      rw [← sq_abs]; exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h3 2
300    nlinarith [sq_nonneg R]
301  · replace hcase := not_le.1 hcase
302    set x : Euc d := WithLp.toLp 2 y with hx
303    have hxn : ‖x‖ < δ := by
304      have := norm_toLp_le_card_mul hd0 y
305      have hdpos : (0:ℝ) < d := by positivity
306      calc ‖x‖ ≤ d * ‖y‖ := this
307        _ < d * θ := by gcongr
308        _ = δ := by rw [hθ]; field_simp
309    have hx0 : x ≠ 0 := by
310      intro h0
311      apply hy
312      ext i
313      have := congrArg (fun z : Euc d => z i) h0
314      simpa [hx] using this
315    have hxpos : 0 < ‖x‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hx0
316    have hg : |periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)| ≤ c * ‖x‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2)) + R := hloc x hx0 hxn
317    have h5 : ‖x‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2)) ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2)) :=
318      Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hypos (norm_le_norm_toLp y) (by
319        have : 0 ≤ (s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2 := by positivity
320        linarith)
321    have h6 : |periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)| ≤ c * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2)) + R :=
322      hg.trans (by have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h5 hc; linarith)
323    have h7 : periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) ^ 2 ≤ (c * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2)) + R) ^ 2 := by
324      rw [← sq_abs]; exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h6 2
325    have h8 : (‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2))) ^ 2 = ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + (d : ℝ) / 2)) := by
326      rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hypos.le]
327      congr 1; push_cast; ring
328    have h9 : 0 ≤ M ^ 2 := by positivity
329    calc _ ≤ _ := h7
330      _ ≤ 2 * (c * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2))) ^ 2 + 2 * R ^ 2 := by nlinarith [sq_nonneg (c * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + (d : ℝ) / 2) / 2)) - R)]
331      _ = 2 * c ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + (d : ℝ) / 2)) + 2 * R ^ 2 := by rw [mul_pow, h8]; ring
332      _ ≤ _ := by linarith
333
334lemma riesz_sq_integrable_volume {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hs : s < (d : ℝ) / 2) :
335    Integrable (fun x : Torus d => periodicRiesz d s x ^ 2) := by
336  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
337  obtain ⟨K1, K2, hK1, hK2, hbd⟩ := riesz_sq_box (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hs
338  refine ⟨((aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz hd hs0 (by linarith)).pow 2), ?_⟩
339  unfold HasFiniteIntegral
340  rw [lintegral_torus_box (fun x => ‖periodicRiesz d s x ^ 2‖ₑ)]
341  have hI : IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => K1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + (d : ℝ) / 2)) + K2) (Box d) :=
342    ((integrableOn_Box_rpow hd0 (α := s + (d : ℝ) / 2) (by linarith)).const_mul K1).add
343      (integrableOn_const (measure_Box_ne_top d))
344  have hne : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume.restrict (Box d)), y ≠ 0 := ae_restrict_of_ae (ae_ne_pi hd0)
345  calc ∫⁻ y in Box d, ‖periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) ^ 2‖ₑ
346      ≤ ∫⁻ y in Box d, ENNReal.ofReal (K1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + (d : ℝ) / 2)) + K2) := by
347        refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
348        filter_upwards [hne, ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)] with y hy hyB
349        rw [← ofReal_norm, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
350        exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hbd y hyB hy)
351    _ = ∫⁻ y in Box d, ‖K1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + (d : ℝ) / 2)) + K2‖ₑ := by
352        refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
353        rw [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
354    _ < ∞ := hI.2
355

357lemma qmp_sub (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [SFinite μ] (hμ : μ ≪ volume) :
358    Measure.QuasiMeasurePreserving (fun z : Torus d × Torus d => z.1 - z.2) (μ.prod μ) volume := by
359  have h2 : MeasurePreserving (fun z : Torus d × Torus d => z.1 - z.2)
360      ((volume : Measure (Torus d)).prod volume) volume :=
361    (measurePreserving_fst (μ := (volume : Measure (Torus d))) (ν := (volume : Measure (Torus d)))).comp
362      (measurePreserving_sub_prod (volume : Measure (Torus d)) (volume : Measure (Torus d)))
363  exact h2.quasiMeasurePreserving.mono_left (hμ.prod hμ)

365lemma lintegral_prod_conv_le {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
366    (F : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞) (hF : AEMeasurable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => F (p.1 - p.2))
367      ((withDensityReal ρ).prod (withDensityReal ρ))) :
368    ∫⁻ p : Torus d × Torus d, F (p.1 - p.2) ∂((withDensityReal ρ).prod (withDensityReal ρ)) ≤
369      ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, F y := by
370  have := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
371  rw [lintegral_prod _ hF]
372  show ∫⁻ x, ∫⁻ y, F (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ _
373  calc _ ≤ ∫⁻ _x, (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, F y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) :=
374        lintegral_mono fun x => lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ F x
375    _ = _ := by rw [lintegral_const, measure_univ, mul_one]

380/-- Lemma A.35 (i), the "automatic" squared-kernel condition (source lines 11507-11509): for
381`0 ≤ s < d/2` and a bounded probability density, `∬ g(x-y)² d(μ°)^{⊗2} < ∞`. -/
382theorem periodicRiesz_sq_integrable {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hs : s < (d : ℝ) / 2)
383    (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) :
384    Integrable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ^ 2)
385      ((withDensityReal ρ).prod (withDensityReal ρ)) := by
386  have := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
387  have hvol := riesz_sq_integrable_volume (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hs
388  have hm : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2))
389      ((withDensityReal ρ).prod (withDensityReal ρ)) :=
390    (KernelAux.aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz hd hs0 (by linarith)).comp_quasiMeasurePreserving
391      (qmp_sub _ (withDensityReal_ac ρ))
392  refine ⟨hm.pow 2, ?_⟩
393  unfold HasFiniteIntegral
394  have h1 := lintegral_prod_conv_le hρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ‖periodicRiesz d s z ^ 2‖ₑ)
395    (hm.aemeasurable.pow_const 2).enorm
396  refine lt_of_le_of_lt h1 (ENNReal.mul_lt_top ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top hvol.hasFiniteIntegral)

400lemma aesm_pair {g : Torus d → ℝ} (hgm : AEStronglyMeasurable g volume) {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} :
401    AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => g (p.1 - p.2))
402      ((withDensityReal ρ).prod (withDensityReal ρ)) :=
403  have := sigmaFinite_withDensityReal ρ
404  hgm.comp_quasiMeasurePreserving (qmp_sub _ (withDensityReal_ac ρ))

406lemma memLp_energy (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {N : ℕ}
407    (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hW : MemLp (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => g (p.1 - p.2)) 2 (μ.prod μ)) :
408    MemLp (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergyKernel g X μ) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
409  have hfun : (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergyKernel g X μ) = fun X =>
410      1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, SecondMomentAux.hoeffK g μ (X i) (X j) -
411        1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2 * ∑ i, (convMeasure g μ (X i) - SecondMomentAux.gMean g μ / 2) :=
412    funext fun X => SecondMomentAux.energy_decomp μ hN hW X
413  rw [hfun]
414  have hc : MemLp (convMeasure g μ) 2 μ := (SecondMomentAux.h_memLp_sq_le g μ hW).1
415  have hA : MemLp (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
416      SecondMomentAux.hoeffK g μ (X i) (X j)) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
417    refine memLp_finsetSum _ fun i _ => memLp_finsetSum _ fun j hj => ?_
418    exact SecondMomentAux.memLp_hoeffK_pair μ hW (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj).symm
419  have hB : MemLp (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, (convMeasure g μ (X i) -
420      SecondMomentAux.gMean g μ / 2)) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
421    refine memLp_finsetSum _ fun i _ => ?_
422    have h1 : MemLp (fun X : Config d N => convMeasure g μ (X i)) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
423      hc.comp_measurePreserving (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => μ) i)
424    exact h1.sub (memLp_const _)
425  exact (hA.const_mul _).sub (hB.const_mul _)

427lemma eLpNorm_eq_ofReal_sqrt {α : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] {ν : Measure α} [IsProbabilityMeasure ν]
428    {k : α → ℝ} (hk : MemLp k 2 ν) :
429    eLpNorm k 2 ν = ENNReal.ofReal (Real.sqrt (∫ x, k x ^ 2 ∂ν)) := by
430  have key : (eLpNorm k 2 ν).toReal = Real.sqrt (∫ x, k x ^ 2 ∂ν) := by
431    rw [hk.eLpNorm_eq_integral_rpow_norm (by norm_num) (by norm_num), ENNReal.toReal_ofReal
432      (by positivity)]
433    simp [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow]
434  rw [← key, ENNReal.ofReal_toReal hk.eLpNorm_ne_top]

437lemma conv_bound_unif {g : Torus d → ℝ} (hg : Integrable g) {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (Λ : ℝ)
438    (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (x : Torus d) :
439    |convMeasure g (withDensityReal ρ) x| ≤
440      (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ‖g y‖).toReal := by
441  have h1 := norm_integral_le_lintegral_norm (μ := withDensityReal ρ) (fun y => g (x - y))
442  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at h1
443  refine h1.trans (ENNReal.toReal_mono ?_ (KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ
444    (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal ‖g z‖) x))
445  refine ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top ?_
446  have := hg.hasFiniteIntegral
447  unfold HasFiniteIntegral at this
448  simp only [← ofReal_norm] at this
449  exact this.ne

454/-- Lemma A.35 (i), second display `B:eq:initial-energy-second-moment` (source lines
45511502-11506): if moreover `0 ≤ s < d/2` and `∬ g² d(μ°)^{⊗2} < ∞`, then
456`(E[(F_N^0 + o_N)²])^{1/2} ≤ C (N⁻¹ ∨ o'_N)`, uniformly over densities bounded by `Λ`. -/
457theorem initial_energy_iid_L2 {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hs : s < (d : ℝ) / 2)
458    (C₀ Λ : ℝ) :
459    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
460      Integrable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ^ 2)
461        ((withDensityReal ρ).prod (withDensityReal ρ)) →
462      ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
463        eLpNorm (fun X : Config d N => rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oN d s C₀ N) 2
464            (Measure.pi fun _ => withDensityReal ρ) ≤
465          ENNReal.ofReal (C * max ((N : ℝ)⁻¹) (oPrime d s Λ N)) := by
466  have hsd : s < d := by linarith
467  have hgi := KernelAux.integrable_periodicRiesz (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
468  have hgm := KernelAux.aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
469  have hsqv := riesz_sq_integrable_volume (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hs
470  set K : ℝ := (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (periodicRiesz d s y ^ 2)).toReal with hK
471  set M : ℝ := (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ‖periodicRiesz d s y‖).toReal with hM
472  set C' : ℝ := max (1 + K) M with hC'
473  have hK0 : 0 ≤ K := ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
474  have hM0 : 0 ≤ M := ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
475  set C₁ : ℝ := C' * C' + C' with hC₁
476  have hC'0 : 0 ≤ C' := (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ 1 + K).trans (le_max_left _ _)
477  refine ⟨C₁ * (|C₀| + 1) + 2 * (|C₀| + 1), fun ρ hρ hΛ hint2 N hN => ?_⟩
478  have hprob := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
479  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
480  have hΛ1 := one_le_of_probDensity hρ Λ hΛ
481  have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
482  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN1
483  have hmeas := aesm_pair (g := periodicRiesz d s) hgm (ρ := ρ)
484  have hW : MemLp (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2)) 2 (μ.prod μ) :=
485    (memLp_two_iff_integrable_sq hmeas).mpr hint2
486  set o : ℝ := |oN d s C₀ N| with ho
487  have ho0 : 0 ≤ o := abs_nonneg _
488  have hMb : ∀ x, |convMeasure (periodicRiesz d s) μ x| ≤ M :=
489    fun x => conv_bound_unif hgi Λ hΛ x
490  have hG : ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, (1 + periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ^ 2) ∂(μ.prod μ) ≤ C' := by
491    rw [integral_add (integrable_const _) hint2]
492    rw [integral_const]
493    simp only [measure_univ, Measure.real, ENNReal.toReal_one, smul_eq_mul, one_mul]
494    have hF : AEMeasurable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d =>
495        ENNReal.ofReal (periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ^ 2)) (μ.prod μ) :=
496      hint2.aestronglyMeasurable.aemeasurable.ennreal_ofReal
497    have h1 := lintegral_prod_conv_le hρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (periodicRiesz d s z ^ 2)) hF
498    have h2 : ∫ p : Torus d × Torus d, periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2) ^ 2 ∂(μ.prod μ) ≤ K := by
499      rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => sq_nonneg _)
500        hint2.aestronglyMeasurable]
501      refine ENNReal.toReal_mono ?_ h1
502      exact ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hsqv.lintegral_lt_top.ne
503    exact le_trans (by linarith) (le_max_left (1 + K) M)
504  obtain ⟨CN, hCN0, hCN, hbound⟩ := second_moment_modulatedEnergy_iid
505    (N := N) (periodicRiesz d s) μ (fun x => by rw [periodicRiesz_neg]) hmeas o ho0 hint2 M hMb hN1
506  have hCN' : CN ≤ C₁ := hCN C' hG (le_max_right _ _)
507  have hFm := memLp_energy (periodicRiesz d s) μ hN1 hW
508  have hFo : MemLp (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergyKernel (periodicRiesz d s) X μ + o) 2
509      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := hFm.add (memLp_const _)
510  have e1 : eLpNorm (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergyKernel (periodicRiesz d s) X μ + o) 2
511      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (CN * max ((N : ℝ)⁻¹) o) := by
512    rw [eLpNorm_eq_ofReal_sqrt hFo]
513    exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hbound
514  have e2 : (fun X : Config d N => rieszEnergy d s X μ + oN d s C₀ N) =
515      (fun X : Config d N => modulatedEnergyKernel (periodicRiesz d s) X μ + o) +
516        fun _ => oN d s C₀ N - o := by
517    funext X; simp only [Pi.add_apply, rieszEnergy]; ring
518  have e3 : eLpNorm (fun _ : Config d N => oN d s C₀ N - o) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) ≤
519      ENNReal.ofReal (2 * o) := by
520    rw [eLpNorm_const _ (by norm_num) (NeZero.ne _)]
521    simp only [measure_univ, ENNReal.toReal_ofNat, ENNReal.one_rpow, mul_one]
522    rw [← ofReal_norm]
523    refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
524    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_le]
525    have := abs_le.1 (le_refl o)
526    constructor <;> cases abs_cases (oN d s C₀ N) <;> linarith
527  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩ := oN_abs_le hd hs0 C₀ Λ hΛ1 N hN1
528  rw [e2]
529  refine (eLpNorm_add_le (by norm_num)).trans ?_
530  refine (add_le_add e1 e3).trans ?_
531  rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_add (mul_nonneg (by linarith) (le_max_of_le_left (by positivity))) (by positivity)]
532  refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
533  set oP := oPrime d s Λ N with hoP
534  set mx := max ((N : ℝ)⁻¹) oP with hmx
535  have hm1 : max ((N : ℝ)⁻¹) o ≤ (|C₀| + 1) * mx := by
536    refine max_le ?_ ?_
537    · calc (N : ℝ)⁻¹ ≤ oP := h1
538        _ ≤ (|C₀| + 1) * oP := by nlinarith [abs_nonneg C₀]
539        _ ≤ _ := by gcongr; exact le_max_right _ _
540    · calc o ≤ (|C₀| + 1) * oP := h2
541        _ ≤ _ := by gcongr; exact le_max_right _ _
542  have hmx0 : 0 ≤ mx := h3.trans (le_max_right _ _)
543  have ho1 : o ≤ (|C₀| + 1) * mx := (le_max_right _ _).trans hm1
544  calc CN * max ((N : ℝ)⁻¹) o + 2 * o ≤ C₁ * ((|C₀| + 1) * mx) + 2 * ((|C₀| + 1) * mx) := by
545        gcongr
546    _ = _ := by ring

552/-- Lemma A.35 (ii) (source lines 11510-11511), static content: for a deterministic
553collision-free configuration path `Y` (any probability space, all randomness absent), the
554energy moments are the computable numbers `sup_t (F_N(Y_t, μ^t) + o_N)` and the second-moment
555quantity `𝔈_{N,2}` equals `𝔈_N`, i.e. `B:ass:MF2` holds with `𝔈'_N = 𝔈_N`.  (The dynamical
556statement that `β_N = ∞` dynamics started from deterministic data stays deterministic belongs to
557the particle-dynamics package.) -/
558theorem energyMoment_deterministic {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω)
559    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (N : ℕ) (hN : 2 ≤ N) (Y : ℝ≥0 → Config d N)
560    (hY : ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) S.T, Y (Real.toNNReal t) ∉ collisionSet d N) :
561    S.energyMoment P N (fun t _ => Y t) =
562        ⨆ t : Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) S.T, S.energyCompletion N (Y (Real.toNNReal t)) t ∧
563      S.energyMoment2 P N (fun t _ => Y t) = S.energyMoment P N (fun t _ => Y t) ∧
564      (S.EnergyMomentFinite P N (fun t _ => Y t) ↔
565        S.EnergyMoment2Finite P N (fun t _ => Y t)) := by
566  have hc : ∀ t : Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) S.T, 0 ≤ S.energyCompletion N (Y (Real.toNNReal t)) t := fun t =>
567    S.energy_nonneg N hN t t.2 _ (hY t t.2)
568  have h1 : ∀ t : Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) S.T,
569      ∫ ω, S.energyCompletion N (Y (Real.toNNReal t)) t ∂P =
570        S.energyCompletion N (Y (Real.toNNReal t)) t := fun t => by simp
571  have h2 : ∀ t : Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) S.T,
572      Real.sqrt (∫ ω, (S.energyCompletion N (Y (Real.toNNReal t)) t) ^ 2 ∂P) =
573        S.energyCompletion N (Y (Real.toNNReal t)) t := fun t => by
574    simp [Real.sqrt_sq (hc t)]
575  have e1 : S.energyMoment P N (fun t _ => Y t) =
576      ⨆ t : Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) S.T, S.energyCompletion N (Y (Real.toNNReal t)) t := by
577    unfold GeneralSetup.energyMoment
578    exact iSup_congr h1
579  refine ⟨e1, ?_, ?_⟩
580  · rw [e1]
581    unfold GeneralSetup.energyMoment2
582    exact iSup_congr h2
583  · constructor
584    · rintro ⟨-, hb⟩
585      refine ⟨fun t ht => ?_, ?_⟩
586      · exact memLp_const _
587      · convert hb using 2
588        funext t
589        exact (h2 t).trans (h1 t).symm
590    · rintro ⟨-, hb⟩
591      refine ⟨fun t ht => ?_, ?_⟩
592      · exact integrable_const _
593      · convert hb using 2
594        funext t
595        exact (h1 t).trans (h2 t).symm
596
597end GeneralSetup

601/-- The bridge covariance `μ°(ψφ) - μ°(ψ) μ°(φ)` of Example A.17 (source lines 10471-10472). -/
602def iidCov (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (ψ φ : Torus d → ℝ) : ℝ :=
603  pair μ (fun x => ψ x * φ x) - pair μ ψ * pair μ φ
604

607lemma sqrt_mul_rpow (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) (a : ℝ) :
608    Real.sqrt N * (N : ℝ) ^ (a - 1) = (N : ℝ) ^ (a - 1 / 2) := by
609  have hN' : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
610  rw [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, ← Real.rpow_add hN']
611  congr 1; ring
612
613lemma log_term_tendsto :
614    Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => Real.sqrt N * (Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) / N)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
615  have h1 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 / 2 : ℝ))) atTop (𝓝 0) :=
616    (tendsto_rpow_neg_atTop (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1 / 2)).comp tendsto_natCast_atTop_atTop
617  have h2 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => Real.log N / (N : ℝ) ^ (1 / 2 : ℝ)) atTop (𝓝 0) :=
618    ((isLittleO_log_rpow_atTop (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1 / 2)).tendsto_div_nhds_zero).comp
619      tendsto_natCast_atTop_atTop
620  have h3 := h1.add h2
621  rw [add_zero] at h3
622  refine h3.congr' ?_
623  filter_upwards [eventually_gt_atTop 0] with N hN
624  have hN' : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
625  have hlog : Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) = 1 + Real.log N := by
626    rw [Real.log_mul (Real.exp_pos 1).ne' hN'.ne', Real.log_exp]
627  rw [hlog, Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, Real.rpow_neg hN'.le]
628  have : (N : ℝ) ^ (1 / 2 : ℝ) * (N : ℝ) ^ (1 / 2 : ℝ) = N := by
629    rw [← Real.rpow_add hN']; norm_num
630  have hp : 0 < (N : ℝ) ^ (1 / 2 : ℝ) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hN' _
631  field_simp
632  nlinarith [this]
633
634end InitialDataAux
635
636open InitialDataAux in
637/-- Example A.17 (source lines 10473-10475): with general energy bound of order
638`N^{s/d-1} + 1_{s=0} log(eN)/N` at normalisation `κ_N = √N`, the sufficient central-limit
639condition `√N (N^{s/d-1} + 1_{s=0} log(eN)/N) → 0` holds exactly when `s < d/2`. -/
640theorem iid_energy_scaling_iff {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
641    Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => Real.sqrt N * ((N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) +
642        (if s = 0 then Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) / N else 0))) atTop (𝓝 0) ↔
643      s < (d : ℝ) / 2 := by
644  have hdpos : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
645  have key : ∀ N : ℕ, 0 < N → Real.sqrt N * ((N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) +
646        (if s = 0 then Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) / N else 0)) =
647      (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1 / 2) + (if s = 0 then Real.sqrt N * (Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) / N) else 0) := by
648    intro N hN
649    rw [mul_add, sqrt_mul_rpow N hN]
650    split_ifs <;> simp
651  have hlt : s < (d : ℝ) / 2 ↔ s / d - 1 / 2 < 0 := by
652    rw [sub_neg, div_lt_iff₀ hdpos]
653    constructor <;> intro h <;> linarith
654  constructor
655  · intro h
656    by_contra hnot
657    have hs : s ≠ 0 := by
658      rintro rfl
659      exact hnot (by positivity)
660    have hge : 0 ≤ s / d - 1 / 2 := by
661      rw [hlt] at hnot; exact not_lt.1 hnot
662    have h' : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1 / 2)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
663      refine h.congr' ?_
664      filter_upwards [eventually_gt_atTop 0] with N hN
665      rw [key N hN]; simp [hs]
666    have h1 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1 / 2)) atTop (𝓝 1) ∨
667        Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1 / 2)) atTop atTop := by
668      rcases hge.eq_or_lt with h0 | h0
669      · left
670        rw [← h0]
671        simp only [Real.rpow_zero]
672        exact tendsto_const_nhds
673      · right
674        exact (tendsto_rpow_atTop h0).comp tendsto_natCast_atTop_atTop
675    rcases h1 with h1 | h1
676    · exact one_ne_zero (tendsto_nhds_unique h1 h')
677    · exact not_tendsto_nhds_of_tendsto_atTop h1 0 h'
678  · intro hs
679    have hneg := hlt.1 hs
680    have h1 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1 / 2)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
681      have := tendsto_rpow_neg_atTop (y := -(s / d - 1 / 2)) (by linarith)
682      simp only [neg_neg] at this
683      exact this.comp tendsto_natCast_atTop_atTop
684    have h2 : Tendsto (fun N : ℕ => (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1 / 2) +
685        (if s = 0 then Real.sqrt N * (Real.log (Real.exp 1 * N) / N) else 0)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
686      split_ifs
687      · simpa using h1.add log_term_tendsto
688      · simpa using h1
689    refine h2.congr' ?_
690    filter_upwards [eventually_gt_atTop 0] with N hN
691    exact (key N hN).symm
692
693/-! ### Lemma A.36: statistics-level initial assumptions for i.i.d. data -/
694
695/-- The statistic `⟨ψ, √N(μ_N - μ°)⟩ = √N (μ_N(ψ) - μ°(ψ))`. -/
696def iidStat (μ : Measure (Torus d)) {N : ℕ} (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N) : ℝ :=
697  Real.sqrt N * (pair (empirical X) ψ - pair μ ψ)
698

702theorem negSobolev_secondCountable (d : ℕ) (m : ℝ) : SecondCountableTopology (NegSobolev d m) := by
703  let b : HilbertBasis (NonzeroLattice d) ℝ (lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) :=
704    default
705  have hdense := b.dense_span
706  have hcount : (Set.range b).Countable := Set.countable_range _
707  have hsep := TopologicalSpace.IsSeparable.span (R := ℝ) hcount.isSeparable
708  have : TopologicalSpace.IsSeparable (Set.univ : Set (lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)) := by
709    have h2 := hsep.closure
710    rwa [← Submodule.topologicalClosure_coe, hdense, Submodule.top_coe] at h2
711  have : TopologicalSpace.SeparableSpace (lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) :=
712    TopologicalSpace.isSeparable_univ_iff.mp this
713  exact (inferInstance : SecondCountableTopology (lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2))

715lemma continuous_realMode (k : NonzeroLattice d) : Continuous (Torus.realMode k) := by
716  unfold Torus.realMode
717  split_ifs
718  · exact continuous_const.mul (Torus.continuous_cosMode _)
719  · exact continuous_const.mul (Torus.continuous_sinMode _)
720
721open Classical in
722lemma isSmooth_realMode (k : NonzeroLattice d) : Torus.IsSmooth (Torus.realMode k) := by
723  unfold Torus.IsSmooth Torus.IsCk
724  have hdot : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (fun y : Euc d => Lattice.dot k.1 y) := by
725    unfold Lattice.dot
726    refine ContDiff.sum fun i _ => contDiff_const.mul ?_
727    exact (EuclideanSpace.proj i : Euc d →L[ℝ] ℝ).contDiff
728  have hlift : Torus.lift (Torus.realMode k) = fun y : Euc d =>
729      if Lattice.IsPosHalf k.1 then Real.sqrt 2 * Real.cos (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.dot k.1 y)
730      else Real.sqrt 2 * Real.sin (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.dot k.1 y) := by
731    funext y
732    unfold Torus.lift Torus.realMode
733    simp only [Function.comp, Torus.cosMode_mk, Torus.sinMode_mk]
734  rw [hlift]
735  split_ifs
736  · exact contDiff_const.mul (Real.contDiff_cos.comp (contDiff_const.mul hdot))
737  · exact contDiff_const.mul (Real.contDiff_sin.comp (contDiff_const.mul hdot))
738
739/-- the trigonometric polynomial with prescribed real coefficients on a finite set -/
740def trigPoly (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ) : Torus d → ℝ :=
741  fun x => ∑ k ∈ T, c k * Torus.realMode k x
742
743lemma isSmooth_trigPoly (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ) :
744    Torus.IsSmooth (trigPoly T c) := by
745  unfold Torus.IsSmooth Torus.IsCk
746  have : Torus.lift (trigPoly T c) = fun y => ∑ k ∈ T, c k * Torus.lift (Torus.realMode k) y := by
747    funext y; simp [Torus.lift, trigPoly]
748  rw [this]
749  refine ContDiff.sum fun k _ => contDiff_const.mul ?_
750  exact isSmooth_realMode k
751
752lemma continuous_trigPoly (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ) :
753    Continuous (trigPoly T c) := by
754  unfold trigPoly
755  exact continuous_finsetSum _ fun k _ => continuous_const.mul (continuous_realMode k)
756
757lemma integrable_realMode_mul (k l : NonzeroLattice d) :
758    Integrable (fun x => Torus.realMode k x * Torus.realMode l x) :=
759  ((continuous_realMode k).mul (continuous_realMode l)).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
760    (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
761
762lemma funRealCoeff_trigPoly (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ)
763    (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
764    Torus.funRealCoeff (trigPoly T c) k = if k ∈ T then c k else 0 := by
765  unfold Torus.funRealCoeff trigPoly
766  have : ∀ x, Torus.realMode k x * ∑ l ∈ T, c l * Torus.realMode l x =
767      ∑ l ∈ T, c l * (Torus.realMode k x * Torus.realMode l x) := by
768    intro x; rw [Finset.mul_sum]; exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l _ => by ring
769  simp_rw [this]
770  rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun l _ => (integrable_realMode_mul k l).const_mul _]
771  simp_rw [integral_const_mul, Torus.realMode_orthonormal]
772  simp [Finset.sum_ite_eq]
773
774lemma integral_trigPoly_sq (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ) :
775    ∫ x, trigPoly T c x ^ 2 = ∑ k ∈ T, c k ^ 2 := by
776  have : ∀ x, trigPoly T c x ^ 2 = ∑ k ∈ T, ∑ l ∈ T,
777      c k * c l * (Torus.realMode k x * Torus.realMode l x) := by
778    intro x; unfold trigPoly; rw [sq, Finset.sum_mul_sum]
779    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l _ => by ring
780  simp_rw [this]
781  rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun k _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun l _ =>
782    (integrable_realMode_mul k l).const_mul _]
783  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k hk => ?_
784  rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun l _ => (integrable_realMode_mul k l).const_mul _]
785  simp_rw [integral_const_mul, Torus.realMode_orthonormal]
786  simp [Finset.sum_ite_eq, hk, sq]

788/-- the dual vector representing `F ↦ eval F ψ` -/
789def yvec (m : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ)
790    (h : Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2) :
791    NegSobolev d m :=
792  (⟨fun k => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k, by
793    show Memℓp _ 2
794    rw [Torus.memℓp_two_iff]
795    refine h.congr fun k => ?_
796    simp only [mul_pow, Torus.jp_rpow_sq]⟩ :
797    lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
798
799lemma eval_eq_inner (m : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ)
800    (h : Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2)
801    (F : NegSobolev d m) : NegSobolev.eval F ψ = inner ℝ (yvec m ψ h) F := by
802  have := lp.inner_eq_tsum (𝕜 := ℝ) (G := fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ)
803    (yvec m ψ h : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
804    (F : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
805  refine Eq.trans ?_ this.symm
806  unfold NegSobolev.eval NegSobolev.fourierCoeff NegSobolev.coord
807  refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
808  simp only [yvec, RCLike.inner_apply, conj_trivial]
809  ring

812lemma iidCov_le {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
813    (φ : Torus d → ℝ) (hφ : Continuous φ) :
814    iidCov (withDensityReal ρ) φ φ ≤ Λ * ∫ x, φ x ^ 2 := by
815  have hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ := (zero_le_one.trans (one_le_of_probDensity hρ Λ hΛ))
816  have hint : Integrable (fun x => φ x * φ x) (volume : Measure (Torus d)) :=
817    (hφ.mul hφ).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
818  have h1 : iidCov (withDensityReal ρ) φ φ ≤ pair (withDensityReal ρ) (fun x => φ x * φ x) := by
819    unfold iidCov
820    nlinarith [mul_self_nonneg (pair (withDensityReal ρ) φ)]
821  refine h1.trans ?_
822  unfold pair
823  calc ∫ x, φ x * φ x ∂(withDensityReal ρ)
824      ≤ ∫ x, φ x * φ x ∂(ENNReal.ofReal Λ • (volume : Measure (Torus d))) :=
825        integral_mono_measure (KernelAux2.withDensityReal_le ρ Λ hΛ)
826          (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => mul_self_nonneg _)
827          (hint.smul_measure ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
828    _ = Λ * ∫ x, φ x ^ 2 := by
829        rw [integral_smul_measure, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hΛ0, smul_eq_mul]
830        simp only [sq]
831
832lemma eval_sq_lintegral_le (m : ℝ) (hm0 : 0 ≤ m) {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ)
833    (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m)) [IsProbabilityMeasure ν]
834    (hν : IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m ν (iidCov (withDensityReal ρ)))
835    (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : MemSobolev m ψ) :
836    ∫⁻ F, ENNReal.ofReal (NegSobolev.eval F ψ ^ 2) ∂ν ≤
837      ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d,
838        Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2) := by
839  have := negSobolev_secondCountable d m
840  have hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ := (zero_le_one.trans (one_le_of_probDensity hρ Λ hΛ))
841  have hs := hψ.2
842  set c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ := Torus.funRealCoeff ψ with hc
843  set y : NegSobolev d m := yvec m ψ hs with hy
844  have hG := hν.1.1
845  have hmem : MemLp id 2 ν := IsGaussian.memLp_two_id
846  have hmean : ∫ z, id z ∂ν = 0 := gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hν.1
847  -- the covariance of a general vector
848  have hcov : ∀ x : NegSobolev d m, covarianceBilin ν x x = ∫ F, inner ℝ x F ^ 2 ∂ν := by
849    intro x
850    rw [covarianceBilin_apply hmem]
851    simp only [hmean, sub_zero, sq]
852  -- finite approximations
853  have hbound : ∀ T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d),
854      covarianceBilin ν (yvec m (trigPoly T c) (by
855        refine summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := T) fun k hk => ?_
856        rw [funRealCoeff_trigPoly]; simp [hk])) (yvec m (trigPoly T c) (by
857        refine summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := T) fun k hk => ?_
858        rw [funRealCoeff_trigPoly]; simp [hk])) ≤
859        Λ * ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * c k ^ 2 := by
860    intro T
861    set hT : Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d =>
862        Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff (trigPoly T c) k ^ 2 := by
863      refine summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := T) fun k hk => ?_
864      rw [funRealCoeff_trigPoly]; simp [hk]
865    rw [hcov]
866    have h1 : ∀ F : NegSobolev d m, inner ℝ (yvec m (trigPoly T c) hT) F ^ 2 =
867        NegSobolev.eval F (trigPoly T c) * NegSobolev.eval F (trigPoly T c) := by
868      intro F; rw [← eval_eq_inner, sq]
869    simp_rw [h1]
870    rw [hν.2 _ _ (isSmooth_trigPoly T c) (isSmooth_trigPoly T c)]
871    refine (iidCov_le hρ Λ hΛ _ (continuous_trigPoly T c)).trans ?_
872    rw [integral_trigPoly_sq]
873    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hΛ0
874    calc ∑ k ∈ T, c k ^ 2 ≤ ∑ k ∈ T, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * c k ^ 2 := by
875          refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun k _ => ?_
876          have : 1 ≤ Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) :=
877            Real.one_le_rpow (Torus.jp_ge_one _) (by linarith)
878          nlinarith [sq_nonneg (c k)]
879      _ ≤ _ := hs.sum_le_tsum T (fun k _ => mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _)
880            (sq_nonneg _))
881  -- convergence of the truncations
882  have hTs : ∀ T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d), Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d =>
883      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff (trigPoly T c) k ^ 2 := fun T => by
884    refine summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := T) fun k hk => ?_
885    rw [funRealCoeff_trigPoly]; simp [hk]
886  have hyT : ∀ T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d), yvec m (trigPoly T c) (hTs T) =
887      ∑ i ∈ T, (lp.single 2 i (NegSobolev.coord y i) :
888        lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) := by
889    intro T
890    refine Subtype.ext (funext fun k => ?_)
891    simp only [lp.coeFn_sum, Finset.sum_apply, lp.single_apply]
892    have e1 : NegSobolev.coord (yvec m (trigPoly T c) (hTs T)) k =
893        Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m * (if k ∈ T then c k else 0) := by
894      show Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m * Torus.funRealCoeff (trigPoly T c) k = _
895      rw [funRealCoeff_trigPoly]
896    change NegSobolev.coord (yvec m (trigPoly T c) (hTs T)) k = _
897    rw [e1]
898    by_cases hk : k ∈ T
899    · simp [hk, NegSobolev.coord, hy, yvec, hc]
900    · simp [hk]
901  have hsum := lp.hasSum_single (E := fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) (p := 2) ENNReal.ofNat_ne_top
902    (y : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
903  have htend : Tendsto (fun T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) => yvec m (trigPoly T c) (hTs T)) atTop
904      (𝓝 y) := by
905    simp_rw [hyT]
906    exact hsum
907  have hcont : Continuous (fun x : NegSobolev d m => covarianceBilin ν x x) :=
908    (covarianceBilin ν).continuous.clm_apply continuous_id
909  have hlim := (hcont.tendsto y).comp htend
910  have hle : covarianceBilin ν y y ≤ Λ * ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d,
911      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * c k ^ 2 := le_of_tendsto' hlim fun T => hbound T
912  -- conclusion
913  have hint : Integrable (fun F : NegSobolev d m => inner ℝ y F ^ 2) ν := by
914    have := IsGaussian.memLp_dual ν (innerSL ℝ y) 2 (by simp)
915    exact this.integrable_sq
916  have hfun : (fun F : NegSobolev d m => ENNReal.ofReal (NegSobolev.eval F ψ ^ 2)) =
917      fun F => ENNReal.ofReal (inner ℝ y F ^ 2) := by
918    funext F; rw [eval_eq_inner m ψ hs F]
919  rw [hfun, ← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => sq_nonneg _),
920    ← hcov y]
921  exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hle
922

925lemma integral_sum_sq_iid (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (N : ℕ)
926    {u : Torus d → ℝ} (hu : MemLp u 2 μ) (hu0 : ∫ x, u x ∂μ = 0) :
927    ∫ X : Config d N, (∑ i, u (X i)) ^ 2 ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) =
928      N * ∫ x, u x ^ 2 ∂μ := by
929  have hi : ∀ i : Fin N, MemLp (fun X : Config d N => u (X i)) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) :=
930    fun i => hu.comp_measurePreserving (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => μ) i)
931  have hexp : ∀ X : Config d N, (∑ i, u (X i)) ^ 2 = ∑ i, ∑ j, u (X i) * u (X j) := by
932    intro X; rw [sq, Finset.sum_mul_sum]
933  have hmul : ∀ i j : Fin N, Integrable (fun X : Config d N => u (X i) * u (X j))
934      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := fun i j => (hi i).integrable_mul (hi j)
935  simp_rw [hexp]
936  rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j _ => hmul i j]
937  have hdiag : ∀ i : Fin N, ∫ X : Config d N, u (X i) * u (X i) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) =
938      ∫ x, u x ^ 2 ∂μ := by
939    intro i
940    have := integral_comp_eval (μ := fun _ : Fin N => μ) (i := i)
941      (f := fun x => u x * u x) ((hu.aestronglyMeasurable.mul hu.aestronglyMeasurable))
942    simpa [sq] using this
943  have hrow : ∀ i : Fin N, ∫ X : Config d N, ∑ j, u (X i) * u (X j) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) =
944      ∫ x, u x ^ 2 ∂μ := by
945    intro i
946    rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun j _ => hmul i j]
947    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i]
948    · exact hdiag i
949    · intro j _ hji
950      have hH : Integrable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => u p.1 * u p.2) (μ.prod μ) :=
951        (hu.integrable one_le_two).mul_prod (hu.integrable one_le_two)
952      rw [integral_pair_comp μ (Ne.symm hji) (H := fun p => u p.1 * u p.2) hH, integral_prod_mul, hu0]
953      simp
954    · simp
955  simp_rw [hrow]
956  simp

959lemma sq_integral_le_sobolev (m : ℝ) (hm0 : 0 ≤ m) {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (hψ : MemSobolev m ψ) :
960    ∫ x, ψ x ^ 2 ≤ sobolevNorm m ψ ^ 2 := by
961  have hs := hψ.2
962  rw [Torus.parseval_realCoeff ψ hψ.1, sobolevNorm, Real.sq_sqrt (by
963    have : 0 ≤ ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2 :=
964      tsum_nonneg fun k => mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _) (sq_nonneg _)
965    positivity)]
966  have h2 : Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2 := by
967    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => sq_nonneg _) (fun k => ?_) hs
968    have : 1 ≤ Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) := Real.one_le_rpow (Torus.jp_ge_one _) (by linarith)
969    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k)]
970  have := h2.tsum_le_tsum (fun k => by
971    have : 1 ≤ Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) := Real.one_le_rpow (Torus.jp_ge_one _) (by linarith)
972    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k)]) hs
973  linarith
974
975lemma iidStat_lintegral_le (m : ℝ) (hm0 : 0 ≤ m) {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ)
976    (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (N : ℕ) (hN : 1 ≤ N) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : MemSobolev m ψ) :
977    ∫⁻ X, ENNReal.ofReal (iidStat (withDensityReal ρ) ψ X ^ 2)
978        ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => withDensityReal ρ) ≤
979      ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * sobolevNorm m ψ ^ 2) := by
980  have hprob := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
981  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
982  have hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ := (zero_le_one.trans (one_le_of_probDensity hρ Λ hΛ))
983  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
984  have hψμ : MemLp ψ 2 μ := by
985    have h1 : MemLp ψ 2 (ENNReal.ofReal Λ • (volume : Measure (Torus d))) :=
986      hψ.1.smul_measure ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
987    exact h1.mono_measure (KernelAux2.withDensityReal_le ρ Λ hΛ)
988  set p := pair μ ψ with hp
989  set u : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => ψ x - p with hu_def
990  have hu : MemLp u 2 μ := hψμ.sub (memLp_const p)
991  have hu0 : ∫ x, u x ∂μ = 0 := by
992    simp only [hu_def]
993    rw [integral_sub (hψμ.integrable one_le_two) (integrable_const _), integral_const]
994    simp [hp, pair]
995  have hstat : ∀ X : Config d N, iidStat μ ψ X = (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * ∑ i, u (X i) := by
996    intro X
997    unfold iidStat
998    rw [pair_empirical (by omega)]
999    simp only [hu_def, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin,
1000      nsmul_eq_mul]
1001    have hs : Real.sqrt N * Real.sqrt N = N := Real.mul_self_sqrt hNpos.le
1002    have hs0 : 0 < Real.sqrt N := Real.sqrt_pos.2 hNpos
1003    rw [← hp]
1004    field_simp
1005    rw [Real.sq_sqrt hNpos.le]
1006  have hU : MemLp (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, u (X i)) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
1007    refine memLp_finsetSum _ fun i _ => ?_
1008    exact hu.comp_measurePreserving (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => μ) i)
1009  have hsq : ∀ X : Config d N, iidStat μ ψ X ^ 2 = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * (∑ i, u (X i)) ^ 2 := by
1010    intro X
1011    rw [hstat, mul_pow, inv_pow, Real.sq_sqrt hNpos.le]
1012  have hint : Integrable (fun X : Config d N => iidStat μ ψ X ^ 2) (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
1013    simp_rw [hsq]
1014    exact hU.integrable_sq.const_mul _
1015  rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hint (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => sq_nonneg _)]
1016  refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
1017  simp_rw [hsq]
1018  rw [integral_const_mul, integral_sum_sq_iid μ N hu hu0, ← mul_assoc, inv_mul_cancel₀ hNpos.ne', one_mul]
1019  -- variance bound
1020  have hvar : ∫ x, u x ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, ψ x ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1021    have h1 := variance_eq_integral (μ := μ) hψμ.aemeasurable
1022    have h2 := variance_eq_sub hψμ
1023    have h3 : ∫ x, u x ^ 2 ∂μ = ∫ ω, (ψ ω - μ[ψ]) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
1024      simp only [hu_def, hp, pair]
1025    have h4 : μ[ψ ^ 2] = ∫ x, ψ x ^ 2 ∂μ := rfl
1026    rw [h3, ← h1, h2, h4]
1027    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (μ[ψ])]
1028  have hΛle : ∫ x, ψ x ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ Λ * ∫ x, ψ x ^ 2 := by
1029    calc ∫ x, ψ x ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, ψ x ^ 2 ∂(ENNReal.ofReal Λ • (volume : Measure (Torus d))) :=
1030          integral_mono_measure (KernelAux2.withDensityReal_le ρ Λ hΛ)
1031            (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => sq_nonneg _)
1032            (hψ.1.integrable_sq.smul_measure ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
1033      _ = _ := by rw [integral_smul_measure, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hΛ0, smul_eq_mul]
1034  have := sq_integral_le_sobolev m hm0 hψ
1035  nlinarith
1036
1037end InitialDataAux

1039open InitialDataAux in
1040/-- Lemma A.36 `M:B:lem:iid-init`, (I2) (source lines 11544-11545 with `B:item:I2`, line
104110211): for `m₀ > d/2`, `μ° = ρ dx` with `ρ ≤ Λ`, and the Gaussian field `η° ∼ ν` of
1042Example A.17 on `E_{m₀}`,
1043`E|⟨ψ, √N(μ_N^0 - μ°)⟩|² + E|⟨ψ, η°⟩|² ≤ C Λ ‖ψ‖²_{H^{m₀}}` for every `ψ ∈ H^{m₀}`, with
1044`C = C(d, m₀)`. -/
1045theorem iid_init_I2 (m₀ : ℝ) (hm₀ : (d : ℝ) / 2 < m₀) :
1046    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (Λ : ℝ), IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
1047      ∀ ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m₀), IsProbabilityMeasure ν →
1048        IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m₀ ν (iidCov (withDensityReal ρ)) →
1049      ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → ∀ ψ : Torus d → ℝ, MemSobolev m₀ ψ →
1050        ∫⁻ X, ENNReal.ofReal (iidStat (withDensityReal ρ) ψ X ^ 2)
1051            ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => withDensityReal ρ) +
1052          ∫⁻ F, ENNReal.ofReal (NegSobolev.eval F ψ ^ 2) ∂ν ≤
1053          ENNReal.ofReal (C * Λ * sobolevNorm m₀ ψ ^ 2) := by
1054  have hm0 : 0 ≤ m₀ := (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ (d : ℝ) / 2).trans hm₀.le
1055  refine ⟨2, fun ρ Λ hρ hΛ ν hν hνG N hN ψ hψ => ?_⟩
1056  have h1 := iidStat_lintegral_le m₀ hm0 hρ Λ hΛ N hN ψ hψ
1057  have h2 := eval_sq_lintegral_le m₀ hm0 hρ Λ hΛ ν hνG ψ hψ
1058  have hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ := (zero_le_one.trans (one_le_of_probDensity hρ Λ hΛ))
1059  have hs := hψ.2
1060  have hle : ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m₀) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2 ≤
1061      sobolevNorm m₀ ψ ^ 2 := by
1062    have hnn : 0 ≤ (Torus.mean ψ) ^ 2 +
1063        ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m₀) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2 := by
1064      have : 0 ≤ ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m₀) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2 :=
1065        tsum_nonneg fun k => mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _) (sq_nonneg _)
1066      positivity
1067    rw [sobolevNorm, Real.sq_sqrt hnn]
1068    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (Torus.mean ψ)]
1069  calc _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * sobolevNorm m₀ ψ ^ 2) + ENNReal.ofReal (Λ * sobolevNorm m₀ ψ ^ 2) :=
1070        add_le_add h1 (h2.trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hle hΛ0)))
1071    _ = _ := by
1072        rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_add (by positivity) (by positivity)]
1073        congr 1; ring

1077/-- Gluing: two random elements with prescribed statistics `T`, `T'` and a coupling `π` of the
1078statistics' laws. -/
1079theorem glue_two {A B : Type} [MeasurableSpace A] [StandardBorelSpace A] [Nonempty A]
1080    [MeasurableSpace B] [StandardBorelSpace B] [Nonempty B] {n : ℕ}
1081    (μ : Measure A) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (ν : Measure B) [IsProbabilityMeasure ν]
1082    {T : A → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)} {T' : B → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)}
1083    (hT : Measurable T) (hT' : Measurable T')
1084    (π : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n))) [IsProbabilityMeasure π]
1085    (h1 : π.map Prod.fst = μ.map T) (h2 : π.map Prod.snd = ν.map T') :
1086    ∃ P : Measure (A × B), IsProbabilityMeasure P ∧ P.map Prod.fst = μ ∧ P.map Prod.snd = ν ∧
1087      P.map (fun ω => (T ω.1, T' ω.2)) = π := by
1088  have hm1 : Measurable (fun a : A => (T a, a)) := hT.prodMk measurable_id
1089  have hm2 : Measurable (fun a : B => (T' a, a)) := hT'.prodMk measurable_id
1090  let ρ1 : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × A) := μ.map (fun a => (T a, a))
1091  let ρ2 : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × B) := ν.map (fun a => (T' a, a))
1092  have hρ1f : ρ1.fst = μ.map T := by
1093    rw [Measure.fst, Measure.map_map measurable_fst hm1]; rfl
1094  have hρ2f : ρ2.fst = ν.map T' := by
1095    rw [Measure.fst, Measure.map_map measurable_fst hm2]; rfl
1096  have hρ1s : ρ1.snd = μ := by
1097    rw [Measure.snd, Measure.map_map measurable_snd hm1]; exact Measure.map_id
1098  have hρ2s : ρ2.snd = ν := by
1099    rw [Measure.snd, Measure.map_map measurable_snd hm2]; exact Measure.map_id
1100  let κ1 : Kernel (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) A := ρ1.condKernel
1101  let κ2 : Kernel (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) B := ρ2.condKernel
1102  let K : Kernel (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) (A × B) :=
1103    (κ1.comap Prod.fst measurable_fst) ×ₖ (κ2.comap Prod.snd measurable_snd)
1104  have hdis1 : ρ1.fst ⊗ₘ κ1 = ρ1 := Measure.disintegrate ρ1 κ1
1105  have hdis2 : ρ2.fst ⊗ₘ κ2 = ρ2 := Measure.disintegrate ρ2 κ2
1106  have hset1 : MeasurableSet {p : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × A | T p.2 = p.1} :=
1107    measurableSet_eq_fun (hT.comp measurable_snd) measurable_fst
1108  have hset2 : MeasurableSet {p : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × B | T' p.2 = p.1} :=
1109    measurableSet_eq_fun (hT'.comp measurable_snd) measurable_fst
1110  have hae1 : ∀ᵐ e ∂(μ.map T), ∀ᵐ a ∂(κ1 e), T a = e := by
1111    have hρae : ∀ᵐ p ∂ρ1, T p.2 = p.1 := by
1112      show ∀ᵐ p ∂(μ.map (fun a => (T a, a))), T p.2 = p.1
1113      rw [ae_map_iff hm1.aemeasurable hset1]
1114      exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun a => rfl
1115    rw [← hdis1, Measure.ae_compProd_iff hset1] at hρae
1116    rwa [hρ1f] at hρae
1117  have hae2 : ∀ᵐ e ∂(ν.map T'), ∀ᵐ a ∂(κ2 e), T' a = e := by
1118    have hρae : ∀ᵐ p ∂ρ2, T' p.2 = p.1 := by
1119      show ∀ᵐ p ∂(ν.map (fun a => (T' a, a))), T' p.2 = p.1
1120      rw [ae_map_iff hm2.aemeasurable hset2]
1121      exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun a => rfl
1122    rw [← hdis2, Measure.ae_compProd_iff hset2] at hρae
1123    rwa [hρ2f] at hρae
1124  have hπ1 : ∀ᵐ e ∂π, ∀ᵐ a ∂(κ1 e.1), T a = e.1 := by
1125    rw [← h1] at hae1
1126    exact ae_of_ae_map measurable_fst.aemeasurable hae1
1127  have hπ2 : ∀ᵐ e ∂π, ∀ᵐ a ∂(κ2 e.2), T' a = e.2 := by
1128    rw [← h2] at hae2
1129    exact ae_of_ae_map measurable_snd.aemeasurable hae2
1130  have hKapp : ∀ e, K e = (κ1 e.1).prod (κ2 e.2) := fun e => by
1131    simp only [K]; rw [Kernel.prod_apply]; rfl
1132  have hKm : Measurable K := K.measurable
1133  refine ⟨K ∘ₘ π, inferInstance, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
1134  · ext s hs
1135    rw [Measure.map_apply measurable_fst hs]
1136    show Measure.bind π K _ = _
1137    rw [Measure.bind_apply (measurable_fst hs) hKm.aemeasurable]
1138    have e1 : ∀ e, K e (Prod.fst ⁻¹' s) = κ1 e.1 s := fun e => by
1139      rw [hKapp e, show (Prod.fst ⁻¹' s : Set (A × B)) = s ×ˢ Set.univ from by ext; simp,
1140        Measure.prod_prod]
1141      simp
1142    simp_rw [e1]
1143    rw [← lintegral_map (f := fun x => κ1 x s) (Kernel.measurable_coe κ1 hs) measurable_fst, h1,
1144      ← hρ1f]
1145    have hsn : ρ1.snd = κ1 ∘ₘ ρ1.fst := by
1146      conv_lhs => rw [← hdis1]
1147      exact Measure.snd_compProd _ _
1148    rw [← hρ1s, hsn]
1149    show _ = Measure.bind ρ1.fst κ1 s
1150    rw [Measure.bind_apply hs κ1.measurable.aemeasurable]
1151  · ext s hs
1152    rw [Measure.map_apply measurable_snd hs]
1153    show Measure.bind π K _ = _
1154    rw [Measure.bind_apply (measurable_snd hs) hKm.aemeasurable]
1155    have e1 : ∀ e, K e (Prod.snd ⁻¹' s) = κ2 e.2 s := fun e => by
1156      rw [hKapp e, show (Prod.snd ⁻¹' s : Set (A × B)) = Set.univ ×ˢ s from by ext; simp,
1157        Measure.prod_prod]
1158      simp
1159    simp_rw [e1]
1160    rw [← lintegral_map (f := fun x => κ2 x s) (Kernel.measurable_coe κ2 hs) measurable_snd, h2,
1161      ← hρ2f]
1162    have hsn : ρ2.snd = κ2 ∘ₘ ρ2.fst := by
1163      conv_lhs => rw [← hdis2]
1164      exact Measure.snd_compProd _ _
1165    rw [← hρ2s, hsn]
1166    show _ = Measure.bind ρ2.fst κ2 s
1167    rw [Measure.bind_apply hs κ2.measurable.aemeasurable]
1168  · ext W hW
1169    have hmm : Measurable (fun ω : A × B => (T ω.1, T' ω.2)) :=
1170      (hT.comp measurable_fst).prodMk (hT'.comp measurable_snd)
1171    rw [Measure.map_apply hmm hW]
1172    show Measure.bind π K _ = _
1173    rw [Measure.bind_apply (hmm hW) hKm.aemeasurable]
1174    have : ∀ᵐ e ∂π, K e ((fun ω : A × B => (T ω.1, T' ω.2)) ⁻¹' W) = W.indicator 1 e := by
1175      filter_upwards [hπ1, hπ2] with e he1 he2
1176      rw [hKapp e]
1177      have a1 : ∀ᵐ ab ∂((κ1 e.1).prod (κ2 e.2)), T ab.1 = e.1 :=
1178        Measure.quasiMeasurePreserving_fst.ae he1
1179      have a2 : ∀ᵐ ab ∂((κ1 e.1).prod (κ2 e.2)), T' ab.2 = e.2 :=
1180        Measure.quasiMeasurePreserving_snd.ae he2
1181      by_cases heW : e ∈ W
1182      · have hh : ∀ᵐ ab ∂((κ1 e.1).prod (κ2 e.2)),
1183            ab ∈ (fun ω : A × B => (T ω.1, T' ω.2)) ⁻¹' W := by
1184          filter_upwards [a1, a2] with ab h1 h2
1185          simp only [Set.mem_preimage, h1, h2]
1186          exact heW
1187        rw [Set.indicator_of_mem heW, Pi.one_apply]
1188        exact (prob_compl_eq_zero_iff (hmm hW)).1 (ae_iff.mp hh)
1189      · have hh : ∀ᵐ ab ∂((κ1 e.1).prod (κ2 e.2)),
1190            ab ∉ (fun ω : A × B => (T ω.1, T' ω.2)) ⁻¹' W := by
1191          filter_upwards [a1, a2] with ab h1 h2
1192          simp only [Set.mem_preimage, h1, h2]
1193          exact heW
1194        rw [Set.indicator_of_notMem heW]
1195        exact measure_eq_zero_iff_ae_notMem.2 hh
1196    rw [lintegral_congr_ae this, lintegral_indicator hW]
1197    simp
1198

1200/-- Per-test-function `W₁` estimate from a Lindeberg-type bound. -/
1201lemma w1_test_bound {n : ℕ} {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω)
1202    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (X Y : Ω → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) (hX : Measurable X)
1203    (hY : Measurable Y) (hX2 : MemLp X 2 P) (hY2 : MemLp Y 2 P) {Lm : ℝ}
1204    (hlind : ∀ {K : ℝ} {g : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → ℝ}, Yurinskii.TestFn K g →
1205      |∫ ω, g (X ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, g (Y ω) ∂P| ≤ K * Lm)
1206    {h : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → ℝ} (hh : LipschitzWith 1 h) {ε R : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε)
1207    (hR : 0 < R) :
1208    ∫ ω, h (X ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, h (Y ω) ∂P ≤
1209      (∫ ω, ‖X ω‖ ^ 2 ∂P + ∫ ω, ‖Y ω‖ ^ 2 ∂P) / (4 * R) + 2 * ε * Real.sqrt n +
1210        2 / ε ^ 2 * Lm := by
1211  set u : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → ℝ := fun x => h x - h 0
1212  have hu : LipschitzWith 1 u := LipschitzWith.of_dist_le_mul fun x y => by
1213    simpa [u, Real.dist_eq] using hh.dist_le_mul x y
1214  set f : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → ℝ := fun x => max (-R) (min R (u x))
1215  have hfL : LipschitzWith 1 f := (hu.const_min R).const_max (-R)
1216  have hfc : Continuous f := hfL.continuous
1217  have hfR : ∀ y, |f y| ≤ R := fun y => by
1218    rw [abs_le]; simp only [f]; constructor
1219    · exact le_max_left _ _
1220    · exact max_le (by linarith) (min_le_left _ _)
1221  have huf : ∀ x, |u x - f x| ≤ ‖x‖ ^ 2 / (4 * R) := fun x => by
1222    refine clamp_err hR ?_
1223    have := hu.dist_le_mul x 0
1224    simpa [u, Real.dist_eq] using this
1225  set g := gsmooth ε f
1226  have hgc : ContDiff ℝ 2 g := contDiff_gsmooth hε hfc hfR
1227  have hfg : ∀ x, |f x - g x| ≤ ε * Real.sqrt n := fun x => by
1228    rw [abs_sub_comm]; exact gsmooth_sub_le hε hfc hfR hfL x
1229  have hTF : Yurinskii.TestFn (1 * (2 / ε ^ 2)) g := by
1230    refine ⟨hgc, by positivity, ⟨R, abs_gsmooth_le hε hfc hfR⟩,
1231      ⟨1, norm_fderiv_gsmooth_le hε hfc hfR hfL⟩,
1232      ⟨Real.sqrt n / ε, norm_fderiv_fderiv_gsmooth_le hε hfc hfR hfL⟩, ?_⟩
1233    intro x y v
1234    exact hess_gsmooth_lip hε hfc hfR (c := 1) zero_le_one
1235      (fun x y => by simpa [Real.dist_eq, dist_eq_norm] using hfL.dist_le_mul x y) x y v
1236  have hXi : Integrable (fun ω => ‖X ω‖) P := (hX2.integrable one_le_two).norm
1237  have hYi : Integrable (fun ω => ‖Y ω‖) P := (hY2.integrable one_le_two).norm
1238  have hX2i : Integrable (fun ω => ‖X ω‖ ^ 2) P := hX2.integrable_norm_pow two_ne_zero
1239  have hY2i : Integrable (fun ω => ‖Y ω‖ ^ 2) P := hY2.integrable_norm_pow two_ne_zero
1240  have hhX := integrable_lip_comp P hh hX hXi
1241  have hhY := integrable_lip_comp P hh hY hYi
1242  have hgb : ∀ z, |g z| ≤ R := abs_gsmooth_le hε hfc hfR
1243  have hgX : Integrable (fun ω => g (X ω)) P :=
1244    integrable_comp_of_continuous P hgc.continuous hgb hX.aemeasurable
1245  have hgY : Integrable (fun ω => g (Y ω)) P :=
1246    integrable_comp_of_continuous P hgc.continuous hgb hY.aemeasurable
1247  have hpt : ∀ ω, h (X ω) - h (Y ω) ≤
1248      (‖X ω‖ ^ 2 + ‖Y ω‖ ^ 2) / (4 * R) + 2 * ε * Real.sqrt n + (g (X ω) - g (Y ω)) := by
1249    intro ω
1250    have a1 := abs_le.1 (huf (X ω))
1251    have a2 := abs_le.1 (huf (Y ω))
1252    have a3 := abs_le.1 (hfg (X ω))
1253    have a4 := abs_le.1 (hfg (Y ω))
1254    have e : h (X ω) - h (Y ω) = u (X ω) - u (Y ω) := by simp [u]
1255    rw [e, add_div]
1256    linarith [a1.2, a2.1, a3.2, a4.1]
1257  have hint : ∫ ω, h (X ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, h (Y ω) ∂P ≤
1258      (∫ ω, ‖X ω‖ ^ 2 ∂P + ∫ ω, ‖Y ω‖ ^ 2 ∂P) / (4 * R) + 2 * ε * Real.sqrt n +
1259        (∫ ω, g (X ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, g (Y ω) ∂P) := by
1260    have hc : ∫ _ω, (2 * ε * Real.sqrt n) ∂P = 2 * ε * Real.sqrt n := by simp
1261    rw [← integral_sub hhX hhY, ← integral_sub hgX hgY, ← integral_add hX2i hY2i,
1262      ← integral_div, ← hc,
1263      ← integral_add (f := fun ω => (‖X ω‖ ^ 2 + ‖Y ω‖ ^ 2) / (4 * R))
1264        (g := fun _ => 2 * ε * Real.sqrt n) ((hX2i.add hY2i).div_const _) (integrable_const _),
1265      ← integral_add (f := fun ω => (‖X ω‖ ^ 2 + ‖Y ω‖ ^ 2) / (4 * R) + 2 * ε * Real.sqrt n)
1266        (g := fun ω => g (X ω) - g (Y ω))
1267        (((hX2i.add hY2i).div_const _).add (integrable_const _)) (hgX.sub hgY)]
1268    exact integral_mono (hhX.sub hhY)
1269      ((((hX2i.add hY2i).div_const _).add (integrable_const _)).add (hgX.sub hgY)) hpt
1270  have hl := (le_abs_self _).trans (hlind hTF)
1271  have : 1 * (2 / ε ^ 2) * Lm = 2 / ε ^ 2 * Lm := by ring
1272  linarith
1273

1275open Yurinskii in
1276/-- `W₁` distance between a sum of i.i.d. centered vectors and its Gaussian analogue. -/
1277theorem w1_iid_gauss {n N : ℕ} (ρ : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n))) [IsProbabilityMeasure ρ]
1278    (h0 : ∀ c, ∫ x, x c ∂ρ = 0) (h3 : Integrable (fun x => ‖x‖ ^ 3) ρ) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1279    wassersteinE 1 ((Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ).map (fun p => ∑ i, p i))
1280      (multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) (∑ _i : Fin N, momentMatrix ρ)) ≤
1281    ENNReal.ofReal (2 * ε * Real.sqrt n +
1282      2 / ε ^ 2 * ((2 + c4 / 2) * (N * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ))) := by
1283  set G := multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) (∑ _i : Fin N, momentMatrix ρ)
1284  have h2ρ : Integrable (fun x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) => ‖x‖ ^ 2) ρ :=
1285    (integrable_norm_of_pow3 (P := ρ) (Y := id) measurable_id h3).2
1286  have hQ : (momentMatrix ρ).PosSemidef := momentMatrix_posSemidef _ h2ρ
1287  have hQs : (∑ _i : Fin N, momentMatrix ρ).PosSemidef :=
1288    Matrix.posSemidef_sum _ fun _ _ => hQ
1289  have : IsProbabilityMeasure G := inferInstance
1290  let P : Measure ((Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) × EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) :=
1291    (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ).prod G
1292  let X : (Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) × EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) :=
1293    fun ω => ∑ i, ω.1 i
1294  let Y : (Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) × EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) :=
1295    fun ω => ω.2
1296  have hXm : Measurable X :=
1297    Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => (measurable_pi_apply i).comp measurable_fst
1298  have hYm : Measurable Y := measurable_snd
1299  have hmemρ : MemLp (id : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → _) 2 ρ :=
1300    (memLp_two_iff_integrable_sq_norm aestronglyMeasurable_id).2 h2ρ
1301  have hmemG : MemLp (id : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → _) 2 G := IsGaussian.memLp_two_id
1302  have hmp1 : ∀ i : Fin N, MeasurePreserving (fun ω : (Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) ×
1303      EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) => ω.1 i) P ρ := fun i =>
1304    (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => ρ) i).comp
1305      (measurePreserving_fst (μ := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ) (ν := G))
1306  have hX2 : MemLp X 2 P := by
1307    have : X = ∑ i : Fin N, (fun ω : (Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) ×
1308        EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) => ω.1 i) := by
1309      funext ω; simp [X]
1310    rw [this]
1311    exact memLp_finsetSum' _ fun i _ => hmemρ.comp_measurePreserving (hmp1 i)
1312  have hY2 : MemLp Y 2 P :=
1313    hmemG.comp_measurePreserving (measurePreserving_snd (μ := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ) (ν := G))
1314  have hlawY : P.map Y = G := by
1315    have := (measurePreserving_snd (μ := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ) (ν := G)).map_eq
1316    exact this
1317  have hlawX : P.map X = (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ).map (fun p => ∑ i, p i) := by
1318    have hsm : Measurable (fun p : Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) => ∑ i, p i) :=
1319      Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => measurable_pi_apply i
1320    have e : X = (fun p : Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) => ∑ i, p i) ∘ Prod.fst := rfl
1321    rw [e, ← Measure.map_map hsm measurable_fst]
1322    congr 1
1323    exact (measurePreserving_fst (μ := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ) (ν := G)).map_eq
1324  have hlind : ∀ {K : ℝ} {g : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) → ℝ}, TestFn K g →
1325      |∫ ω, g (X ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, g (Y ω) ∂P| ≤ K * ((2 + c4 / 2) * (N * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ)) := by
1326    intro K g hg
1327    have e1 : ∫ ω, g (X ω) ∂P = ∫ x, g x ∂((Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ).map
1328        (fun p => ∑ i, p i)) := by
1329      rw [← hlawX, integral_map hXm.aemeasurable hg.measurable.aestronglyMeasurable]
1330    have e2 : ∫ ω, g (Y ω) ∂P = ∫ x, g x ∂G := by
1331      rw [← hlawY, integral_map hYm.aemeasurable hg.measurable.aestronglyMeasurable]
1332    rw [e1, e2]
1333    have := lindeberg_gauss hg (fun _ : Fin N => ρ) (fun i c => h0 c) (fun i => h3)
1334    refine this.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1335    simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
1336  have hdual := wassersteinE_one_le_of_dual P X Y hXm hYm hX2 hY2
1337    (2 * ε * Real.sqrt n + 2 / ε ^ 2 * ((2 + c4 / 2) * (N * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ))) (by
1338      intro h hh
1339      refine le_of_forall_pos_le_add fun η hη => ?_
1340      set m := ∫ ω, ‖X ω‖ ^ 2 ∂P + ∫ ω, ‖Y ω‖ ^ 2 ∂P
1341      have hm0 : 0 ≤ m := add_nonneg (integral_nonneg fun _ => by positivity)
1342        (integral_nonneg fun _ => by positivity)
1343      have hR : 0 < m / (4 * η) + 1 := by positivity
1344      have := w1_test_bound P X Y hXm hYm hX2 hY2 hlind hh hε hR
1345      have hmR : m / (4 * (m / (4 * η) + 1)) ≤ η := by
1346        rw [div_le_iff₀ (by positivity)]
1347        have : η * (4 * (m / (4 * η) + 1)) = m + 4 * η := by field_simp
1348        rw [this]; linarith
1349      linarith)
1350  have e1 : law P X = (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ).map (fun p => ∑ i, p i) := hlawX
1351  have e2 : law P Y = G := hlawY
1352  rw [e1, e2] at hdual
1353  exact hdual
1354

1356/-- A coupling with cost close to the `W₁` distance. -/
1357lemma exists_coupling_of_w1 {n : ℕ} (P Q : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)))
1358    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] [IsProbabilityMeasure Q] {t η : ℝ} (ht : 0 ≤ t) (hη : 0 < η)
1359    (h : wassersteinE 1 P Q ≤ ENNReal.ofReal t) :
1360    ∃ π : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)),
1361      IsProbabilityMeasure π ∧ π.map Prod.fst = P ∧ π.map Prod.snd = Q ∧
1362      ∫⁻ z, ‖z.1 - z.2‖ₑ ∂π ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (t + η) := by
1363  have hlt : wassersteinE 1 P Q < ENNReal.ofReal (t + η) := by
1364    refine h.trans_lt ?_
1365    exact (ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff (by linarith)).2 (by linarith)
1366  unfold wassersteinE at hlt
1367  obtain ⟨π, hπ⟩ := iInf_lt_iff.1 hlt
1368  have hcpl : IsCoupling π.1 P Q := π.2
1369  refine ⟨π.1, hcpl.isProbabilityMeasure, hcpl.1, hcpl.2, ?_⟩
1370  have : ∫⁻ z, ‖z.1 - z.2‖ₑ ∂π.1 = (∫⁻ z, ENNReal.ofReal (‖z.1 - z.2‖ ^ (1 : ℝ)) ∂π.1) ^ (1 / (1 : ℝ)) := by
1371    simp [ofReal_norm]
1372  rw [this]
1373  exact hπ.le

1376open Yurinskii in
1377/-- Abstract coupling: sum of i.i.d. centered vectors versus Gaussian, glued back to the
1378underlying spaces. -/
1379theorem coupling_core {A B : Type} [MeasurableSpace A] [StandardBorelSpace A] [Nonempty A]
1380    [MeasurableSpace B] [StandardBorelSpace B] [Nonempty B] {n N : ℕ}
1381    (μA : Measure A) [IsProbabilityMeasure μA] (νB : Measure B) [IsProbabilityMeasure νB]
1382    {T : A → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)} {T' : B → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)}
1383    (hT : Measurable T) (hT' : Measurable T')
1384    (ρ0 : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n))) [IsProbabilityMeasure ρ0]
1385    (h0 : ∀ c, ∫ x, x c ∂ρ0 = 0) (h3 : Integrable (fun x => ‖x‖ ^ 3) ρ0)
1386    (hlawT : μA.map T = (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ0).map (fun p => ∑ i, p i))
1387    (hlawT' : νB.map T' = multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n))
1388      (∑ _i : Fin N, momentMatrix ρ0)) {ε η : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (hη : 0 < η) :
1389    ∃ P : Measure (A × B), IsProbabilityMeasure P ∧ P.map Prod.fst = μA ∧ P.map Prod.snd = νB ∧
1390      ∫⁻ ω, ‖T ω.1 - T' ω.2‖ₑ ∂P ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * ε * Real.sqrt n +
1391        2 / ε ^ 2 * ((2 + c4 / 2) * (N * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0)) + η) := by
1392  have hw := w1_iid_gauss (N := N) ρ0 h0 h3 hε
1393  have hI0 : 0 ≤ ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0 := integral_nonneg fun x => by positivity
1394  have ht : 0 ≤ 2 * ε * Real.sqrt n + 2 / ε ^ 2 * ((2 + c4 / 2) * (N * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0)) := by
1395    have := c4_nonneg
1396    positivity
1397  obtain ⟨π, hπ, hπ1, hπ2, hcost⟩ := exists_coupling_of_w1 _ _ ht hη hw
1398  obtain ⟨P, hP, hP1, hP2, hP3⟩ := glue_two μA νB hT hT' π
1399    (by rw [hπ1, hlawT]) (by rw [hπ2, hlawT'])
1400  refine ⟨P, hP, hP1, hP2, ?_⟩
1401  have hmm : Measurable (fun ω : A × B => (T ω.1, T' ω.2)) :=
1402    (hT.comp measurable_fst).prodMk (hT'.comp measurable_snd)
1403  have hf : Measurable (fun z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) × EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n) => ‖z.1 - z.2‖ₑ) :=
1404    (measurable_fst.sub measurable_snd).enorm
1405  have : ∫⁻ ω, ‖T ω.1 - T' ω.2‖ₑ ∂P = ∫⁻ z, ‖z.1 - z.2‖ₑ ∂π := by
1406    rw [← hP3, lintegral_map hf hmm]
1407  rw [this]
1408  exact hcost
