1411/-- The Sobolev embedding constant. -/
1412def sobCs (d : ℕ) (m : ℝ) : ℝ :=
1413  Real.sqrt 2 * Real.sqrt (∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m)))
1414
1415/-- Fourier-series representative of an `H^m` function (`m > d/2`): `ψ = mean ψ + f` a.e. with
1416`f` continuous, the uniform limit of the trigonometric partial sums, and `‖f‖_∞ ≤ C ‖ψ‖_{H^m}`. -/
1417lemma sobolev_rep (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) / 2 < m) {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (hψ : MemSobolev m ψ) :
1418    ∃ f : Torus d → ℝ, Continuous f ∧ (∀ᵐ x, ψ x = Torus.mean ψ + f x) ∧
1419      (∀ x, |f x| ≤ sobCs d m * sobolevNorm m ψ) ∧
1420      TendstoUniformly (fun T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) => trigPoly T (Torus.funRealCoeff ψ)) f
1421        atTop := by
1422  have hm' : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m := by linarith
1423  have hS2 := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) m hm'
1424  set c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ := Torus.funRealCoeff ψ with hc
1425  have hs := hψ.2
1426  set W : ℝ := ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * c k ^ 2 with hW
1427  have hW0 : 0 ≤ W := tsum_nonneg fun k => mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _) (sq_nonneg _)
1428  -- summability of the coefficients
1429  have hcs := Torus.cs_tsum (fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m))
1430    (fun k => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m * |c k|) (fun k => Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _)
1431    (fun k => mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _) (abs_nonneg _))
1432    (by
1433      refine hS2.congr fun k => ?_
1434      rw [Torus.jp_rpow_sq]; ring_nf)
1435    (by
1436      refine hs.congr fun k => ?_
1437      rw [mul_pow, Torus.jp_rpow_sq, sq_abs])
1438  have hcab : ∀ k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m * |c k|) = |c k| := by
1439    intro k
1440    rw [← mul_assoc, ← Real.rpow_add (Torus.jp_pos _)]; simp
1441  have hsumabs : Summable fun k => |c k| := by
1442    simpa [hcab] using hcs.1
1443  have hsumbound : ∑' k, |c k| ≤ Real.sqrt (∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m))) *
1444      Real.sqrt W := by
1445    have := hcs.2
1446    simp only [hcab] at this
1447    refine this.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1448    congr 2
1449    · refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
1450      rw [Torus.jp_rpow_sq]; ring_nf
1451    · refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
1452      rw [mul_pow, Torus.jp_rpow_sq, sq_abs]
1453  -- the function
1454  set u : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ := fun k => Real.sqrt 2 * |c k| with hu
1455  have hu_s : Summable u := hsumabs.mul_left _
1456  have hterm : ∀ (k : NonzeroLattice d) (x : Torus d), ‖c k * Torus.realMode k x‖ ≤ u k := by
1457    intro k x
1458    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, mul_comm]
1459    exact mul_le_mul (Torus.abs_realMode_le k x) le_rfl (abs_nonneg _) (Real.sqrt_nonneg _) |>.trans_eq (by simp [hu])
1460  set f : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => ∑' k, c k * Torus.realMode k x with hf
1461  have hfc : Continuous f := continuous_tsum (fun k => continuous_const.mul (continuous_realMode k)) hu_s hterm
1462  have hunif : TendstoUniformly (fun T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) => trigPoly T c) f atTop := by
1463    have := tendstoUniformlyOn_tsum (f := fun (k : NonzeroLattice d) (x : Torus d) => c k * Torus.realMode k x)
1464      hu_s (s := Set.univ) (fun n x _ => hterm n x)
1465    rw [tendstoUniformlyOn_univ] at this
1466    exact this
1467  have hfbound : ∀ x, |f x| ≤ sobCs d m * sobolevNorm m ψ := by
1468    intro x
1469    have h1 : |f x| ≤ ∑' k, u k := by
1470      have := norm_tsum_le_tsum_norm ((hu_s.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => norm_nonneg _) (fun k => hterm k x)))
1471      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
1472      exact this.trans (Summable.tsum_le_tsum (fun k => hterm k x)
1473        (hu_s.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => norm_nonneg _) (fun k => hterm k x)) hu_s)
1474    have h2 : ∑' k, u k = Real.sqrt 2 * ∑' k, |c k| := tsum_mul_left
1475    have h3 : Real.sqrt W ≤ sobolevNorm m ψ := by
1476      unfold sobolevNorm
1477      exact Real.sqrt_le_sqrt (by nlinarith [sq_nonneg (Torus.mean ψ)])
1478    calc |f x| ≤ ∑' k, u k := h1
1479      _ = Real.sqrt 2 * ∑' k, |c k| := h2
1480      _ ≤ Real.sqrt 2 * (Real.sqrt (∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m))) * Real.sqrt W) :=
1481          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsumbound (Real.sqrt_nonneg _)
1482      _ ≤ Real.sqrt 2 * (Real.sqrt (∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m))) * sobolevNorm m ψ) := by
1483          gcongr
1484      _ = _ := by unfold sobCs; ring
1485  refine ⟨f, hfc, ?_, hfbound, hunif⟩
1486  -- weighted limits of the partial sums
1487  have hlim : ∀ w : Torus d → ℝ, Continuous w → ∀ B : ℝ, (∀ x, |w x| ≤ B) →
1488      Tendsto (fun T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) => ∫ x, w x * trigPoly T c x) atTop
1489        (𝓝 (∫ x, w x * f x)) := by
1490    intro w hw B hB
1491    refine tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence
1492      (bound := fun _ => B * (Real.sqrt 2 * ∑' k, |c k|)) ?_ ?_ (integrable_const _) ?_
1493    · exact Eventually.of_forall fun T =>
1494        (hw.mul (continuous_trigPoly T c)).aestronglyMeasurable
1495    · refine Eventually.of_forall fun T => Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_
1496      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul]
1497      have h1 : |trigPoly T c x| ≤ Real.sqrt 2 * ∑' k, |c k| := by
1498        unfold trigPoly
1499        calc |∑ k ∈ T, c k * Torus.realMode k x| ≤ ∑ k ∈ T, |c k * Torus.realMode k x| :=
1500              Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _
1501          _ ≤ ∑ k ∈ T, u k := Finset.sum_le_sum fun k _ => by
1502              simpa [Real.norm_eq_abs] using hterm k x
1503          _ ≤ ∑' k, u k := hu_s.sum_le_tsum T (fun k _ => by simp [hu])
1504          _ = _ := tsum_mul_left
1505      have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := (abs_nonneg _).trans (hB x)
1506      exact mul_le_mul (hB x) h1 (abs_nonneg _) hB0
1507    · refine Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_
1508      exact (hunif.tendsto_at x).const_mul (w x)
1509  have hcoef : ∀ k : NonzeroLattice d, Torus.funRealCoeff f k = c k := by
1510    intro k
1511    have h := hlim (Torus.realMode k) (continuous_realMode k) (Real.sqrt 2)
1512      (fun x => Torus.abs_realMode_le k x)
1513    have h2 : ∀ᶠ T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) in atTop,
1514        ∫ x, Torus.realMode k x * trigPoly T c x = c k := by
1515      filter_upwards [eventually_ge_atTop {k}] with T hT
1516      have := funRealCoeff_trigPoly T c k
1517      unfold Torus.funRealCoeff at this
1518      rw [this]; simp [hT (Finset.mem_singleton_self k)]
1519    have h3 := h.congr' h2
1520    exact tendsto_nhds_unique h3 tendsto_const_nhds
1521  have hmean0 : ∫ x, f x = 0 := by
1522    have h := hlim (fun _ => 1) continuous_const 1 (fun x => by simp)
1523    have h2 : ∀ᶠ T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) in atTop,
1524        ∫ x, (1 : ℝ) * trigPoly T c x = 0 := by
1525      refine Eventually.of_forall fun T => ?_
1526      simp only [one_mul]
1527      unfold trigPoly
1528      rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun k _ => ((continuous_realMode k).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
1529        (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)).const_mul _]
1530      simp [integral_const_mul, Torus.integral_realMode]
1531    have h3 := h.congr' h2
1532    have := tendsto_nhds_unique h3 tendsto_const_nhds
1533    simpa using this
1534  -- the difference has vanishing Fourier data
1535  have hψL2 : MemLp ψ 2 volume := hψ.1
1536  have hfL2 : MemLp f 2 volume := by
1537    refine MemLp.of_bound hfc.aestronglyMeasurable (sobCs d m * sobolevNorm m ψ)
1538      (Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
1539    simpa using hfbound x
1540  set g : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => ψ x - Torus.mean ψ - f x with hg
1541  have hgL2 : MemLp g 2 volume := (hψL2.sub (memLp_const _)).sub hfL2
1542  have hint_ek : ∀ (h : Torus d → ℝ), MemLp h 2 volume → ∀ k : NonzeroLattice d,
1543      Integrable (fun x => Torus.realMode k x * h x) := by
1544    intro h hh k
1545    exact (hh.integrable one_le_two).bdd_mul (c := Real.sqrt 2)
1546      (continuous_realMode k).aestronglyMeasurable
1547      (Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simpa using Torus.abs_realMode_le k x)
1548  have hgc : ∀ k, Torus.funRealCoeff g k = 0 := by
1549    intro k
1550    have e : ∀ x, Torus.realMode k x * g x =
1551        Torus.realMode k x * ψ x - Torus.mean ψ * Torus.realMode k x -
1552          Torus.realMode k x * f x := fun x => by simp only [hg]; ring
1553    unfold Torus.funRealCoeff
1554    simp_rw [e]
1555    have i1 := hint_ek ψ hψL2 k
1556    have i2 : Integrable (fun x => Torus.mean ψ * Torus.realMode k x) :=
1557      ((continuous_realMode k).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
1558        (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)).const_mul _
1559    have i3 := hint_ek f hfL2 k
1560    have i12 : Integrable (fun x => Torus.realMode k x * ψ x - Torus.mean ψ * Torus.realMode k x) :=
1561      i1.sub i2
1562    rw [integral_sub i12 i3, integral_sub i1 i2, integral_const_mul,
1563      Torus.integral_realMode]
1564    have := hcoef k
1565    unfold Torus.funRealCoeff at this
1566    rw [this]
1567    simp [hc, Torus.funRealCoeff]
1568  have hgmean : Torus.mean g = 0 := by
1569    unfold Torus.mean
1570    have i1 : Integrable ψ := hψL2.integrable one_le_two
1571    have i3 : Integrable f := hfL2.integrable one_le_two
1572    simp only [hg]
1573    have i12 : Integrable (fun x => ψ x - Torus.mean ψ) := i1.sub (integrable_const _)
1574    rw [integral_sub i12 i3, integral_sub i1 (integrable_const _), hmean0]
1575    simp [Torus.mean]
1576  have hpars := Torus.parseval_realCoeff g hgL2
1577  simp only [hgmean, hgc, ne_eq, OfNat.ofNat_ne_zero, not_false_eq_true, zero_pow, tsum_zero,
1578    add_zero] at hpars
1579  have hz : ∫ x, g x ^ 2 = 0 := by simpa using hpars
1580  have hsq : Integrable (fun x => g x ^ 2) := hgL2.integrable_sq
1581  have h0 : (fun x => g x ^ 2) =ᵐ[volume] 0 :=
1582    (integral_eq_zero_iff_of_nonneg (fun x => sq_nonneg _) hsq).1 hz
1583  filter_upwards [h0] with x hx
1584  have : g x = 0 := by simpa using hx
1585  simp only [hg] at this
1586  linarith
1587

1589lemma tendsto_pair_mul {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
1590    {ι : Type*} {l : Filter ι} [l.IsCountablyGenerated]
1591    {F G : ι → Torus d → ℝ} {f g : Torus d → ℝ}
1592    (hF : ∀ n, Continuous (F n)) (hG : ∀ n, Continuous (G n))
1593    (hFu : TendstoUniformly F f l) (hGu : TendstoUniformly G g l) {Bf Bg : ℝ}
1594    (hfB : ∀ x, |f x| ≤ Bf) (hgB : ∀ x, |g x| ≤ Bg) :
1595    Tendsto (fun n => pair μ (fun x => F n x * G n x)) l (𝓝 (pair μ (fun x => f x * g x))) := by
1596  unfold pair
1597  have hBf : 0 ≤ Bf := (abs_nonneg _).trans (hfB 0)
1598  have hBg : 0 ≤ Bg := (abs_nonneg _).trans (hgB 0)
1599  refine tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence
1600    (bound := fun _ => (Bf + 1) * (Bg + 1)) ?_ ?_ (integrable_const _) ?_
1601  · exact Eventually.of_forall fun n => ((hF n).mul (hG n)).aestronglyMeasurable
1602  · have h1 := (Metric.tendstoUniformly_iff.1 hFu) 1 one_pos
1603    have h2 := (Metric.tendstoUniformly_iff.1 hGu) 1 one_pos
1604    filter_upwards [h1, h2] with n hn1 hn2
1605    refine Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_
1606    have a1 : |F n x| ≤ Bf + 1 := by
1607      have h := hn1 x
1608      rw [Real.dist_eq] at h
1609      have h' := abs_sub_abs_le_abs_sub (F n x) (f x)
1610      rw [abs_sub_comm] at h'
1611      linarith [hfB x]
1612    have a2 : |G n x| ≤ Bg + 1 := by
1613      have h := hn2 x
1614      rw [Real.dist_eq] at h
1615      have h' := abs_sub_abs_le_abs_sub (G n x) (g x)
1616      rw [abs_sub_comm] at h'
1617      linarith [hgB x]
1618    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul]
1619    exact mul_le_mul a1 a2 (abs_nonneg _) (by linarith)
1620  · exact Eventually.of_forall fun x => (hFu.tendsto_at x).mul (hGu.tendsto_at x)
1621

1623lemma summable_trigPoly (m : ℝ) (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ) :
1624    Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d =>
1625      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff (trigPoly T c) k ^ 2 := by
1626  refine summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := T) fun k hk => ?_
1627  rw [funRealCoeff_trigPoly]; simp [hk]

1629lemma yvec_trig_tendsto (m : ℝ) {ψ : Torus d → ℝ}
1630    (hs : Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff ψ k ^ 2) :
1631    Tendsto (fun T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) =>
1632      yvec m (trigPoly T (Torus.funRealCoeff ψ)) (summable_trigPoly m T _)) atTop
1633      (𝓝 (yvec m ψ hs)) := by
1634  set c : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ := Torus.funRealCoeff ψ with hc
1635  set y : NegSobolev d m := yvec m ψ hs with hy
1636  have hyT : ∀ T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d), yvec m (trigPoly T c) (summable_trigPoly m T c) =
1637      ∑ i ∈ T, (lp.single 2 i (NegSobolev.coord y i) :
1638        lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) := by
1639    intro T
1640    refine Subtype.ext (funext fun k => ?_)
1641    simp only [lp.coeFn_sum, Finset.sum_apply, lp.single_apply]
1642    have e1 : NegSobolev.coord (yvec m (trigPoly T c) (summable_trigPoly m T c)) k =
1643        Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m * (if k ∈ T then c k else 0) := by
1644      show Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m * Torus.funRealCoeff (trigPoly T c) k = _
1645      rw [funRealCoeff_trigPoly]
1646    change NegSobolev.coord (yvec m (trigPoly T c) (summable_trigPoly m T c)) k = _
1647    rw [e1]
1648    by_cases hk : k ∈ T
1649    · simp [hk, NegSobolev.coord, hy, yvec, hc]
1650    · simp [hk]
1651  have hsum := lp.hasSum_single (E := fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) (p := 2) ENNReal.ofNat_ne_top
1652    (y : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
1653  simp_rw [hyT]
1654  exact hsum

1656lemma iidCov_congr_rep {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {ψ φ f g : Torus d → ℝ}
1657    {a b : ℝ} (hf : Continuous f) (hg : Continuous g)
1658    (hψ : ∀ᵐ x ∂μ, ψ x = a + f x) (hφ : ∀ᵐ x ∂μ, φ x = b + g x) :
1659    iidCov μ ψ φ = iidCov μ f g := by
1660  have hfi : Integrable f μ := hf.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1661  have hgi : Integrable g μ := hg.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1662  have hfgi : Integrable (fun x => f x * g x) μ :=
1663    (hf.mul hg).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1664  unfold iidCov pair
1665  have e1 : ∫ x, ψ x * φ x ∂μ = ∫ x, ((a * b + a * g x) + (b * f x + f x * g x)) ∂μ := by
1666    refine integral_congr_ae ?_
1667    filter_upwards [hψ, hφ] with x h1 h2
1668    rw [h1, h2]; ring
1669  have e2 : ∫ x, ψ x ∂μ = a + ∫ x, f x ∂μ := by
1670    rw [integral_congr_ae (hψ.mono fun x h => h), integral_add (integrable_const _) hfi]
1671    simp
1672  have e3 : ∫ x, φ x ∂μ = b + ∫ x, g x ∂μ := by
1673    rw [integral_congr_ae (hφ.mono fun x h => h), integral_add (integrable_const _) hgi]
1674    simp
1675  rw [e1, e2, e3]
1676  have i1 : Integrable (fun x => a * b + a * g x) μ :=
1677    (integrable_const _).add (hgi.const_mul a)
1678  have i2 : Integrable (fun x => b * f x + f x * g x) μ := (hfi.const_mul b).add hfgi
1679  rw [integral_add i1 i2, integral_add (integrable_const _) (hgi.const_mul a),
1680    integral_add (hfi.const_mul b) hfgi]
1681  simp only [integral_const_mul, integral_const, probReal_univ, one_smul]
1682  ring
1683
1684
1685lemma gauss_cov_eval (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) / 2 < m) {μ : Measure (Torus d)}
1686    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (hμ : μ ≪ volume) {ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m)}
1687    [IsProbabilityMeasure ν] (hν : IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m ν (iidCov μ)) {ψ φ : Torus d → ℝ}
1688    (hψ : MemSobolev m ψ) (hφ : MemSobolev m φ) :
1689    ∫ F, NegSobolev.eval F ψ * NegSobolev.eval F φ ∂ν = iidCov μ ψ φ := by
1690  have := negSobolev_secondCountable d m
1691  have hG := hν.1.1
1692  have hmem : MemLp id 2 ν := IsGaussian.memLp_two_id
1693  have hmean : ∫ z, id z ∂ν = 0 := gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hν.1
1694  have hcov : ∀ x y : NegSobolev d m, covarianceBilin ν x y =
1695      ∫ F, inner ℝ x F * inner ℝ y F ∂ν := by
1696    intro x y
1697    rw [covarianceBilin_apply hmem]
1698    simp only [hmean, sub_zero]
1699  obtain ⟨fψ, hfψc, hfψae, hfψB, hfψu⟩ := sobolev_rep m hm hψ
1700  obtain ⟨fφ, hfφc, hfφae, hfφB, hfφu⟩ := sobolev_rep m hm hφ
1701  set cψ := Torus.funRealCoeff ψ
1702  set cφ := Torus.funRealCoeff φ
1703  have hsψ := hψ.2
1704  have hsφ := hφ.2
1705  have h1 : ∫ F, NegSobolev.eval F ψ * NegSobolev.eval F φ ∂ν =
1706      covarianceBilin ν (yvec m ψ hsψ) (yvec m φ hsφ) := by
1707    rw [hcov]
1708    refine integral_congr_ae (Eventually.of_forall fun F => ?_)
1709    simp only [eval_eq_inner m ψ hsψ, eval_eq_inner m φ hsφ]
1710  rw [h1]
1711  have hcont : Continuous (fun p : NegSobolev d m × NegSobolev d m => covarianceBilin ν p.1 p.2) :=
1712    (covarianceBilin ν).continuous₂
1713  have hA := (hcont.tendsto (yvec m ψ hsψ, yvec m φ hsφ)).comp
1714    ((yvec_trig_tendsto m hsψ).prodMk_nhds (yvec_trig_tendsto m hsφ))
1715  have hEq : ∀ T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d),
1716      covarianceBilin ν (yvec m (trigPoly T cψ) (summable_trigPoly m T cψ))
1717        (yvec m (trigPoly T cφ) (summable_trigPoly m T cφ)) =
1718      iidCov μ (trigPoly T cψ) (trigPoly T cφ) := by
1719    intro T
1720    rw [hcov]
1721    have : ∀ F : NegSobolev d m, inner ℝ (yvec m (trigPoly T cψ) (summable_trigPoly m T cψ)) F *
1722        inner ℝ (yvec m (trigPoly T cφ) (summable_trigPoly m T cφ)) F =
1723        NegSobolev.eval F (trigPoly T cψ) * NegSobolev.eval F (trigPoly T cφ) := by
1724      intro F
1725      rw [eval_eq_inner m _ (summable_trigPoly m T cψ), eval_eq_inner m _ (summable_trigPoly m T cφ)]
1726    simp_rw [this]
1727    exact hν.2 _ _ (isSmooth_trigPoly T cψ) (isSmooth_trigPoly T cφ)
1728  have hA' : Tendsto (fun T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) => iidCov μ (trigPoly T cψ) (trigPoly T cφ))
1729      atTop (𝓝 (covarianceBilin ν (yvec m ψ hsψ) (yvec m φ hsφ))) := by
1730    refine hA.congr fun T => ?_
1731    exact hEq T
1732  have hB : Tendsto (fun T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) => iidCov μ (trigPoly T cψ) (trigPoly T cφ))
1733      atTop (𝓝 (iidCov μ fψ fφ)) := by
1734    unfold iidCov
1735    refine Tendsto.sub ?_ (Tendsto.mul ?_ ?_)
1736    · exact tendsto_pair_mul (fun T => continuous_trigPoly T cψ) (fun T => continuous_trigPoly T cφ)
1737        hfψu hfφu hfψB hfφB
1738    · have := tendsto_pair_mul (μ := μ) (G := fun _ _ => (1 : ℝ)) (g := fun _ => 1)
1739        (fun T => continuous_trigPoly T cψ) (fun _ => continuous_const) hfψu
1740        (tendsto_const_nhds (x := (1:ℝ))).tendstoUniformly_const hfψB (fun _ => by simp : ∀ x : Torus d, |(1 : ℝ)| ≤ 1)
1741      simpa using this
1742    · have := tendsto_pair_mul (μ := μ) (G := fun _ _ => (1 : ℝ)) (g := fun _ => 1)
1743        (fun T => continuous_trigPoly T cφ) (fun _ => continuous_const) hfφu
1744        (tendsto_const_nhds (x := (1:ℝ))).tendstoUniformly_const hfφB (fun _ => by simp : ∀ x : Torus d, |(1 : ℝ)| ≤ 1)
1745      simpa using this
1746  have hrep := iidCov_congr_rep (μ := μ) hfψc hfφc (hμ.ae_le hfψae) (hμ.ae_le hfφae)
1747  rw [hrep]
1748  exact tendsto_nhds_unique hA' hB
1749

1751lemma gauss_law_eval (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) / 2 < m) {μ : Measure (Torus d)}
1752    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (hμ : μ ≪ volume) {ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m)}
1753    [IsProbabilityMeasure ν] (hν : IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m ν (iidCov μ)) {p : ℕ}
1754    (ψ : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : ∀ a, MemSobolev m (ψ a)) (M : Matrix (Fin p) (Fin p) ℝ)
1755    (hM : M.PosSemidef) (hMe : ∀ a b, M a b = iidCov μ (ψ a) (ψ b)) :
1756    ν.map (fun F => (WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p))) =
1757      multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) M := by
1758  have := negSobolev_secondCountable d m
1759  have hG := hν.1.1
1760  have hmeanν : ∫ z, id z ∂ν = 0 := gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hν.1
1761  set y : Fin p → NegSobolev d m := fun a => yvec m (ψ a) (hψ a).2 with hy
1762  let A : NegSobolev d m →L[ℝ] EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) :=
1763    ∑ a : Fin p, (innerSL ℝ (y a)).smulRight (EuclideanSpace.single a (1 : ℝ))
1764  have hA : ∀ F, A F = (WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) :
1765      EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) := by
1766    intro F
1767    ext b
1768    simp [A, Pi.single_apply, eval_eq_inner m (ψ b) (hψ b).2 F, hy]
1769  have hfun : (fun F => (WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) :
1770      EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p))) = fun F => A F := funext fun F => (hA F).symm
1771  rw [hfun]
1772  have hGA : IsGaussian (ν.map A) := inferInstance
1773  have hGM : IsGaussian (multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) M) := inferInstance
1774  have hmA : (ν.map A)[id] = 0 := by
1775    show ∫ z, id z ∂(ν.map A) = 0
1776    rw [integral_map A.continuous.aemeasurable aestronglyMeasurable_id]
1777    simp only [id]
1778    rw [A.integral_comp_id_comm IsGaussian.integrable_id]
1779    have : ∫ F, F ∂ν = 0 := hmeanν
1780    rw [this, map_zero]
1781  refine IsGaussian.ext (hmA.trans integral_id_multivariateGaussian'.symm) ?_
1782  ext u v
1783  rw [covarianceBilin_multivariateGaussian hM]
1784  have hmemA : MemLp id 2 (ν.map A) := IsGaussian.memLp_two_id
1785  rw [covarianceBilin_apply hmemA, hmA, integral_map A.continuous.aemeasurable (by fun_prop)]
1786  simp only [id, sub_zero]
1787  have hint : ∀ a b, Integrable (fun F : NegSobolev d m => inner ℝ (y a) F * inner ℝ (y b) F) ν :=
1788    fun a b => (IsGaussian.memLp_dual ν (innerSL ℝ (y a)) 2 (by simp)).integrable_mul
1789      (IsGaussian.memLp_dual ν (innerSL ℝ (y b)) 2 (by simp))
1790  have hcb : ∀ a b, ∫ F, inner ℝ (y a) F * inner ℝ (y b) F ∂ν = M a b := by
1791    intro a b
1792    rw [hMe, ← gauss_cov_eval m hm hμ hν (hψ a) (hψ b)]
1793    refine integral_congr_ae (Eventually.of_forall fun F => ?_)
1794    simp only [eval_eq_inner m (ψ a) (hψ a).2, eval_eq_inner m (ψ b) (hψ b).2, hy]
1795  have hin : ∀ (w : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) F, inner ℝ w (A F) = ∑ a, w a * inner ℝ (y a) F := by
1796    intro w F
1797    rw [hA F]
1798    simp [PiLp.inner_apply, mul_comm, hy, eval_eq_inner m _ (hψ _).2]
1799  simp_rw [hin]
1800  simp only [Finset.sum_mul_sum]
1801  have hint2 : ∀ a b, Integrable (fun F : NegSobolev d m =>
1802      u a * inner ℝ (y a) F * (v b * inner ℝ (y b) F)) ν := fun a b =>
1803    ((hint a b).const_mul (u a * v b)).congr (Eventually.of_forall fun F => by ring)
1804  have hL : ∫ F, ∑ a, ∑ b, u a * inner ℝ (y a) F * (v b * inner ℝ (y b) F) ∂ν =
1805      ∑ a, ∑ b, u a * v b * M a b := by
1806    rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun a _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun b _ => hint2 a b]
1807    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
1808    rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun b _ => hint2 a b]
1809    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun b _ => ?_
1810    have : ∀ F : NegSobolev d m, u a * inner ℝ (y a) F * (v b * inner ℝ (y b) F) =
1811        (u a * v b) * (inner ℝ (y a) F * inner ℝ (y b) F) := fun F => by ring
1812    simp_rw [this]
1813    rw [integral_const_mul, hcb]
1814  rw [hL]
1815  simp only [dotProduct, Matrix.mulVec, Finset.mul_sum]
1816  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun b _ => ?_
1817  ring
1818

1821lemma integrable_contA (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {f : Torus d → ℝ}
1822    (hf : Continuous f) : Integrable f μ :=
1823  hf.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1824
1825/-- The normalised, centered summand vector `ξ(x) = N^{-1/2} (f_a(x) - μ(f_a))_a`. -/
1826def xiVec (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (N : ℕ) {p : ℕ} (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (x : Torus d) :
1827    EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) :=
1828  WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * (f a x - pair μ (f a))
1829
1830lemma xiVec_apply (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (N : ℕ) {p : ℕ} (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (x : Torus d)
1831    (a : Fin p) : xiVec μ N f x a = (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * (f a x - pair μ (f a)) := rfl

1833lemma continuous_xiVec (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (N : ℕ) {p : ℕ} (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ)
1834    (hf : ∀ a, Continuous (f a)) : Continuous (xiVec μ N f) := by
1835  unfold xiVec
1836  exact (PiLp.continuous_toLp 2 _).comp
1837    (continuous_pi fun a => continuous_const.mul ((hf a).sub continuous_const))

1839lemma abs_pair_le {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {f : Torus d → ℝ} {B : ℝ}
1840    (hB : ∀ x, |f x| ≤ B) : |pair μ f| ≤ B := by
1841  unfold pair
1842  have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := μ) (f := f) (C := B)
1843    (Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simpa using hB x)
1844  simpa using this
1845
1846lemma abs_xiVec_le {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (N : ℕ) {p : ℕ}
1847    (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) {B : ℝ} (hB : ∀ a x, |f a x| ≤ B) (x : Torus d) (a : Fin p) :
1848    |xiVec μ N f x a| ≤ 2 * B / Real.sqrt N := by
1849  rw [xiVec_apply, abs_mul, abs_inv, abs_of_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _)]
1850  have h1 : |f a x - pair μ (f a)| ≤ 2 * B :=
1851    (abs_sub _ _).trans (by linarith [hB a x, abs_pair_le (μ := μ) (hB a)])
1852  rw [div_eq_inv_mul]
1853  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (inv_nonneg.2 (Real.sqrt_nonneg _))
1854
1855lemma norm_xiVec_le {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (N : ℕ) {p : ℕ}
1856    (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) {B : ℝ} (hB0 : 0 ≤ B) (hB : ∀ a x, |f a x| ≤ B) (x : Torus d) :
1857    ‖xiVec μ N f x‖ ≤ Real.sqrt p * (2 * B / Real.sqrt N) := by
1858  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
1859  have hc : 0 ≤ 2 * B / Real.sqrt N := by positivity
1860  calc Real.sqrt (∑ a, ‖xiVec μ N f x a‖ ^ 2) ≤ Real.sqrt (∑ _a : Fin p, (2 * B / Real.sqrt N) ^ 2) := by
1861        refine Real.sqrt_le_sqrt (Finset.sum_le_sum fun a _ => ?_)
1862        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]
1863        exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) (abs_xiVec_le N f hB x a) 2
1864    _ = Real.sqrt p * (2 * B / Real.sqrt N) := by
1865        rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul,
1866          Real.sqrt_mul (Nat.cast_nonneg _), Real.sqrt_sq hc]
1867
1868lemma integral_xiVec_coord {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (N : ℕ) {p : ℕ}
1869    (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (hf : ∀ a, Continuous (f a)) (c : Fin p) :
1870    ∫ x, xiVec μ N f x c ∂μ = 0 := by
1871  simp only [xiVec_apply]
1872  rw [integral_const_mul, integral_sub (integrable_contA μ (hf c)) (integrable_const _)]
1873  simp [pair]
1874
1875lemma integral_xiVec_mul {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N)
1876    {p : ℕ} (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (hf : ∀ a, Continuous (f a)) (a b : Fin p) :
1877    ∫ x, xiVec μ N f x a * xiVec μ N f x b ∂μ = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * iidCov μ (f a) (f b) := by
1878  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1879  have hsq : (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
1880    rw [← mul_inv, Real.mul_self_sqrt hNpos.le]
1881  have e : ∀ x, xiVec μ N f x a * xiVec μ N f x b =
1882      (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * ((f a x * f b x - pair μ (f b) * f a x) - pair μ (f a) * f b x +
1883        pair μ (f a) * pair μ (f b)) := by
1884    intro x
1885    simp only [xiVec_apply]
1886    rw [← hsq]; ring
1887  simp_rw [e]
1888  have ia := integrable_contA μ (hf a)
1889  have ib := integrable_contA μ (hf b)
1890  have iab : Integrable (fun x => f a x * f b x) μ := integrable_contA μ ((hf a).mul (hf b))
1891  have i1 : Integrable (fun x => f a x * f b x - pair μ (f b) * f a x) μ := iab.sub (ia.const_mul _)
1892  have i2 : Integrable (fun x => (f a x * f b x - pair μ (f b) * f a x) - pair μ (f a) * f b x) μ :=
1893    i1.sub (ib.const_mul _)
1894  rw [integral_const_mul, integral_add i2 (integrable_const _), integral_sub i1 (ib.const_mul _),
1895    integral_sub iab (ia.const_mul _), integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul]
1896  simp only [integral_const, probReal_univ, one_smul]
1897  unfold iidCov pair
1898  ring

1900lemma integral_norm_sq_xiVec_le {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {N : ℕ}
1901    (hN : 1 ≤ N) {p : ℕ} (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (hf : ∀ a, Continuous (f a)) {B : ℝ}
1902    (hB : ∀ a x, |f a x| ≤ B) :
1903    ∫ x, ‖xiVec μ N f x‖ ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ p * B ^ 2 / N := by
1904  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1905  have e : ∀ x, ‖xiVec μ N f x‖ ^ 2 = ∑ a, xiVec μ N f x a * xiVec μ N f x a := by
1906    intro x
1907    rw [EuclideanSpace.real_norm_sq_eq]
1908    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => by ring
1909  simp_rw [e]
1910  have hi : ∀ a, Integrable (fun x => xiVec μ N f x a * xiVec μ N f x a) μ := by
1911    intro a
1912    have hc : Continuous fun x => xiVec μ N f x a := by
1913      simp only [xiVec_apply]; exact continuous_const.mul ((hf a).sub continuous_const)
1914    exact integrable_contA μ (hc.mul hc)
1915  rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun a _ => hi a]
1916  calc ∑ a, ∫ x, xiVec μ N f x a * xiVec μ N f x a ∂μ = ∑ a : Fin p, (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * iidCov μ (f a) (f a) :=
1917        Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => integral_xiVec_mul hN f hf a a
1918    _ ≤ ∑ _a : Fin p, (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * B ^ 2 := by
1919        refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun a _ => ?_
1920        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (inv_nonneg.2 hNpos.le)
1921        unfold iidCov
1922        have h1 : pair μ (fun x => f a x * f a x) ≤ B ^ 2 := by
1923          unfold pair
1924          have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := μ) (f := fun x => f a x * f a x) (C := B ^ 2)
1925            (Eventually.of_forall fun x => by
1926              rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul]
1927              nlinarith [hB a x, abs_nonneg (f a x)])
1928          simpa using (le_abs_self _).trans (by simpa using this)
1929        nlinarith [sq_nonneg (pair μ (f a))]
1930    _ = p * B ^ 2 / N := by
1931        rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]; field_simp

1933lemma rpow_eighth_facts {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) :
1934    0 < (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8) ∧ (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8) ≤ 1 ∧
1935      ((N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8)) ^ 4 = (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ := by
1936  have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1937  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
1938  refine ⟨Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hNpos _, Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hN1 (by norm_num), ?_⟩
1939  rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hNpos.le, Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, ← Real.rpow_neg hNpos.le]
1940  norm_num

1942lemma coupling_numeric {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (p : ℕ) {B L c : ℝ} (hB : 0 ≤ B) (hc : 0 ≤ c)
1943    (hL : L ≤ 2 * (Real.sqrt p * p) * B ^ 3 / Real.sqrt N) :
1944    2 * ((B + 1) * (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8)) * Real.sqrt p +
1945      2 / ((B + 1) * (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8)) ^ 2 * (c * L) + (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8) ≤
1946    (2 * Real.sqrt p + 4 * c * (Real.sqrt p * p) + 1) * (B + 1) * (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8) := by
1947  obtain ⟨hr0, hr1, hr4⟩ := rpow_eighth_facts hN
1948  set r := (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8) with hr
1949  set q := Real.sqrt p * p with hq
1950  have hq0 : 0 ≤ q := by positivity
1951  have hL' : L ≤ 2 * q * B ^ 3 * r ^ 4 := by
1952    rw [hr4]; rw [div_eq_mul_inv] at hL; linarith
1953  have hB1 : 0 < B + 1 := by linarith
1954  have h2 : 2 / ((B + 1) * r) ^ 2 * (c * L) ≤ 4 * c * q * (B + 1) * r := by
1955    have hden : 0 < ((B + 1) * r) ^ 2 := by positivity
1956    rw [div_mul_eq_mul_div, div_le_iff₀ hden]
1957    have h3 : c * L ≤ c * (2 * q * B ^ 3 * r ^ 4) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hL' hc
1958    have h4 : 2 * (c * L) ≤ 2 * (c * (2 * q * B ^ 3 * r ^ 4)) := by linarith
1959    refine h4.trans ?_
1960    have hB3 : B ^ 3 ≤ (B + 1) ^ 3 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hB (by linarith) 3
1961    have hr2 : r ^ 2 ≤ r := by nlinarith
1962    have hcq : 0 ≤ c * q := mul_nonneg hc hq0
1963    calc 2 * (c * (2 * q * B ^ 3 * r ^ 4)) = 4 * (c * q) * B ^ 3 * r ^ 4 := by ring
1964      _ ≤ 4 * (c * q) * (B + 1) ^ 3 * r ^ 4 := by gcongr
1965      _ = 4 * c * q * (B + 1) * r * ((B + 1) * r) ^ 2 * (r ^ 2 / r) := by field_simp
1966      _ ≤ 4 * c * q * (B + 1) * r * ((B + 1) * r) ^ 2 * 1 := by
1967          gcongr
1968          rw [div_le_one hr0]; exact hr2
1969      _ = _ := by ring
1970  have h5 : 1 ≤ B + 1 := by linarith
1971  have hs0 : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt p := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
1972  calc 2 * ((B + 1) * r) * Real.sqrt p + 2 / ((B + 1) * r) ^ 2 * (c * L) + r
1973      ≤ 2 * ((B + 1) * r) * Real.sqrt p + 4 * c * q * (B + 1) * r + r := by linarith
1974    _ ≤ 2 * ((B + 1) * r) * Real.sqrt p + 4 * c * q * (B + 1) * r + (B + 1) * r := by
1975        have : r ≤ (B + 1) * r := by nlinarith
1976        linarith
1977    _ = (2 * Real.sqrt p + 4 * c * q + 1) * (B + 1) * r := by ring
1978
1979lemma iidStat_eq_of_rep {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N)
1980    {ψ f : Torus d → ℝ} {a : ℝ} (hf : Continuous f) (hrep : ∀ᵐ x ∂μ, ψ x = a + f x)
1981    (X : Config d N) (hX : ∀ i, ψ (X i) = a + f (X i)) :
1982    iidStat μ ψ X = (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * ∑ i, (f (X i) - pair μ f) := by
1983  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1984  have h1 : pair μ ψ = a + pair μ f := by
1985    unfold pair
1986    rw [integral_congr_ae hrep, integral_add (integrable_const _) (integrable_contA μ hf)]
1987    simp
1988  unfold iidStat
1989  rw [pair_empirical (by omega), h1]
1990  simp only [hX, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin,
1991    nsmul_eq_mul, Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
1992  have hs : Real.sqrt N * Real.sqrt N = N := Real.mul_self_sqrt hNpos.le
1993  have hs0 : 0 < Real.sqrt N := Real.sqrt_pos.2 hNpos
1994  field_simp
1995  rw [Real.sq_sqrt hNpos.le]
1996  ring

2000  unfold sobCs; positivity

2015/-- Lemma A.36, `B:eq:iid-coupling` (source lines 11546-11549): for every finite family
2016`ψ_1, …, ψ_p ∈ Y_0 = H^{m₀}` there is a coupling of `X_N^0 ∼ (μ°)^{⊗N}` and `η° ∼ ν` with
2017`E |(⟨ψ_a, √N(μ_N^0 - μ°)⟩)_a - (⟨ψ_a, η°⟩)_a|_{ℓ²} ≤ C_p (1 + max_a ‖ψ_a‖^{3/2}_{H^{m₀}}) N^{-1/8}`.
2018The constant depends on `d, m₀, p` only.  (Hence (I1) with `ε_N = C_p N^{-1/8}`.) -/
2019theorem iid_init_coupling (m₀ : ℝ) (hm₀ : (d : ℝ) / 2 < m₀) (p : ℕ) :
2020    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (Λ : ℝ), IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
2021      ∀ ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m₀), IsProbabilityMeasure ν →
2022        IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m₀ ν (iidCov (withDensityReal ρ)) →
2023      ∀ ψ : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ, (∀ a, MemSobolev m₀ (ψ a)) → ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N →
2024        ∃ (Ω' : Type) (_ : MeasurableSpace Ω') (P' : Measure Ω') (_ : IsProbabilityMeasure P')
2025          (X : Ω' → Config d N) (η : Ω' → NegSobolev d m₀),
2026          Measurable X ∧ Measurable η ∧
2027          law P' X = Measure.pi (fun _ => withDensityReal ρ) ∧ law P' η = ν ∧
2028          ∫⁻ ω, ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => iidStat (withDensityReal ρ) (ψ a) (X ω) :
2029                EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) -
2030              WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval (η ω) (ψ a)‖ₑ ∂P' ≤
2031            ENNReal.ofReal (C * (1 + (⨆ a, sobolevNorm m₀ (ψ a)) ^ ((3 : ℝ) / 2)) *
2032              (N : ℝ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 8)) := by
2033  set K : ℝ := 2 * Real.sqrt p + 4 * (2 + Yurinskii.c4 / 2) * (Real.sqrt p * p) + 1 with hK
2034  refine ⟨2 * K * max (sobCs d m₀) 1, fun ρ Λ hρ hΛ ν hνP hν ψ hψ N hN => ?_⟩
2035  have hμP := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
2036  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμdef
2037  have hμac : μ ≪ volume := withDensityReal_ac ρ
2038  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2039  have := negSobolev_secondCountable d m₀
2040  -- continuous representatives
2041  choose f hfc hfae hfB hfu using fun a => sobolev_rep m₀ hm₀ (hψ a)
2042  set S := ⨆ a, sobolevNorm m₀ (ψ a) with hSdef
2043  have hSa : ∀ a, sobolevNorm m₀ (ψ a) ≤ S := fun a => le_ciSup (f := fun a => sobolevNorm m₀ (ψ a)) (Set.finite_range _).bddAbove a
2044  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := Real.iSup_nonneg fun a => Real.sqrt_nonneg _
2045  have hCs := sobCs_nonneg d m₀
2046  set B := sobCs d m₀ * S with hBdef
2047  have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := mul_nonneg hCs hS0
2048  have hBf : ∀ a x, |f a x| ≤ B := fun a x =>
2049    (hfB a x).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hSa a) hCs)
2050  -- the summand law
2051  set ξ := xiVec μ N f with hξdef
2052  have hξc : Continuous ξ := continuous_xiVec μ N f hfc
2053  have hξm : Measurable ξ := hξc.measurable
2054  set ρ0 : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) := μ.map ξ with hρ0def
2055  have hcoord : ∀ c : Fin p, Measurable fun x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => x c := fun c => by fun_prop
2056  have h0 : ∀ c, ∫ x, x c ∂ρ0 = 0 := by
2057    intro c
2058    rw [hρ0def, integral_map hξm.aemeasurable (hcoord c).aestronglyMeasurable]
2059    exact integral_xiVec_coord N f hfc c
2060  set R := Real.sqrt p * (2 * B / Real.sqrt N) with hR
2061  have hR0 : 0 ≤ R := by positivity
2062  have hξR : ∀ x, ‖ξ x‖ ≤ R := fun x => norm_xiVec_le N f hB0 hBf x
2063  have h3μ : Integrable (fun x => ‖ξ x‖ ^ 3) μ :=
2064    Integrable.of_bound (hξm.norm.pow_const 3).aestronglyMeasurable (R ^ 3)
2065      (ae_of_all _ fun x => by
2066        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
2067        exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (hξR x) 3)
2068  have h3 : Integrable (fun x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ‖x‖ ^ 3) ρ0 := by
2069    rw [hρ0def]
2070    exact (integrable_map_measure (measurable_norm.pow_const 3).aestronglyMeasurable
2071      hξm.aemeasurable).2 h3μ
2072  have hI3 : ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0 ≤ R * (p * B ^ 2 / N) := by
2073    rw [hρ0def, integral_map hξm.aemeasurable (measurable_norm.pow_const 3).aestronglyMeasurable]
2074    have h2 := integral_norm_sq_xiVec_le (μ := μ) hN f hfc hBf
2075    have hi2 : Integrable (fun x => ‖ξ x‖ ^ 2) μ :=
2076      Integrable.of_bound (hξm.norm.pow_const 2).aestronglyMeasurable (R ^ 2)
2077        (ae_of_all _ fun x => by
2078          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
2079          exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (hξR x) 2)
2080    calc ∫ x, ‖ξ x‖ ^ 3 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, R * ‖ξ x‖ ^ 2 ∂μ := by
2081          refine integral_mono h3μ (hi2.const_mul R) fun x => ?_
2082          have := hξR x
2083          nlinarith [norm_nonneg (ξ x), sq_nonneg ‖ξ x‖]
2084      _ = R * ∫ x, ‖ξ x‖ ^ 2 ∂μ := integral_const_mul _ _
2085      _ ≤ R * (p * B ^ 2 / N) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hR0
2086  have hLb : (N : ℝ) * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0 ≤ 2 * (Real.sqrt p * p) * B ^ 3 / Real.sqrt N := by
2087    calc (N : ℝ) * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0 ≤ N * (R * (p * B ^ 2 / N)) :=
2088          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hI3 hNpos.le
2089      _ = 2 * (Real.sqrt p * p) * B ^ 3 / Real.sqrt N := by
2090          rw [hR]; field_simp
2091  -- the law of the statistic
2092  set T : Config d N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) := fun X => ∑ i, ξ (X i) with hTdef
2093  have hsm : Measurable (fun q : Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ∑ i, q i) :=
2094    Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => measurable_pi_apply i

2096    Measurable.of_eval fun i => hξm.comp (measurable_pi_apply i)
2097  have hTm : Measurable T := hsm.comp hξi
2098  have hlawT : (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ).map T =
2099      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ0).map (fun q => ∑ i, q i) := by
2100    have e : T = (fun q : Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ∑ i, q i) ∘
2101        (fun (X : Config d N) (i : Fin N) => ξ (X i)) := rfl
2102    rw [e, ← Measure.map_map hsm hξi, Measure.pi_map_pi (fun i => hξm.aemeasurable)]
2103  -- the Gaussian side
2104  have h2ρ : Integrable (fun x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ‖x‖ ^ 2) ρ0 :=
2105    (Yurinskii.integrable_norm_of_pow3 (P := ρ0) (Y := id) measurable_id h3).2
2106  have hMpsd : (∑ _i : Fin N, Yurinskii.momentMatrix ρ0).PosSemidef :=
2107    Matrix.posSemidef_sum _ fun _ _ => Yurinskii.momentMatrix_posSemidef _ h2ρ
2108  have hMe : ∀ a b, (∑ _i : Fin N, Yurinskii.momentMatrix ρ0) a b = iidCov μ (ψ a) (ψ b) := by
2109    intro a b
2110    simp only [Matrix.sum_apply, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin,
2111      nsmul_eq_mul]
2112    have h1 : Yurinskii.momentMatrix ρ0 a b = ∫ x, ξ x a * ξ x b ∂μ := by
2113      simp only [Yurinskii.momentMatrix, Matrix.of_apply, hρ0def]

2116      (hμac.ae_le (hfae b))
2117    rw [h1, integral_xiVec_mul hN f hfc a b, h2]

2119  have hevc : ∀ a, Continuous fun F : NegSobolev d m₀ => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) := fun a => by
2120    have : (fun F : NegSobolev d m₀ => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a)) =

2122    rw [this]; exact continuous_const.inner continuous_id

2124    fun F => WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) with hT'def

2137    hT'c.measurable ρ0 h0 h3 hlawT hlawT' (ε := (B + 1) * r) (η := r) (by positivity) hr0

2139    (c := 2 + Yurinskii.c4 / 2) hB0 (by have := Yurinskii.c4_nonneg; positivity) hLb

2141  have hae : ∀ᵐ X ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ), ∀ a, iidStat μ (ψ a) X = T X a := by

2153  refine ⟨Config d N × NegSobolev d m₀, inferInstance, P, hP, Prod.fst, Prod.snd, measurable_fst,
2154    measurable_snd, hP1, hP2, ?_⟩

2156      WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval ω.2 (ψ a)‖ₑ ∂P = ∫⁻ ω, ‖T ω.1 - T' ω.2‖ₑ ∂P := by

2161    simp [hω a, hT'def]

2164  refine hnum.trans ?_
2165  have hmax : B + 1 ≤ max (sobCs d m₀) 1 * (S + 1) := by

2167      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_max_left _ _) hS0

2169    nlinarith

2174    · have : S ^ (1 : ℝ) ≤ S ^ ((3 : ℝ) / 2) := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le h (by norm_num)
2175      rw [Real.rpow_one] at this

2182        gcongr
2183        exact hmax.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS3 hmx |>.trans' (by nlinarith))
2184    _ = 2 * K * max (sobCs d m₀) 1 * (1 + S ^ ((3 : ℝ) / 2)) * r := by ring

2188/-- The exponent `𝖼 = (2γ - d)/(4(5γ + d))` of `B:eq:iid-init-field`. -/
2189def iidFieldExponent (d : ℕ) (γ : ℝ) : ℝ := (2 * γ - d) / (4 * (5 * γ + d))

2193lemma coord_eq_jp_mul_fourierCoeff {m : ℝ} (F : NegSobolev d m) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2194    NegSobolev.coord F k = Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * NegSobolev.fourierCoeff F k := by
2195  unfold NegSobolev.fourierCoeff
2196  rw [← mul_assoc, ← Real.rpow_add (Torus.jp_pos k.1)]
2197  simp

2199lemma coord_ofMeasureDiff {m : ℝ} (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) (μ ν : Measure (Torus d))
2200    [IsFiniteMeasure μ] [IsFiniteMeasure ν] (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2201    NegSobolev.coord (NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff m μ ν) k =
2202      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * (Torus.measureRealCoeff μ k - Torus.measureRealCoeff ν k) := by
2203  rw [coord_eq_jp_mul_fourierCoeff, NegSobolev.fourierCoeff_ofMeasureDiff hm μ ν k]

2205/-- The scaled mode `⟨k⟩^{-γ} e_k`. -/
2206def scMode (γ : ℝ) (k : NonzeroLattice d) : Torus d → ℝ :=
2207  fun x => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.realMode k x

2209lemma continuous_scMode (γ : ℝ) (k : NonzeroLattice d) : Continuous (scMode γ k) :=
2210  continuous_const.mul (continuous_realMode k)

2212lemma abs_scMode_le (γ : ℝ) (k : NonzeroLattice d) (x : Torus d) :
2213    |scMode γ k x| ≤ Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) := by
2214  unfold scMode
2215  rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _), mul_comm]
2216  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (Torus.abs_realMode_le k x) (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _)

2218lemma isSmooth_scMode (γ : ℝ) (k : NonzeroLattice d) : Torus.IsSmooth (scMode γ k) := by
2219  have : scMode γ k = trigPoly {k} (fun _ => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) := by
2220    funext x; simp [scMode, trigPoly]
2221  rw [this]; exact isSmooth_trigPoly _ _

2223lemma funRealCoeff_scMode (γ : ℝ) (k l : NonzeroLattice d) :
2224    Torus.funRealCoeff (scMode γ k) l = if l = k then Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) else 0 := by
2225  have : scMode γ k = trigPoly {k} (fun _ => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) := by
2226    funext x; simp [scMode, trigPoly]
2227  rw [this, funRealCoeff_trigPoly]

2230lemma memSobolev_scMode (m γ : ℝ) (k : NonzeroLattice d) : MemSobolev m (scMode γ k) := by
2231  refine ⟨(continuous_scMode γ k).memLp_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _),
2232    ?_⟩
2233  refine summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := {k}) fun l hl => ?_
2234  rw [funRealCoeff_scMode]
2235  simp [show l ≠ k by simpa using hl]

2237lemma eval_scMode {γ : ℝ} (F : NegSobolev d γ) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2238    NegSobolev.eval F (scMode γ k) = NegSobolev.coord F k := by
2239  unfold NegSobolev.eval
2240  rw [tsum_eq_single k]
2241  · rw [funRealCoeff_scMode]
2242    simp only [if_true]
2243    have e : Lattice.jp k.1 ^ γ * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) = 1 := by
2244      rw [← Real.rpow_add (Torus.jp_pos k.1)]; simp
2245    unfold NegSobolev.fourierCoeff
2246    linear_combination (NegSobolev.coord F k) * e
2247  · intro l hl
2248    rw [funRealCoeff_scMode]; simp [hl]

2250lemma iidCov_scMode (γ : ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
2251    (k l : NonzeroLattice d) :
2252    iidCov μ (scMode γ k) (scMode γ l) =
2253      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Lattice.jp l.1 ^ (-γ) *
2254        iidCov μ (Torus.realMode k) (Torus.realMode l) := by
2255  have hk := continuous_realMode (d := d) k
2256  have hl := continuous_realMode (d := d) l
2257  have ik : Integrable (Torus.realMode k) μ := hk.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2258  have il : Integrable (Torus.realMode l) μ := hl.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2259  have ikl : Integrable (fun x => Torus.realMode k x * Torus.realMode l x) μ :=
2260    (hk.mul hl).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2261  unfold iidCov pair scMode
2262  have e : ∀ x, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.realMode k x * (Lattice.jp l.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.realMode l x) =
2263      (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Lattice.jp l.1 ^ (-γ)) * (Torus.realMode k x * Torus.realMode l x) :=
2264    fun x => by ring
2265  simp_rw [e]
2266  rw [integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul]
2267  ring

2269lemma gauss_law_of_cov (m : ℝ) {ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m)}
2270    [IsProbabilityMeasure ν] (hν : IsCenteredGaussian ν) {p : ℕ}
2271    (ψ : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : ∀ a, MemSobolev m (ψ a)) (M : Matrix (Fin p) (Fin p) ℝ)
2272    (hM : M.PosSemidef)
2273    (hcov : ∀ a b, ∫ F, NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) * NegSobolev.eval F (ψ b) ∂ν = M a b) :
2274    ν.map (fun F => (WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p))) =
2275      multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) M := by
2276  have := negSobolev_secondCountable d m
2277  have hG := hν.1
2278  have hmeanν : ∫ z, id z ∂ν = 0 := gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hν
2279  set y : Fin p → NegSobolev d m := fun a => yvec m (ψ a) (hψ a).2 with hy
2280  let A : NegSobolev d m →L[ℝ] EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) :=
2281    ∑ a : Fin p, (innerSL ℝ (y a)).smulRight (EuclideanSpace.single a (1 : ℝ))
2282  have hA : ∀ F, A F = (WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) :
2283      EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) := by
2284    intro F
2285    ext b
2286    simp [A, Pi.single_apply, eval_eq_inner m (ψ b) (hψ b).2 F, hy]
2287  have hfun : (fun F => (WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (ψ a) :
2288      EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p))) = fun F => A F := funext fun F => (hA F).symm
2289  rw [hfun]
2290  have hGA : IsGaussian (ν.map A) := inferInstance
2291  have hGM : IsGaussian (multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) M) := inferInstance
2292  have hmA : (ν.map A)[id] = 0 := by
2293    show ∫ z, id z ∂(ν.map A) = 0
2294    rw [integral_map A.continuous.aemeasurable aestronglyMeasurable_id]
2295    simp only [id]
2296    rw [A.integral_comp_id_comm IsGaussian.integrable_id]
2297    have : ∫ F, F ∂ν = 0 := hmeanν
2298    rw [this, map_zero]
2299  refine IsGaussian.ext (hmA.trans integral_id_multivariateGaussian'.symm) ?_
2300  ext u v
2301  rw [covarianceBilin_multivariateGaussian hM]
2302  have hmemA : MemLp id 2 (ν.map A) := IsGaussian.memLp_two_id
2303  rw [covarianceBilin_apply hmemA, hmA, integral_map A.continuous.aemeasurable (by fun_prop)]
2304  simp only [id, sub_zero]
2305  have hint : ∀ a b, Integrable (fun F : NegSobolev d m => inner ℝ (y a) F * inner ℝ (y b) F) ν :=
2306    fun a b => (IsGaussian.memLp_dual ν (innerSL ℝ (y a)) 2 (by simp)).integrable_mul
2307      (IsGaussian.memLp_dual ν (innerSL ℝ (y b)) 2 (by simp))
2308  have hcb : ∀ a b, ∫ F, inner ℝ (y a) F * inner ℝ (y b) F ∂ν = M a b := by
2309    intro a b
2310    rw [← hcov a b]
2311    refine integral_congr_ae (Eventually.of_forall fun F => ?_)
2312    simp only [eval_eq_inner m (ψ a) (hψ a).2, eval_eq_inner m (ψ b) (hψ b).2, hy]
2313  have hin : ∀ (w : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) F, inner ℝ w (A F) = ∑ a, w a * inner ℝ (y a) F := by
2314    intro w F
2315    rw [hA F]
2316    simp [PiLp.inner_apply, mul_comm, hy, eval_eq_inner m _ (hψ _).2]
2317  simp_rw [hin]
2318  simp only [Finset.sum_mul_sum]
2319  have hint2 : ∀ a b, Integrable (fun F : NegSobolev d m =>
2320      u a * inner ℝ (y a) F * (v b * inner ℝ (y b) F)) ν := fun a b =>
2321    ((hint a b).const_mul (u a * v b)).congr (Eventually.of_forall fun F => by ring)
2322  have hL : ∫ F, ∑ a, ∑ b, u a * inner ℝ (y a) F * (v b * inner ℝ (y b) F) ∂ν =
2323      ∑ a, ∑ b, u a * v b * M a b := by
2324    rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun a _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun b _ => hint2 a b]
2325    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
2326    rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun b _ => hint2 a b]
2327    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun b _ => ?_
2328    have : ∀ F : NegSobolev d m, u a * inner ℝ (y a) F * (v b * inner ℝ (y b) F) =
2329        (u a * v b) * (inner ℝ (y a) F * inner ℝ (y b) F) := fun F => by ring
2330    simp_rw [this]
2331    rw [integral_const_mul, hcb]
2332  rw [hL]
2333  simp only [dotProduct, Matrix.mulVec, Finset.mul_sum]
2334  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun b _ => ?_
2335  ring

2340/-- The index of the `j`-th mode of a finite set of modes. -/
2341def kOf (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (j : Fin T.card) : NonzeroLattice d :=
2342  ((T.equivFin).symm j).1
2343
2344lemma sum_kOf (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (g : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ) :
2345    ∑ j : Fin T.card, g (kOf T j) = ∑ k ∈ T, g k := by
2346  have : ∑ j : Fin T.card, g (kOf T j) = ∑ k : T, g k.1 :=
2347    Fintype.sum_equiv (T.equivFin).symm _ _ (fun j => rfl)
2348  rw [this]
2349  exact Finset.sum_coe_sort T g
2350
2351/-- The block of scaled modes. -/
2352def blk (γ : ℝ) (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) : Fin T.card → Torus d → ℝ :=
2353  fun j => scMode γ (kOf T j)

2355lemma abs_pair_scMode_le {γ : ℝ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
2356    (k : NonzeroLattice d) : |pair μ (scMode γ k)| ≤ Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) :=
2357  abs_pair_le (abs_scMode_le γ k)

2359lemma abs_xiBlk_le {γ : ℝ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (N : ℕ)
2360    (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (x : Torus d) (j : Fin T.card) :
2361    |xiVec μ N (blk γ T) x j| ≤ (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * (2 * Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp (kOf T j).1 ^ (-γ)) := by
2362  rw [xiVec_apply, abs_mul, abs_inv, abs_of_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _)]
2363  refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (inv_nonneg.2 (Real.sqrt_nonneg _))
2364  refine (abs_sub _ _).trans ?_
2365  have h1 := abs_scMode_le γ (kOf T j) x
2366  have h2 := abs_pair_scMode_le (γ := γ) (μ := μ) (kOf T j)
2367  simp only [blk]
2368  linarith

2370lemma norm_xiBlk_sq_le {γ : ℝ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N)
2371    (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (x : Torus d) (S : ℝ)
2372    (hS : ∑ k ∈ T, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) ≤ S) :
2373    ‖xiVec μ N (blk γ T) x‖ ^ 2 ≤ 8 * S / N := by
2374  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2375  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq, Real.sq_sqrt (Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => sq_nonneg _)]
2376  calc ∑ j, ‖xiVec μ N (blk γ T) x j‖ ^ 2
2377      ≤ ∑ j : Fin T.card, 8 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * Lattice.jp (kOf T j).1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) := by
2378        refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun j _ => ?_
2379        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]
2380        have h := abs_xiBlk_le (γ := γ) (μ := μ) N T x j
2381        have h0 := abs_nonneg (xiVec μ N (blk γ T) x j)
2382        calc |xiVec μ N (blk γ T) x j| ^ 2
2383            ≤ ((Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * (2 * Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp (kOf T j).1 ^ (-γ))) ^ 2 :=
2384              pow_le_pow_left₀ h0 h 2
2385          _ = 8 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * Lattice.jp (kOf T j).1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) := by
2386              rw [mul_pow, inv_pow, Real.sq_sqrt hNpos.le, mul_pow, mul_pow, Real.sq_sqrt (by norm_num),
2387                Torus.jp_rpow_sq]
2388              rw [show 2 * (-γ) = -(2 * γ) by ring]
2389              ring
2390    _ = 8 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ k ∈ T, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) := by
2391        rw [← Finset.mul_sum, sum_kOf T (fun k => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)))]
2392    _ ≤ 8 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * S := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS (by positivity)
2393    _ = 8 * S / N := by ring

2396lemma integral_norm_sq_xiVec_eq {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {N : ℕ}
2397    (hN : 1 ≤ N) {p : ℕ} (f : Fin p → Torus d → ℝ) (hf : ∀ a, Continuous (f a)) :
2398    ∫ x, ‖xiVec μ N f x‖ ^ 2 ∂μ = ∑ a : Fin p, (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * iidCov μ (f a) (f a) := by
2399  have e : ∀ x, ‖xiVec μ N f x‖ ^ 2 = ∑ a, xiVec μ N f x a * xiVec μ N f x a := by
2400    intro x
2401    rw [EuclideanSpace.real_norm_sq_eq]
2402    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => by ring
2403  simp_rw [e]
2404  have hi : ∀ a, Integrable (fun x => xiVec μ N f x a * xiVec μ N f x a) μ := by
2405    intro a
2406    have hc : Continuous fun x => xiVec μ N f x a := by
2407      simp only [xiVec_apply]; exact continuous_const.mul ((hf a).sub continuous_const)
2408    exact integrable_contA μ (hc.mul hc)
2409  rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun a _ => hi a]
2410  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => integral_xiVec_mul hN f hf a a

2412lemma iidCov_scMode_le {γ : ℝ} (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
2413    (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2414    iidCov μ (scMode γ k) (scMode γ k) ≤ 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) := by
2415  have h1 : pair μ (fun x => scMode γ k x * scMode γ k x) ≤ 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) := by
2416    unfold pair
2417    have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := μ) (f := fun x => scMode γ k x * scMode γ k x)
2418      (C := 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)))
2419      (Eventually.of_forall fun x => by
2420        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul]
2421        have h := abs_scMode_le γ k x
2422        have : (Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) * (Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) =
2423            2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) := by
2424          rw [show -(2 * γ) = (-γ) + (-γ) by ring, Real.rpow_add (Torus.jp_pos k.1)]
2425          have := Real.mul_self_sqrt (show (0:ℝ) ≤ 2 by norm_num)
2426          nlinarith [this]
2427        rw [← this]
2428        exact mul_le_mul h h (abs_nonneg _)
2429          (mul_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _) (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _)))
2430    simpa using (le_abs_self _).trans (by simpa using this)
2431  unfold iidCov
2432  nlinarith [sq_nonneg (pair μ (scMode γ k))]

2435/-- The finite-dimensional block of the field-level coupling. -/
2436theorem block_coupling {γ : ℝ} {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
2437    {ν : Measure (NegSobolev d γ)} [IsProbabilityMeasure ν]
2438    (hν : IsCenteredGaussianWithCov γ ν (iidCov μ)) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N)
2439    (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) {S : ℝ}
2440    (hS : ∑ k ∈ T, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) ≤ S) {ε η : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (hη : 0 < η) :
2441    ∃ P : Measure (Config d N × NegSobolev d γ), IsProbabilityMeasure P ∧
2442      P.map Prod.fst = Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => μ) ∧ P.map Prod.snd = ν ∧
2443      ∫⁻ ω, ‖(∑ i, xiVec μ N (blk γ T) (ω.1 i)) -
2444          (WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin T.card => NegSobolev.coord ω.2 (kOf T j) :
2445            EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin T.card))‖ₑ ∂P ≤
2446        ENNReal.ofReal (2 * ε * Real.sqrt T.card +
2447          2 / ε ^ 2 * ((2 + c4 / 2) * (2 * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S / N))) + η) := by
2448  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2449  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := (Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _).trans hS
2450  have := negSobolev_secondCountable d γ
2451  set p := T.card with hp
2452  set f := blk γ T with hf
2453  have hfc : ∀ a, Continuous (f a) := fun a => continuous_scMode γ _
2454  set ξ := xiVec μ N f with hξdef
2455  have hξc : Continuous ξ := continuous_xiVec μ N f hfc
2456  have hξm : Measurable ξ := hξc.measurable
2457  set ρ0 : Measure (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) := μ.map ξ with hρ0def
2458  have hcoord : ∀ c : Fin p, Measurable fun x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => x c := fun c => by fun_prop
2459  have h0 : ∀ c, ∫ x, x c ∂ρ0 = 0 := by
2460    intro c
2461    rw [hρ0def, integral_map hξm.aemeasurable (hcoord c).aestronglyMeasurable]
2462    exact integral_xiVec_coord N f hfc c
2463  set R := Real.sqrt (8 * S / N) with hR
2464  have hR0 : 0 ≤ R := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
2465  have hξR : ∀ x, ‖ξ x‖ ≤ R := fun x =>
2466    Real.le_sqrt_of_sq_le (norm_xiBlk_sq_le hN T x S hS)
2467  have h3μ : Integrable (fun x => ‖ξ x‖ ^ 3) μ :=
2468    Integrable.of_bound (hξm.norm.pow_const 3).aestronglyMeasurable (R ^ 3)
2469      (ae_of_all _ fun x => by
2470        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
2471        exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (hξR x) 3)
2472  have h3 : Integrable (fun x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ‖x‖ ^ 3) ρ0 := by
2473    rw [hρ0def]
2474    exact (integrable_map_measure (measurable_norm.pow_const 3).aestronglyMeasurable
2475      hξm.aemeasurable).2 h3μ
2476  have hI2 : ∫ x, ‖ξ x‖ ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ 2 * S / N := by
2477    rw [hξdef, integral_norm_sq_xiVec_eq hN f hfc]
2478    calc ∑ a : Fin p, (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * iidCov μ (f a) (f a)
2479        ≤ ∑ a : Fin p, (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * (2 * Lattice.jp (kOf T a).1 ^ (-(2 * γ))) :=
2480          Finset.sum_le_sum fun a _ => mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (iidCov_scMode_le μ (kOf T a))
2481            (inv_nonneg.2 hNpos.le)
2482      _ = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * (2 * ∑ k ∈ T, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ))) := by
2483          rw [← Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.mul_sum,
2484            sum_kOf T (fun k => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)))]
2485      _ ≤ (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * (2 * S) := by gcongr
2486      _ = 2 * S / N := by ring
2487  have hI3 : ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0 ≤ R * (2 * S / N) := by
2488    rw [hρ0def, integral_map hξm.aemeasurable (measurable_norm.pow_const 3).aestronglyMeasurable]
2489    have hi2 : Integrable (fun x => ‖ξ x‖ ^ 2) μ :=
2490      Integrable.of_bound (hξm.norm.pow_const 2).aestronglyMeasurable (R ^ 2)
2491        (ae_of_all _ fun x => by
2492          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
2493          exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (hξR x) 2)
2494    calc ∫ x, ‖ξ x‖ ^ 3 ∂μ ≤ ∫ x, R * ‖ξ x‖ ^ 2 ∂μ := by
2495          refine integral_mono h3μ (hi2.const_mul R) fun x => ?_
2496          have := hξR x
2497          nlinarith [norm_nonneg (ξ x), sq_nonneg ‖ξ x‖]
2498      _ = R * ∫ x, ‖ξ x‖ ^ 2 ∂μ := integral_const_mul _ _
2499      _ ≤ R * (2 * S / N) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hI2 hR0
2500  have hLb : (N : ℝ) * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0 ≤ 2 * S * R := by
2501    calc (N : ℝ) * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0 ≤ N * (R * (2 * S / N)) :=
2502          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hI3 hNpos.le
2503      _ = 2 * S * R := by field_simp
2504  -- the law of the statistic
2505  set T0 : Config d N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) := fun X => ∑ i, ξ (X i) with hT0def
2506  have hsm : Measurable (fun q : Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ∑ i, q i) :=
2507    Finset.measurable_sum _ fun i _ => measurable_pi_apply i
2508  have hξi : Measurable (fun (X : Config d N) (i : Fin N) => ξ (X i)) :=
2509    Measurable.of_eval fun i => hξm.comp (measurable_pi_apply i)
2510  have hTm : Measurable T0 := hsm.comp hξi
2511  have hlawT : (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ).map T0 =
2512      (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => ρ0).map (fun q => ∑ i, q i) := by
2513    have e : T0 = (fun q : Fin N → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ∑ i, q i) ∘
2514        (fun (X : Config d N) (i : Fin N) => ξ (X i)) := rfl
2515    rw [e, ← Measure.map_map hsm hξi, Measure.pi_map_pi (fun i => hξm.aemeasurable)]
2516  have h2ρ : Integrable (fun x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) => ‖x‖ ^ 2) ρ0 :=
2517    (Yurinskii.integrable_norm_of_pow3 (P := ρ0) (Y := id) measurable_id h3).2
2518  have hMpsd : (∑ _i : Fin N, Yurinskii.momentMatrix ρ0).PosSemidef :=
2519    Matrix.posSemidef_sum _ fun _ _ => Yurinskii.momentMatrix_posSemidef _ h2ρ
2520  have hevc : ∀ a, Continuous fun F : NegSobolev d γ => NegSobolev.eval F (f a) := fun a => by
2521    have : (fun F : NegSobolev d γ => NegSobolev.eval F (f a)) =
2522        fun F => inner ℝ (yvec γ (f a) (memSobolev_scMode γ γ (kOf T a)).2) F :=
2523      funext fun F => eval_eq_inner γ _ (memSobolev_scMode γ γ (kOf T a)).2 F
2524    rw [this]; exact continuous_const.inner continuous_id
2525  set T' : NegSobolev d γ → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p) :=
2526    fun F => WithLp.toLp 2 fun a => NegSobolev.eval F (f a) with hT'def
2527  have hT'c : Continuous T' := (PiLp.continuous_toLp 2 _).comp (continuous_pi hevc)
2528  have hcov : ∀ a b, ∫ F, NegSobolev.eval F (f a) * NegSobolev.eval F (f b) ∂ν =
2529      (∑ _i : Fin N, Yurinskii.momentMatrix ρ0) a b := by
2530    intro a b
2531    rw [hν.2 (f a) (f b) (isSmooth_scMode γ (kOf T a)) (isSmooth_scMode γ (kOf T b))]
2532    simp only [Matrix.sum_apply, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin,
2533      nsmul_eq_mul]
2534    have h1 : Yurinskii.momentMatrix ρ0 a b = ∫ x, ξ x a * ξ x b ∂μ := by
2535      simp only [Yurinskii.momentMatrix, Matrix.of_apply, hρ0def]
2536      rw [integral_map hξm.aemeasurable (by fun_prop)]
2537    rw [h1, hξdef, integral_xiVec_mul hN f hfc a b]
2538    field_simp
2539  have hlawT' : ν.map T' = multivariateGaussian (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p))
2540      (∑ _i : Fin N, Yurinskii.momentMatrix ρ0) :=
2541    gauss_law_of_cov γ hν.1 f (fun a => memSobolev_scMode γ γ _) _ hMpsd hcov
2542  have := standardBorel_config d N
2543  have := standardBorel_negSobolev d γ
2544  have : Nonempty (Config d N) := ⟨fun _ => 0⟩
2545  have : Nonempty (NegSobolev d γ) := ⟨0⟩
2546  obtain ⟨P, hP, hP1, hP2, hcost⟩ := coupling_core (μA := Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) ν hTm
2547    hT'c.measurable ρ0 h0 h3 hlawT hlawT' (ε := ε) (η := η) hε hη
2548  refine ⟨P, hP, hP1, hP2, ?_⟩
2549  have hT'eq : ∀ F : NegSobolev d γ, T' F = (WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin p =>
2550      NegSobolev.coord F (kOf T j) : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p)) := by
2551    intro F; ext j; simp [hT'def, hf, blk, eval_scMode]
2552  have hcongr : ∫⁻ ω, ‖(∑ i, ξ (ω.1 i)) - (WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin p =>
2553      NegSobolev.coord ω.2 (kOf T j) : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin p))‖ₑ ∂P =
2554      ∫⁻ ω, ‖T0 ω.1 - T' ω.2‖ₑ ∂P := by
2555    refine lintegral_congr fun ω => ?_
2556    rw [hT'eq]
2557  refine hcongr.trans_le (hcost.trans (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_))
2558  have hc4 := c4_nonneg
2559  have : (2 + c4 / 2) * ((N : ℝ) * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0) ≤ (2 + c4 / 2) * (2 * S * R) :=
2560    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hLb (by positivity)
2561  have h2 : 2 / ε ^ 2 * ((2 + c4 / 2) * ((N : ℝ) * ∫ x, ‖x‖ ^ 3 ∂ρ0)) ≤
2562      2 / ε ^ 2 * ((2 + c4 / 2) * (2 * S * R)) :=
2563    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this (by positivity)
2564  simp only [hT0def] at *
2565  linarith

2568lemma wA_mem (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) (t : Torus d) :
2569    Memℓp (fun k : NonzeroLattice d => scMode m k t) 2 := by
2570  rw [Torus.memℓp_two_iff]
2571  have hs := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) m hm
2572  refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => sq_nonneg _) (fun k => ?_) (hs.mul_left 2)
2573  unfold scMode
2574  rw [mul_pow, Torus.jp_rpow_sq, show 2 * (-m) = -(2 * m) by ring]
2575  have := Torus.abs_realMode_le k t
2576  have h2 : Torus.realMode k t ^ 2 ≤ 2 := by
2577    rw [← sq_abs]
2578    calc |Torus.realMode k t| ^ 2 ≤ Real.sqrt 2 ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) this 2
2579      _ = 2 := Real.sq_sqrt (by norm_num)
2580  nlinarith [Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos k.1).le (-(2 * m))]
2581
2582/-- The point-evaluation vector `x ↦ (⟨k⟩^{-m} e_k(x))_k`. -/
2583def wA (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) (t : Torus d) : NegSobolev d m :=
2584  (⟨fun k => scMode m k t, wA_mem m hm t⟩ : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
2585
2586lemma coord_wA (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) (t : Torus d) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2587    NegSobolev.coord (wA m hm t) k = scMode m k t := rfl
2588
2589lemma continuous_wA (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) : Continuous (wA m hm) := by
2590  have hs := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) m hm
2591  refine continuous_iff_continuousAt.mpr fun s => ?_
2592  set g : Torus d → ℝ := fun t => ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d,
2593    (scMode m k t - scMode m k s) ^ 2 with hg
2594  have hcont : ∀ k : NonzeroLattice d, Continuous fun t : Torus d =>
2595      (scMode m k t - scMode m k s) ^ 2 := fun k =>
2596    ((continuous_scMode m k).sub continuous_const).pow 2
2597  have hbnd : ∀ (k : NonzeroLattice d) (t : Torus d),
2598      ‖(scMode m k t - scMode m k s) ^ 2‖ ≤ 8 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m)) := by
2599    intro k t
2600    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
2601    have h1 := abs_scMode_le m k t
2602    have h2 := abs_scMode_le m k s
2603    have : |scMode m k t - scMode m k s| ≤ 2 * (Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m)) := by
2604      rw [abs_le] at *; constructor <;> linarith [h1.1, h1.2, h2.1, h2.2]
2605    calc (scMode m k t - scMode m k s) ^ 2 = |scMode m k t - scMode m k s| ^ 2 := (sq_abs _).symm
2606      _ ≤ (2 * (Real.sqrt 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m))) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) this 2
2607      _ = 8 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m)) := by
2608          rw [mul_pow, mul_pow, Real.sq_sqrt (by norm_num), Torus.jp_rpow_sq,
2609            show 2 * (-m) = -(2 * m) by ring]
2610          ring
2611  have hgc : Continuous g :=
2612    continuous_tsum (f := fun (k : NonzeroLattice d) (t : Torus d) =>
2613      (scMode m k t - scMode m k s) ^ 2)
2614      (u := fun k => 8 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m))) hcont (hs.mul_left 8) hbnd
2615  have hnorm : ∀ t, ‖wA m hm t - wA m hm s‖ = Real.sqrt (g t) := by
2616    intro t
2617    obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := NegSobolev.coord_sq (wA m hm t - wA m hm s)
2618    rw [← Real.sqrt_sq (norm_nonneg _), h2]
2619    rfl
2620  have hg0 : g s = 0 := by simp [hg]
2621  rw [ContinuousAt, tendsto_iff_norm_sub_tendsto_zero]
2622  simp_rw [hnorm]
2623  have := (Real.continuous_sqrt.comp hgc).continuousAt (x := s)
2624  simpa [Function.comp_def, hg0] using this.tendsto

2626/-- The scaled empirical fluctuation field `√N (μ_N - μ)` in `E_γ`. -/
2627def fld (γ : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (X : Config d N) : NegSobolev d γ :=
2628  Real.sqrt N • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical X) μ
2629
2630lemma pair_zero_measure (φ : Torus d → ℝ) : pair (0 : Measure (Torus d)) φ = 0 := by
2631  simp [pair]
2632
2633lemma coord_fld {γ : ℝ} (hγ : (d : ℝ) < 2 * γ) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
2634    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (X : Config d N) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2635    NegSobolev.coord (fld γ N μ X) k =
2636      (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * ∑ i, (scMode γ k (X i) - pair μ (scMode γ k)) := by
2637  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2638  have hs : Real.sqrt N * Real.sqrt N = N := Real.mul_self_sqrt hNpos.le
2639  have hs0 : 0 < Real.sqrt N := Real.sqrt_pos.2 hNpos
2640  have hemp : IsProbabilityMeasure (empirical X) := empirical_isProbabilityMeasure (by omega) X
2641  unfold fld
2642  show Real.sqrt N * NegSobolev.coord (NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical X) μ) k = _
2643  rw [coord_ofMeasureDiff hγ]
2644  have h1 : Torus.measureRealCoeff (empirical X) k = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ i, Torus.realMode k (X i) :=
2645    pair_empirical (by omega) X (Torus.realMode k)
2646  have h2 : pair μ (scMode γ k) = Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.measureRealCoeff μ k := by
2647    unfold pair scMode Torus.measureRealCoeff
2648    rw [integral_const_mul]
2649  rw [h2]
2650  simp only [scMode]
2651  rw [h1]
2652  have e : ∑ i : Fin N, (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.realMode k (X i) -
2653      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.measureRealCoeff μ k) =
2654      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * ∑ i, Torus.realMode k (X i) -
2655        N * (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.measureRealCoeff μ k) := by
2656    rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum]
2657    simp
2658  rw [e]
2659  generalize Real.sqrt (N : ℝ) = s at hs hs0 ⊢
2660  rw [← hs]
2661  field_simp
2662
2663
2664lemma coord_sub' {m : ℝ} (F G : NegSobolev d m) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2665    NegSobolev.coord (F - G) k = NegSobolev.coord F k - NegSobolev.coord G k := rfl
2666
2667lemma coord_add' {m : ℝ} (F G : NegSobolev d m) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2668    NegSobolev.coord (F + G) k = NegSobolev.coord F k + NegSobolev.coord G k := rfl
2669
2670lemma coord_zero' {m : ℝ} (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2671    NegSobolev.coord (0 : NegSobolev d m) k = 0 := rfl
2672
2673lemma coord_smul' {m : ℝ} (c : ℝ) (F : NegSobolev d m) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2674    NegSobolev.coord (c • F) k = c * NegSobolev.coord F k := rfl
2675
2676lemma coord_sum' {m : ℝ} {ι : Type*} (s : Finset ι) (F : ι → NegSobolev d m)
2677    (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2678    NegSobolev.coord (∑ i ∈ s, F i) k = ∑ i ∈ s, NegSobolev.coord (F i) k := by
2679  classical
2680  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
2681  | empty => simp [coord_zero']
2682  | insert a s ha ih => rw [Finset.sum_insert ha, Finset.sum_insert ha, coord_add', ih]
2683
2684lemma fld_eq {γ : ℝ} (hγ : (d : ℝ) < 2 * γ) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
2685    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (X : Config d N) :
2686    fld γ N μ X = (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ • ∑ i, wA γ hγ (X i) -
2687      Real.sqrt N • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ μ (0 : Measure (Torus d)) := by
2688  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2689  have hs : Real.sqrt N * Real.sqrt N = N := Real.mul_self_sqrt hNpos.le
2690  have hs0 : 0 < Real.sqrt N := Real.sqrt_pos.2 hNpos
2691  refine Subtype.ext (funext fun k => ?_)
2692  have h1 : NegSobolev.coord (fld γ N μ X) k = _ := coord_fld hγ hN μ X k
2693  have h2 : NegSobolev.coord (NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ μ (0 : Measure (Torus d))) k =
2694      pair μ (scMode γ k) := by
2695    rw [coord_ofMeasureDiff hγ]
2696    unfold pair scMode Torus.measureRealCoeff
2697    rw [integral_const_mul]
2698    simp
2699  show NegSobolev.coord (fld γ N μ X) k = NegSobolev.coord ((Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ • ∑ i, wA γ hγ (X i) -
2700      Real.sqrt N • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ μ (0 : Measure (Torus d))) k
2701  rw [h1, coord_sub', coord_smul', coord_smul', coord_sum', h2]
2702  simp only [coord_wA]
2703  rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
2704  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
2705  generalize Real.sqrt (N : ℝ) = s at hs hs0 ⊢
2706  rw [← hs]
2707  field_simp
2708
2709lemma continuous_fld {γ : ℝ} (hγ : (d : ℝ) < 2 * γ) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
2710    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] : Continuous (fld γ N μ) := by
2711  have : fld γ N μ = fun X => (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ • ∑ i, wA γ hγ (X i) -
2712      Real.sqrt N • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ μ (0 : Measure (Torus d)) :=
2713    funext fun X => fld_eq hγ hN μ X
2714  rw [this]
2715  have hsum : Continuous fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, wA γ hγ (X i) :=
2716    continuous_finset_sum _ fun i _ => (continuous_wA γ hγ).comp (continuous_apply i)
2717  exact (hsum.const_smul (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹).sub continuous_const

2720/-- Projection onto the modes in `T`. -/
2721def projT {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (F : NegSobolev d m) : NegSobolev d m :=
2722  (∑ k ∈ T, (lp.single 2 k (NegSobolev.coord F k) : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) :
2723    lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
2724
2725lemma coord_projT {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (F : NegSobolev d m)
2726    (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2727    NegSobolev.coord (projT T F) k = if k ∈ T then NegSobolev.coord F k else 0 := by
2728  classical
2729  show ((∑ l ∈ T, (lp.single 2 l (NegSobolev.coord F l) : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) :
2730    lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ) k = _
2731  simp only [lp.coeFn_sum, Finset.sum_apply, lp.single_apply]
2732  by_cases hk : k ∈ T
2733  · simp [hk]
2734  · simp [hk]
2735
2736lemma projT_sub {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (F G : NegSobolev d m) :
2737    projT T (F - G) = projT T F - projT T G := by
2738  refine Subtype.ext (funext fun k => ?_)
2739  show NegSobolev.coord (projT T (F - G)) k = NegSobolev.coord (projT T F - projT T G) k
2740  simp only [coord_projT, coord_sub']
2741  split_ifs <;> simp
2742
2743lemma norm_sq_eq_tsum {m : ℝ} (F : NegSobolev d m) :
2744    ‖F‖ ^ 2 = ∑' k, NegSobolev.coord F k ^ 2 := (NegSobolev.coord_sq F).2
2745
2746lemma norm_sq_projT {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (F : NegSobolev d m) :
2747    ‖projT T F‖ ^ 2 = ∑ k ∈ T, NegSobolev.coord F k ^ 2 := by
2748  rw [norm_sq_eq_tsum]
2749  rw [tsum_eq_sum (s := T)]
2750  · exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k hk => by rw [coord_projT, if_pos hk]
2751  · intro k hk; rw [coord_projT, if_neg hk]; simp
2752
2753lemma norm_sq_sub_projT {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (F : NegSobolev d m) :
2754    ‖F - projT T F‖ ^ 2 = ∑' k, (if k ∈ T then 0 else NegSobolev.coord F k ^ 2) := by
2755  rw [norm_sq_eq_tsum]
2756  refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
2757  rw [coord_sub', coord_projT]
2758  split_ifs <;> simp
2759
2760lemma norm_projT_eq {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (F : NegSobolev d m) :
2761    ‖projT T F‖ = ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin T.card => NegSobolev.coord F (kOf T j) :
2762      EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin T.card))‖ := by
2763  have h1 : ‖projT T F‖ ^ 2 = ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin T.card => NegSobolev.coord F (kOf T j) :
2764      EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin T.card))‖ ^ 2 := by
2765    rw [norm_sq_projT, EuclideanSpace.norm_eq, Real.sq_sqrt (Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => sq_nonneg _)]
2766    simp only [Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
2767    rw [sum_kOf T (fun k => NegSobolev.coord F k ^ 2)]
2768  exact (sq_eq_sq₀ (norm_nonneg _) (norm_nonneg _)).1 h1
2769
2770lemma norm_sub_le_blocks {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (v w : NegSobolev d m) :
2771    ‖v - w‖ ≤ ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin T.card => NegSobolev.coord v (kOf T j) :
2772        EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin T.card)) -
2773      (WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin T.card => NegSobolev.coord w (kOf T j) :
2774        EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin T.card))‖ + ‖v - projT T v‖ + ‖w - projT T w‖ := by
2775  have h1 : v - w = projT T (v - w) + ((v - projT T v) - (w - projT T w)) := by
2776    rw [projT_sub]; abel
2777  have h2 : ‖projT T (v - w)‖ = ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin T.card => NegSobolev.coord v (kOf T j) :
2778        EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin T.card)) -
2779      (WithLp.toLp 2 fun j : Fin T.card => NegSobolev.coord w (kOf T j) :
2780        EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin T.card))‖ := by
2781    rw [norm_projT_eq]
2782    congr 1
2783  calc ‖v - w‖ = ‖projT T (v - w) + ((v - projT T v) - (w - projT T w))‖ := by rw [← h1]
2784    _ ≤ ‖projT T (v - w)‖ + ‖(v - projT T v) - (w - projT T w)‖ := norm_add_le _ _
2785    _ ≤ ‖projT T (v - w)‖ + (‖v - projT T v‖ + ‖w - projT T w‖) := by
2786        gcongr; exact norm_sub_le _ _
2787    _ = _ := by rw [h2, add_assoc]
2788
2789
2790lemma abs_coord_le_norm {m : ℝ} (F : NegSobolev d m) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2791    |NegSobolev.coord F k| ≤ ‖F‖ := by
2792  have := lp.norm_apply_le_norm (p := 2) (by norm_num) (F : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2) k
2793  rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
2794  exact this
2795
2796lemma continuous_coord {m : ℝ} (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2797    Continuous fun F : NegSobolev d m => NegSobolev.coord F k := by
2798  have h : ∀ F G : NegSobolev d m, |NegSobolev.coord F k - NegSobolev.coord G k| ≤ ‖F - G‖ := by
2799    intro F G
2800    have := abs_coord_le_norm (F - G) k
2801    simpa [coord_sub'] using this
2802  refine (LipschitzWith.of_dist_le_mul (K := 1) fun F G => ?_).continuous
2803  simpa [Real.dist_eq, dist_eq_norm] using h F G
2804
2805lemma norm_projT_le {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) (F : NegSobolev d m) :
2806    ‖projT T F‖ ≤ ‖F‖ := by
2807  have h : ‖projT T F‖ ^ 2 ≤ ‖F‖ ^ 2 := by
2808    rw [norm_sq_projT, norm_sq_eq_tsum]
2809    exact (NegSobolev.coord_sq F).1.sum_le_tsum T (fun k _ => sq_nonneg _)
2810  exact (sq_le_sq₀ (norm_nonneg _) (norm_nonneg _)).1 h

2812lemma continuous_projT {m : ℝ} (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) :
2813    Continuous (projT (m := m) T) := by
2814  refine (LipschitzWith.of_dist_le_mul (K := 1) fun F G => ?_).continuous
2815  rw [dist_eq_norm, dist_eq_norm, ← projT_sub]
2816  simpa using norm_projT_le T (F - G)

2818lemma lintegral_sq_tail_le {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω)
2819    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {m : ℝ} (F : Ω → NegSobolev d m) (hF : Measurable F)
2820    (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) {b : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ} (hb : Summable b)
2821    (hb0 : ∀ k, 0 ≤ b k)
2822    (hk' : ∀ k, ∫⁻ ω, ENNReal.ofReal (NegSobolev.coord (F ω) k ^ 2) ∂P ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (b k)) :
2823    ∫⁻ ω, ‖F ω - projT T (F ω)‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P ≤
2824      ENNReal.ofReal (∑' k, if k ∈ T then 0 else b k) := by
2825  have hmeas : ∀ k, Measurable fun ω => NegSobolev.coord (F ω) k :=
2826    fun k => (continuous_coord k).measurable.comp hF
2827  have e1 : ∀ ω, ‖F ω - projT T (F ω)‖ₑ ^ 2 =
2828      ∑' k, ENNReal.ofReal (if k ∈ T then 0 else NegSobolev.coord (F ω) k ^ 2) := by
2829    intro ω
2830    rw [← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _), norm_sq_sub_projT]
2831    rw [ENNReal.ofReal_tsum_of_nonneg]
2832    · intro k; split_ifs <;> positivity
2833    · refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => ?_) (fun k => ?_) (NegSobolev.coord_sq (F ω)).1
2834      · split_ifs <;> positivity
2835      · split_ifs <;> simp [sq_nonneg]
2836  simp_rw [e1]
2837  have hm2 : ∀ k, Measurable fun ω => ENNReal.ofReal
2838      (if k ∈ T then 0 else NegSobolev.coord (F ω) k ^ 2) := by
2839    intro k
2840    by_cases hk : k ∈ T
2841    · simp [hk]
2842    · simp only [hk, if_false]
2843      exact ENNReal.measurable_ofReal.comp ((hmeas k).pow_const 2)
2844  rw [lintegral_tsum fun k => (hm2 k).aemeasurable]
2845  rw [ENNReal.ofReal_tsum_of_nonneg (fun k => by split_ifs <;> simp [hb0]) ?_]
2846  · refine ENNReal.tsum_le_tsum fun k => ?_
2847    by_cases hk : k ∈ T
2848    · simp [hk]
2849    · simp only [hk, if_false]
2850      exact hk' k
2851  · exact hb.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => by split_ifs <;> simp [hb0]) (fun k => by split_ifs <;> simp [hb0])
2852

2854lemma lintegral_tail_le {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω)
2855    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {m : ℝ} (F : Ω → NegSobolev d m) (hF : Measurable F)
2856    (T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d)) {b : NonzeroLattice d → ℝ} (hb : Summable b)
2857    (hb0 : ∀ k, 0 ≤ b k)
2858    (hk : ∀ k, ∫⁻ ω, ENNReal.ofReal (NegSobolev.coord (F ω) k ^ 2) ∂P ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (b k)) :
2859    ∫⁻ ω, ‖F ω - projT T (F ω)‖ₑ ∂P ≤
2860      ENNReal.ofReal (Real.sqrt (∑' k, if k ∈ T then 0 else b k)) := by
2861  have hg : Measurable fun ω => F ω - projT T (F ω) :=
2862    hF.sub ((continuous_projT T).measurable.comp hF)
2863  have h2 := lintegral_sq_tail_le P F hF T hb hb0 hk
2864  have hτ0 : 0 ≤ ∑' k, if k ∈ T then (0:ℝ) else b k :=
2865    tsum_nonneg fun k => by split_ifs <;> simp [hb0]
2866  rw [← eLpNorm_one_eq_lintegral_enorm hg.aestronglyMeasurable]
2867  refine (eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_of_exponent_le (p := 1) (q := 2) (by norm_num)).trans ?_
2868  rw [eLpNorm_eq_lintegral_rpow_enorm_toReal (by norm_num) (by norm_num) hg.aestronglyMeasurable]
2869  simp only [ENNReal.toReal_ofNat]
2870  have : ∀ ω, ‖F ω - projT T (F ω)‖ₑ ^ (2 : ℝ) = ‖F ω - projT T (F ω)‖ₑ ^ 2 := fun ω => by
2871    rw [ENNReal.rpow_two]
2872  simp_rw [this]
2873  calc (∫⁻ ω, ‖F ω - projT T (F ω)‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P) ^ (1 / (2 : ℝ)) ≤
2874      (ENNReal.ofReal (∑' k, if k ∈ T then 0 else b k)) ^ (1 / (2 : ℝ)) :=
2875        ENNReal.rpow_le_rpow h2 (by norm_num)
2876    _ = ENNReal.ofReal (Real.sqrt (∑' k, if k ∈ T then 0 else b k)) := by
2877        rw [ENNReal.ofReal_rpow_of_nonneg hτ0 (by norm_num), Real.sqrt_eq_rpow]

2880lemma iidCov_eq_integral_sq {μ : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {f : Torus d → ℝ}
2881    (hf : Continuous f) : iidCov μ f f = ∫ x, (f x - pair μ f) ^ 2 ∂μ := by
2882  have i1 := integrable_contA μ hf
2883  have i2 : Integrable (fun x => f x * f x) μ := integrable_contA μ (hf.mul hf)
2884  have e : ∀ x, (f x - pair μ f) ^ 2 = f x * f x - 2 * pair μ f * f x + pair μ f ^ 2 := fun x => by ring
2885  simp_rw [e]
2886  have j1 : Integrable (fun x => f x * f x - 2 * pair μ f * f x) μ := i2.sub (i1.const_mul _)
2887  rw [integral_add j1 (integrable_const _), integral_sub i2 (i1.const_mul _), integral_const_mul]
2888  simp only [integral_const, probReal_univ, one_smul]
2889  unfold iidCov pair
2890  ring
