2892lemma coord_fld_sq_lintegral {γ : ℝ} (hγ : (d : ℝ) < 2 * γ) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N)
2893    (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
2894    ∫⁻ X, ENNReal.ofReal (NegSobolev.coord (fld γ N μ X) k ^ 2) ∂(Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) ≤
2895      ENNReal.ofReal (2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ))) := by
2896  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2897  set u : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ * (scMode γ k x - pair μ (scMode γ k)) with hu
2898  have huc : Continuous u := continuous_const.mul ((continuous_scMode γ k).sub continuous_const)
2899  have hum : MemLp u 2 μ := huc.memLp_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2900  have hu0 : ∫ x, u x ∂μ = 0 := by
2901    simp only [hu]
2902    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_sub (integrable_contA μ (continuous_scMode γ k))
2903      (integrable_const _)]
2904    simp [pair]
2905  have hsum_eq : ∀ X : Config d N, NegSobolev.coord (fld γ N μ X) k = ∑ i, u (X i) := by
2906    intro X
2907    rw [coord_fld hγ hN μ X k, Finset.mul_sum]
2908  have hU : MemLp (fun X : Config d N => ∑ i, u (X i)) 2 (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) := by
2909    refine memLp_finsetSum _ fun i _ => ?_
2910    exact hum.comp_measurePreserving (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => μ) i)
2911  simp_rw [hsum_eq]
2912  rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hU.integrable_sq
2913    (Eventually.of_forall fun _ => sq_nonneg _)]
2914  refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
2915  rw [integral_sum_sq_iid μ N hum hu0]
2916  have h1 : ∫ x, u x ^ 2 ∂μ = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * iidCov μ (scMode γ k) (scMode γ k) := by
2917    rw [iidCov_eq_integral_sq (continuous_scMode γ k)]
2918    simp only [hu, mul_pow, inv_pow]
2919    rw [integral_const_mul, Real.sq_sqrt hNpos.le]
2920  rw [h1, ← mul_assoc, mul_inv_cancel₀ hNpos.ne', one_mul]
2921  exact iidCov_scMode_le μ k

2946lemma field_numeric {γ d c κ s : ℝ} (hd : 0 ≤ d) (hγ : d / 2 < γ) {N : ℕ} (hN : 1 ≤ N)
2947    (hc : c = (2 * γ - d) / (4 * (5 * γ + d))) (hκ : κ = 1 / (5 * γ + d)) (hs : s = γ + d / 2) :
2948    0 < (N : ℝ) ^ (-c) ∧ (N : ℝ) ^ (-c) ≤ 1 ∧
2949      ((N : ℝ) ^ κ) ^ s * (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ ≤ ((N : ℝ) ^ (-c)) ^ 3 ∧
2950      ((N : ℝ) ^ κ) ^ (-(2 * γ - s) / 2) = (N : ℝ) ^ (-c) := by
2951  have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2952  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
2953  have hγ0 : 0 < γ := by linarith
2954  have h5 : 0 < 5 * γ + d := by linarith
2955  have hc0 : 0 ≤ c := by
2956    have : 0 < 2 * γ - d := by linarith
2957    rw [hc]; positivity
2958  refine ⟨Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hNpos _, Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hN1 (by linarith), ?_, ?_⟩
2959  · rw [← Real.rpow_mul hNpos.le, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hNpos.le,
2960      Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, ← Real.rpow_neg hNpos.le, ← Real.rpow_add hNpos]
2961    refine Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hN1 ?_
2962    simp only [hc, hκ, hs, Nat.cast_ofNat]
2963    rw [← sub_nonneg]
2964    have e : -((2 * γ - d) / (4 * (5 * γ + d))) * 3 - (1 / (5 * γ + d) * (γ + d / 2) + -(1 / 2)) =
2965        3 * d / (4 * (5 * γ + d)) := by
2966      field_simp
2967      ring
2968    rw [e]
2969    positivity
2970  · rw [← Real.rpow_mul hNpos.le]
2971    congr 1
2972    simp only [hc, hκ, hs]
2973    field_simp
2974    ring
2975

2977lemma field_assembly {r q Ss S c' N n : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r) (hq : 0 < q) (hSs : 0 ≤ Ss) (hS : 0 ≤ S)
2978    (hc' : 0 ≤ c') (hN : 0 < N) (hn0 : 0 ≤ n) (hn : n ≤ Ss * q ^ 2)
2979    (hK : q ^ 2 * (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ ≤ r ^ 3) :
2980    2 * (r / q) * Real.sqrt n + 2 / (r / q) ^ 2 * (c' * (2 * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S / N))) + r ≤
2981      (2 * Real.sqrt Ss + 4 * c' * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S) + 1) * r := by
2982  have h1 : Real.sqrt n ≤ Real.sqrt Ss * q := by
2983    calc Real.sqrt n ≤ Real.sqrt (Ss * q ^ 2) := Real.sqrt_le_sqrt hn
2984      _ = Real.sqrt Ss * q := by rw [Real.sqrt_mul hSs, Real.sqrt_sq hq.le]
2985  have h2 : 2 * (r / q) * Real.sqrt n ≤ 2 * Real.sqrt Ss * r := by
2986    calc 2 * (r / q) * Real.sqrt n ≤ 2 * (r / q) * (Real.sqrt Ss * q) :=
2987          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
2988      _ = 2 * Real.sqrt Ss * r := by field_simp
2989  have hsN : 0 < Real.sqrt N := Real.sqrt_pos.2 hN
2990  have h3 : 2 / (r / q) ^ 2 * (c' * (2 * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S / N))) ≤
2991      4 * c' * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S) * r := by
2992    have e : Real.sqrt (8 * S / N) = Real.sqrt (8 * S) * (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹ := by
2993      rw [Real.sqrt_div (by positivity), div_eq_mul_inv]
2994    rw [e]
2995    have hpos : 0 ≤ 4 * c' * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S) := by positivity
2996    calc 2 / (r / q) ^ 2 * (c' * (2 * S * (Real.sqrt (8 * S) * (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹)))
2997        = 4 * c' * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S) * (q ^ 2 * (Real.sqrt N)⁻¹) / r ^ 2 := by
2998          field_simp
2999          ring
3000      _ ≤ 4 * c' * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S) * r ^ 3 / r ^ 2 := by
3001          gcongr
3002      _ = 4 * c' * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S) * r := by field_simp
3003  linarith

3025lemma finite_modes {s K : ℝ} (hs : (d : ℝ) < 2 * (s / 2)) (hK : 0 < K) (hs0 : 0 < s) :

3034  intro hlt
3035  have := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos (Torus.jp_pos k.1) hk (show -(2 * (s / 2)) ≤ 0 by linarith)
3036  linarith

3058/-- Lemma A.38 `M:B:lem:iid-init-field` (source lines 11581-11587): for `γ > d/2`, every
3059probability measure `μ°` on `𝕋^d`, and the Gaussian field `η° ∼ ν` of Example A.17 on
3060`E_γ = H^{-γ}_0`, there is a coupling of `X_N^0 ∼ (μ°)^{⊗N}` and `η°` with
3061`E ‖√N(μ_N^0 - μ°) - η°‖_{H^{-γ}} ≤ C N^{-𝖼} log^{1/2} N`, `𝖼 = (2γ-d)/(4(5γ+d))`, and
3062`E‖√N(μ_N^0 - μ°)‖² + E‖η°‖² ≤ C`, with `C = C(d, γ)` (uniform over `μ°`). -/
3063theorem iid_init_field (γ : ℝ) (hγ : (d : ℝ) / 2 < γ) :
3064    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ (μ : Measure (Torus d)), IsProbabilityMeasure μ →
3065      ∀ ν : Measure (NegSobolev d γ), IsProbabilityMeasure ν →
3066        IsCenteredGaussianWithCov γ ν (iidCov μ) →
3067      ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
3068        ∃ (Ω' : Type) (_ : MeasurableSpace Ω') (P' : Measure Ω') (_ : IsProbabilityMeasure P')
3069          (X : Ω' → Config d N) (η : Ω' → NegSobolev d γ),
3070          Measurable X ∧ Measurable η ∧
3071          law P' X = Measure.pi (fun _ => μ) ∧ law P' η = ν ∧
3072          ∫⁻ ω, ‖Real.sqrt N • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical (X ω)) μ - η ω‖ₑ ∂P' ≤
3073            ENNReal.ofReal (C * (N : ℝ) ^ (-iidFieldExponent d γ) *
3074              Real.sqrt (Real.log N)) ∧
3075          ∫⁻ ω, ‖Real.sqrt N • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical (X ω)) μ‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P' +
3076              ∫⁻ ω, ‖η ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P' ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C := by
3077  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ d := Nat.cast_nonneg d
3078  have hγ2 : (d : ℝ) < 2 * γ := by linarith
3079  set sh : ℝ := (γ + d / 2) / 2 with hsh
3080  have hsh2 : (d : ℝ) < 2 * sh := by rw [hsh]; linarith
3081  have hS_s := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) γ hγ2
3082  have hSs_s := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) sh hsh2
3083  set S : ℝ := ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * γ)) with hSdef
3084  set Ss : ℝ := ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * sh)) with hSsdef
3085  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := tsum_nonneg fun k => Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _
3086  have hSs0 : 0 ≤ Ss := tsum_nonneg fun k => Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _
3087  set c' : ℝ := 2 + Yurinskii.c4 / 2 with hc'
3088  have hc'0 : 0 ≤ c' := by have := Yurinskii.c4_nonneg; positivity
3089  set A' : ℝ := 2 * Real.sqrt Ss + 4 * c' * S * Real.sqrt (8 * S) + 1 + 2 * Real.sqrt (2 * Ss) with hA'
3090  have hA'0 : 0 ≤ A' := by positivity
3091  refine ⟨2 * A' + 4 * S, fun μ hμ ν hνP hν N hN2 => ?_⟩
3092  have hN : 1 ≤ N := by omega
3093  have hN1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
3094  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3095  have h5 : 0 < 5 * γ + d := by linarith
3096  obtain ⟨hr0, hr1, hKr, hKt⟩ := field_numeric (c := iidFieldExponent d γ) (κ := 1 / (5 * γ + d))
3097    (s := γ + d / 2) hd0 hγ hN rfl rfl rfl
3098  set r : ℝ := (N : ℝ) ^ (-iidFieldExponent d γ) with hrdef
3099  set K : ℝ := (N : ℝ) ^ (1 / (5 * γ + d)) with hKdef
3100  have hKpos : 0 < K := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hNpos _
3101  set q : ℝ := K ^ sh with hqdef
3102  have hqpos : 0 < q := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hKpos _
3103  have hq2 : q ^ 2 = K ^ (γ + d / 2) := by
3104    rw [hqdef, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hKpos.le, hsh]
3105    congr 1; push_cast; ring
3106  -- the finite set of modes
3107  have hfin := finite_modes (d := d) (s := γ + d / 2) (K := K) (by linarith) hKpos (by linarith)
3108  set T : Finset (NonzeroLattice d) := hfin.toFinset with hT
3109  have hTmem : ∀ k, k ∈ T ↔ Lattice.jp k.1 ≤ K := fun k => by simp [hT]
3110  have h2sh : 2 * sh = γ + d / 2 := by rw [hsh]; ring
3111  -- (A) cardinality
3112  have hq2' : q ^ 2 = K ^ (2 * sh) := by
3113    rw [hqdef, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hKpos.le]; congr 1; push_cast; ring

3289/-- Lemma A.38, positivity of the exponent and its limit `1/10` as `γ → ∞` (source lines
329011586-11587). -/
3291theorem iidFieldExponent_pos_tendsto (hd : 1 ≤ d) :
3292    (∀ γ : ℝ, (d : ℝ) / 2 < γ → 0 < iidFieldExponent d γ) ∧
3293      Tendsto (iidFieldExponent d) atTop (𝓝 (1 / 10)) := by
3294  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ d := Nat.cast_nonneg d
3295  refine ⟨fun γ hγ => ?_, ?_⟩
3296  · unfold iidFieldExponent
3297    have : 0 < 2 * γ - d := by linarith
3298    have : 0 < 4 * (5 * γ + d) := by nlinarith
3299    positivity
3300  · have h1 : Tendsto (fun γ : ℝ => (2 - (d : ℝ) / γ) / (4 * (5 + (d : ℝ) / γ))) atTop
3301        (𝓝 ((2 - 0) / (4 * (5 + 0)))) := by
3302      have h0 : Tendsto (fun γ : ℝ => (d : ℝ) / γ) atTop (𝓝 0) :=
3303        tendsto_const_nhds.div_atTop tendsto_id
3304      exact (tendsto_const_nhds.sub h0).div (tendsto_const_nhds.mul (tendsto_const_nhds.add h0))
3305        (by norm_num)
3306    norm_num at h1
3307    refine h1.congr' ?_
3308    filter_upwards [eventually_gt_atTop (0 : ℝ)] with γ hγ
3309    unfold iidFieldExponent
3310    field_simp

3314/-- The lattice point `z_j = (j_1/n, …, j_d/n)` of the uniform lattice of spacing `1/n`. -/
3315def latticePoint (n : ℕ) (j : Fin d → Fin n) : Torus d :=
3316  fun i => ((((j i : ℕ) : ℝ) / (n : ℝ) : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle)
3317
3318/-- The perturbed lattice `Y_N = {z + κ⁻¹ a(z) : z ∈ Λ_N}`, `N = n^d`, indexed through
3319`finFunctionFinEquiv : (Fin d → Fin n) ≃ Fin (n^d)`. -/
3320def perturbedLattice (n : ℕ) (κ : ℝ) (a : Torus d → Euc d) : Config d (n ^ d) :=
3321  fun j => latticePoint n (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm j) +
3322    Torus.mk (κ⁻¹ • a (latticePoint n (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm j)))

3326/-- boundedness of derivatives of periodic lifts, `E`-valued -/
3327theorem bddAbove_norm_iteratedFDeriv_lift_E {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]
3328    (j : ℕ) (φ : Torus d → E) (h : ContDiff ℝ j (Torus.lift φ)) :
3329    BddAbove (Set.range fun x : Euc d => ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) x‖) := by
3330  have hc : Continuous fun x : Euc d => ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) x‖ :=
3331    (h.continuous_iteratedFDeriv le_rfl).norm
3332  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := (isCompact_closedBall (0 : Euc d) d).exists_bound_of_continuousOn hc.continuousOn
3333  refine ⟨‖M‖, ?_⟩
3334  rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩
3335  set m : Lattice d := fun i => ⌊x i⌋ with hm
3336  set y : Euc d := x + Lattice.toEuc (-m) with hy
3337  have hg : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) x‖ = ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) y‖ := by
3338    rw [hy, Torus.iteratedFDeriv_lift_periodic]
3339  have hyc : ∀ i, 0 ≤ y i ∧ y i < 1 := by
3340    intro i
3341    have := Int.floor_le (x i)
3342    have h2 := Int.lt_floor_add_one (x i)
3343    simp only [hy, hm, Lattice.toEuc, PiLp.add_apply, Pi.neg_apply, Int.cast_neg]
3344    constructor <;> linarith
3345  have hyb : y ∈ Metric.closedBall (0 : Euc d) d := by
3346    rw [mem_closedBall_zero_iff, EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
3347    apply Real.sqrt_le_iff.mpr
3348    refine ⟨Nat.cast_nonneg d, ?_⟩
3349    calc ∑ i, ‖y i‖ ^ 2 ≤ ∑ _i : Fin d, (1 : ℝ) := by
3350          apply Finset.sum_le_sum
3351          intro i _
3352          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
3353          have := hyc i
3354          nlinarith
3355      _ = d := by simp
3356      _ ≤ (d : ℝ) ^ 2 := by
3357          rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos d with h0 | h0
3358          · simp [h0]
3359          · have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ d := by exact_mod_cast h0
3360            nlinarith
3361  show ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) x‖ ≤ ‖M‖
3362  rw [hg]
3363  have := hM y hyb
3364  rw [norm_norm] at this
3365  exact this.trans (Real.le_norm_self M)

3367/-- second-order Taylor bound for a function with Lipschitz derivative -/
3368lemma taylor2 {g : Euc d → ℝ} (hg : Differentiable ℝ g) {M : ℝ} (hM0 : 0 ≤ M)
3369    (hM : ∀ u v, ‖fderiv ℝ g u - fderiv ℝ g v‖ ≤ M * ‖u - v‖) (x h : Euc d) :
3370    |g (x + h) - g x - fderiv ℝ g x h| ≤ M * ‖h‖ ^ 2 := by
3371  set k : Euc d → ℝ := fun y => g y - fderiv ℝ g x y with hk
3372  have hkd : ∀ y, HasFDerivAt k (fderiv ℝ g y - fderiv ℝ g x) y := fun y =>
3373    (hg y).hasFDerivAt.sub (fderiv ℝ g x).hasFDerivAt
3374  have hb : ∀ y ∈ Metric.closedBall x ‖h‖, ‖fderiv ℝ g y - fderiv ℝ g x‖ ≤ M * ‖h‖ := by
3375    intro y hy
3376    refine (hM y x).trans ?_
3377    rw [mem_closedBall_iff_norm] at hy
3378    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hy hM0
3379  have := (convex_closedBall x ‖h‖).norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_hasFDerivWithin_le
3380    (f := k) (f' := fun y => fderiv ℝ g y - fderiv ℝ g x) (s := Metric.closedBall x ‖h‖)
3381    (fun y _ => (hkd y).hasFDerivWithinAt) hb (Metric.mem_closedBall_self (norm_nonneg h))
3382    (show x + h ∈ Metric.closedBall x ‖h‖ by simp)
3383  simp only [hk] at this
3384  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
3385  have e : g (x + h) - fderiv ℝ g x (x + h) - (g x - fderiv ℝ g x x) = g (x + h) - g x - fderiv ℝ g x h := by
3386    simp only [map_add]; ring
3387  rw [e] at this
3388  calc _ ≤ M * ‖h‖ * ‖x + h - x‖ := this
3389    _ = M * ‖h‖ ^ 2 := by simp [sq]; ring

3393def zhat (n : ℕ) (j : Fin d → Fin n) : Euc d := WithLp.toLp 2 fun i => ((j i : ℕ) : ℝ) / n

3395/-- the lattice average of a function on `ℝ^d` -/
3396def latAvg (n : ℕ) (f : Euc d → ℝ) (y : Euc d) : ℝ :=
3397  ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, f (zhat n j + y)
3398
3399lemma zhat_shift (n' : ℕ) (i : Fin d) (j : Fin d → Fin (n' + 1)) :
3400    ∃ m : Lattice d, zhat (n' + 1) (j + Pi.single i 1) =
3401      zhat (n' + 1) j + ((1 : ℝ) / (n' + 1 : ℕ)) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ) + Lattice.toEuc m := by
3402  refine ⟨fun l => if l = i ∧ j i = Fin.last n' then -1 else 0, ?_⟩
3403  ext l
3404  simp only [zhat, PiLp.add_apply, PiLp.smul_apply, PiLp.single_apply, Lattice.toEuc,
3405    Pi.add_apply]
3406  by_cases hl : l = i
3407  · subst hl
3408    simp only [Pi.single_eq_same, ite_true, true_and, smul_eq_mul, mul_one]
3409    rw [Fin.val_add_one]
3410    have hn : ((n' + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
3411    by_cases hj : j l = Fin.last n'
3412    · simp only [hj, ite_true, Fin.val_last]
3413      push_cast
3414      field_simp
3415      ring
3416    · simp only [hj, ite_false]
3417      push_cast
3418      field_simp
3419      ring
3420  · simp [hl]
3421
3422
3423lemma latAvg_shift (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) {f : Euc d → ℝ}
3424    (hf : ∀ (y : Euc d) (m : Lattice d), f (y + Lattice.toEuc m) = f y) (i : Fin d) (y : Euc d) :
3425    latAvg n f (y + ((1 : ℝ) / n) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ)) = latAvg n f y := by
3426  obtain ⟨n', rfl⟩ : ∃ n', n = n' + 1 := ⟨n - 1, by omega⟩
3427  unfold latAvg
3428  congr 1
3429  let σ : (Fin d → Fin (n' + 1)) ≃ (Fin d → Fin (n' + 1)) := Equiv.addRight (Pi.single i 1)
3430  rw [← Equiv.sum_comp σ (fun j => f (zhat (n' + 1) j + y))]
3431  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
3432  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := zhat_shift n' i j
3433  show f (zhat (n' + 1) j + (y + ((1 : ℝ) / ((n' + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ))) =
3434    f (zhat (n' + 1) (j + Pi.single i 1) + y)
3435  rw [hm]
3436  have : zhat (n' + 1) j + ((1 : ℝ) / ((n' + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ) +
3437      Lattice.toEuc m + y = (zhat (n' + 1) j + (y + ((1 : ℝ) / ((n' + 1 : ℕ) : ℝ)) •
3438        EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ))) + Lattice.toEuc m := by abel
3439  rw [this, hf]
3440
3441
3442lemma exists_box_rep (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) {f : Euc d → ℝ}
3443    (hf : ∀ (y : Euc d) (m : Lattice d), f (y + Lattice.toEuc m) = f y) (y : Euc d) :
3444    ∃ y' : Euc d, latAvg n f y' = latAvg n f y ∧ ∀ i, |y' i| ≤ 1 / (2 * n) := by
3445  have hnpos : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3446  let P : AddSubgroup (Euc d) :=
3447    { carrier := {v | ∀ y, latAvg n f (y + v) = latAvg n f y}
3448      zero_mem' := fun y => by simp
3449      add_mem' := fun {v w} hv hw y => by
3450        show latAvg n f (y + (v + w)) = latAvg n f y
3451        rw [← add_assoc]; exact (hw _).trans (hv y)
3452      neg_mem' := fun {v} hv y => by
3453        show latAvg n f (y + -v) = latAvg n f y
3454        have := hv (y + -v)
3455        simp only [neg_add_cancel_right] at this
3456        exact this.symm }
3457  have hPi : ∀ i, ((1 : ℝ) / n) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ) ∈ P :=
3458    fun i y => latAvg_shift n hn hf i y
3459  set c : Fin d → ℤ := fun i => ⌊n * y i + 1 / 2⌋ with hc
3460  have hmem : ∑ i, (c i) • (((1 : ℝ) / n) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ)) ∈ P :=
3461    AddSubgroup.sum_mem P fun i _ => P.zsmul_mem (hPi i) (c i)
3462  refine ⟨y + -∑ i, (c i) • (((1 : ℝ) / n) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ)), ?_, ?_⟩
3463  · have := (P.neg_mem hmem) y
3464    exact this
3465  · intro l
3466    have h1 : (∑ i, (c i) • (((1 : ℝ) / n) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ))) l = (c l : ℝ) / n := by
3467      simp only [PiLp.smul_apply, PiLp.single_apply, WithLp.ofLp_sum, Finset.sum_apply,
3468        smul_eq_mul, zsmul_eq_mul]
3469      rw [Finset.sum_eq_single l]
3470      · simp; ring
3471      · intro b _ hb; simp [Ne.symm hb]
3472      · simp
3473    have h2 : (y + -∑ i, (c i) • (((1 : ℝ) / n) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ))) l =
3474        y l - (c l : ℝ) / n := by
3475      simp only [PiLp.add_apply, PiLp.neg_apply, h1]; ring
3476    rw [h2]
3477    have h3 := Int.floor_le (n * y l + 1 / 2)
3478    have h4 := Int.lt_floor_add_one (n * y l + 1 / 2)
3479    have hcl : (c l : ℝ) = ⌊n * y l + 1 / 2⌋ := rfl
3480    rw [← hcl] at h3 h4
3481    have he : (n : ℝ) * (y l - (c l : ℝ) / n) = n * y l - c l := by field_simp
3482    rw [abs_le]
3483    have key : -(1/2 : ℝ) ≤ n * (y l - (c l : ℝ) / n) ∧ n * (y l - (c l : ℝ) / n) ≤ 1 / 2 := by
3484      rw [he]; constructor <;> linarith
3485    have e2 : 1 / (2 * (n : ℝ)) = (1 / 2) / n := by field_simp
3486    rw [e2]
3487    constructor
3488    · calc -(1 / 2 / (n : ℝ)) = (-(1 / 2)) / n := by ring
3489        _ ≤ _ := by rw [div_le_iff₀ hnpos]; nlinarith [key.1]
3490    · rw [le_div_iff₀ hnpos]; nlinarith [key.2]
3491

3493lemma eq_sum_single (v : Euc d) : v = ∑ i, v i • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ) := by
3494  ext l
3495  simp [Finset.sum_apply, Pi.single_apply]
3496
3497lemma norm_le_sum_abs (v : Euc d) : ‖v‖ ≤ ∑ i, |v i| := by
3498  conv_lhs => rw [eq_sum_single v]
3499  refine (norm_sum_le _ _).trans ?_
3500  refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => ?_
3501  rw [norm_smul, PiLp.norm_single]
3502  simp
3503
3504lemma norm_le_card_mul (v : Euc d) {b : ℝ} (h : ∀ i, |v i| ≤ b) : ‖v‖ ≤ d * b := by
3505  refine (norm_le_sum_abs v).trans ?_
3506  calc ∑ i, |v i| ≤ ∑ _i : Fin d, b := Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => h i
3507    _ = d * b := by simp
3508
3509lemma card_lat (n : ℕ) : ((Finset.univ : Finset (Fin d → Fin n)).card : ℝ) = ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
3510  simp

3512lemma latAvg_lip (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) {g : Euc d → ℝ}
3513    (hper : ∀ (y : Euc d) (m : Lattice d), g (y + Lattice.toEuc m) = g y) {L : ℝ} (hL0 : 0 ≤ L)
3514    (hL : ∀ u v, |g u - g v| ≤ L * ‖u - v‖) (y : Euc d) :
3515    |latAvg n g y - latAvg n g 0| ≤ L * (d / (2 * n)) := by
3516  have hnpos : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3517  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
3518  obtain ⟨y', hy', hbox⟩ := exists_box_rep n hn hper y
3519  rw [← hy']
3520  have hy'n : ‖y'‖ ≤ d / (2 * n) := by
3521    have := norm_le_card_mul y' hbox
3522    simpa [div_eq_mul_inv, mul_comm, mul_left_comm, mul_assoc] using this
3523  unfold latAvg
3524  rw [← mul_sub, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr hN)]
3525  have hs : |∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, (g (zhat n j + y') - g (zhat n j + 0))| ≤
3526      ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, L * ‖y'‖ := by
3527    refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans (Finset.sum_le_sum fun j _ => ?_)
3528    refine (hL _ _).trans ?_
3529    simp
3530  have hc : ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, L * ‖y'‖ = ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) * (L * ‖y'‖) := by
3531    simp
3532  rw [hc] at hs
3533  calc ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * |∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, (g (zhat n j + y') - g (zhat n j + 0))|
3534      ≤ ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * (((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) * (L * ‖y'‖)) :=
3535        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hs (inv_nonneg.mpr hN.le)
3536    _ = L * ‖y'‖ := by field_simp
3537    _ ≤ L * (d / (2 * n)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hy'n hL0
3538
3539
3540lemma latAvg_partial_small (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) {g : Euc d → ℝ} (hdiff : Differentiable ℝ g)
3541    {M : ℝ} (hM0 : 0 ≤ M)
3542    (hM : ∀ u v, ‖fderiv ℝ g u - fderiv ℝ g v‖ ≤ M * ‖u - v‖)
3543    (hper : ∀ (y : Euc d) (m : Lattice d), g (y + Lattice.toEuc m) = g y)
3544    (w : Euc d) (i : Fin d) :
3545    |((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + w) (EuclideanSpace.single i 1)|
3546      ≤ M / n := by
3547  have hnpos : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3548  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
3549  set e : Euc d := EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ) with he
3550  set D : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ *
3551    ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + (w + t • e)) e with hD
3552  set φ : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => latAvg n g (w + t • e) with hφ
3553  have hder : ∀ t, HasDerivAt φ (D t) t := by
3554    intro t
3555    have h1 : ∀ j : Fin d → Fin n, HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => g (zhat n j + (w + t • e)))
3556        (fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + (w + t • e)) e) t := by
3557      intro j
3558      have h2 : HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => zhat n j + (w + t • e)) e t := by
3559        have := (((hasDerivAt_id t).smul_const e).const_add w).const_add (zhat n j)
3560        simpa using this
3561      exact (hdiff (zhat n j + (w + t • e))).hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt t h2
3562    have h3 := HasDerivAt.fun_sum (u := (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin d → Fin n))) (fun j _ => h1 j)
3563    exact h3.const_mul _
3564  have hper1 : φ (1 / n) = φ 0 := by
3565    simp only [hφ, zero_smul, add_zero]
3566    have := latAvg_shift n hn hper i w
3567    rw [he]
3568    have e2 : (1 / (n : ℝ)) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ) = (1 / (n : ℝ)) • EuclideanSpace.single i (1 : ℝ) := rfl
3569    simpa using this
3570  obtain ⟨t₀, ht₀, hDt⟩ := exists_hasDerivAt_eq_zero (f := φ) (f' := D)
3571    (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) < 1 / n)
3572    (fun t _ => (hder t).continuousAt.continuousWithinAt) hper1.symm (fun t _ => hder t)
3573  have hD0 : D 0 = ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + w) e := by
3574    simp [hD]
3575  rw [← hD0]
3576  have : D 0 = D 0 - D t₀ := by rw [hDt]; ring
3577  rw [this]
3578  have hdiffs : |D 0 - D t₀| ≤ ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, (M * t₀) := by
3579    simp only [hD, ← mul_sub, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
3580    rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr hN)]
3581    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (inv_nonneg.mpr hN.le)
3582    refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans (Finset.sum_le_sum fun j _ => ?_)
3583    rw [← sub_apply, ← Real.norm_eq_abs]
3584    refine (ContinuousLinearMap.le_opNorm _ _).trans ?_
3585    rw [he, PiLp.norm_single, norm_one, mul_one]
3586    refine (hM _ _).trans ?_
3587    have : zhat n j + (w + (0 : ℝ) • e) - (zhat n j + (w + t₀ • e)) = -(t₀ • e) := by
3588      simp
3589    rw [this, norm_neg, norm_smul, he, PiLp.norm_single, norm_one, mul_one, Real.norm_eq_abs,
3590      abs_of_pos ht₀.1]
3591  refine hdiffs.trans ?_
3592  have hc : ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, (M * t₀) = M * t₀ := by
3593    simp; field_simp
3594  rw [hc]
3595  calc M * t₀ ≤ M * (1 / n) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ht₀.2.le hM0
3596    _ = M / n := by ring
3597

3599lemma latAvg_C2 (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) {g : Euc d → ℝ} (hdiff : Differentiable ℝ g)
3600    {M : ℝ} (hM0 : 0 ≤ M)
3601    (hM : ∀ u v, ‖fderiv ℝ g u - fderiv ℝ g v‖ ≤ M * ‖u - v‖)
3602    (hper : ∀ (y : Euc d) (m : Lattice d), g (y + Lattice.toEuc m) = g y) (y : Euc d) :
3603    |latAvg n g y - latAvg n g 0| ≤ (d * M / n) * (d / (2 * n)) := by
3604  have hnpos : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3605  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
3606  obtain ⟨y', hy', hbox⟩ := exists_box_rep n hn hper y
3607  rw [← hy']
3608  have hy'n : ‖y'‖ ≤ d / (2 * n) := by
3609    have := norm_le_card_mul y' hbox
3610    simpa [div_eq_mul_inv, mul_comm, mul_left_comm, mul_assoc] using this
3611  -- derivative of the lattice average
3612  let DG : Euc d → (Euc d →L[ℝ] ℝ) := fun w =>
3613    ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ • ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + w)
3614  have hDG : ∀ w, HasFDerivAt (latAvg n g) (DG w) w := by
3615    intro w
3616    have h1 : ∀ j : Fin d → Fin n, HasFDerivAt (fun w => g (zhat n j + w))
3617        (fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + w)) w := by
3618      intro j
3619      have := (hdiff (zhat n j + w)).hasFDerivAt.comp w ((hasFDerivAt_id w).const_add (zhat n j))
3620      rw [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_id] at this
3621      exact this
3622    have h3 := HasFDerivAt.fun_sum (u := (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin d → Fin n))) (fun j _ => h1 j)
3623    exact h3.const_mul _ |>.congr_fderiv (by ext v; simp [DG])
3624  have hDGb : ∀ w, ‖DG w‖ ≤ d * M / n := by
3625    intro w
3626    refine ContinuousLinearMap.opNorm_le_bound _ (by positivity) fun v => ?_
3627    have hv : DG w v = ∑ i, v i * (((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ *
3628        ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + w) (EuclideanSpace.single i 1)) := by
3629      conv_lhs => rw [eq_sum_single v]
3630      simp only [map_sum, map_smul, DG, smul_apply, smul_eq_mul,
3631        sum_apply, Finset.mul_sum]
3632    rw [hv, Real.norm_eq_abs]
3633    refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans ?_
3634    calc ∑ i, |v i * (((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ *
3635          ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j + w) (EuclideanSpace.single i 1))|
3636        ≤ ∑ _i : Fin d, ‖v‖ * (M / n) := by
3637          refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => ?_
3638          rw [abs_mul]
3639          exact mul_le_mul (by simpa using PiLp.norm_apply_le v i)
3640            (latAvg_partial_small n hn hdiff hM0 hM hper w i) (abs_nonneg _) (norm_nonneg _)
3641      _ = d * M / n * ‖v‖ := by simp; ring
3642  have hmv := (convex_univ : Convex ℝ (Set.univ : Set (Euc d))).norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_hasFDerivWithin_le
3643    (f := latAvg n g) (f' := DG) (fun w _ => (hDG w).hasFDerivWithinAt) (fun w _ => hDGb w)
3644    (Set.mem_univ 0) (Set.mem_univ y')
3645  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at hmv
3646  refine hmv.trans ?_
3647  rw [sub_zero]
3648  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hy'n (by positivity)

3672lemma torus_quad (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) {f : Torus d → ℝ} (hf : Continuous f) {ε : ℝ}
3673    (h : ∀ y : Euc d, |latAvg n (Torus.lift f) y - latAvg n (Torus.lift f) 0| ≤ ε) :
3674    |((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, f (latticePoint n j) - ∫ x, f x| ≤ ε := by
3675  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
3676  set F : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, f (x + latticePoint n j)
3677    with hF
3678  have hFc : Continuous F := by
3679    refine continuous_const.mul (continuous_finsetSum _ fun j _ => ?_)
3680    exact hf.comp (continuous_id.add continuous_const)
3681  have hFmk : ∀ y : Euc d, F (Torus.mk y) = latAvg n (Torus.lift f) y := by
3682    intro y
3683    simp only [hF, latAvg, Torus.lift, Function.comp, latticePoint_eq, ← mk_add']
3684    congr 1
3685    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
3686    rw [add_comm]
3687  have hint : ∫ x, F x = ∫ x, f x := by
3688    simp only [hF]
3689    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_finsetSum]
3690    · have : ∀ j : Fin d → Fin n, ∫ x, f (x + latticePoint n j) = ∫ x, f x :=
3691        fun j => integral_add_right_eq_self f (latticePoint n j)
3692      simp only [this, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ]
3693      simp
3694      field_simp
3695    · intro j _
3696      exact (hf.comp (continuous_id.add continuous_const)).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
3697        (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
3698  have hF0 : F 0 = ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, f (latticePoint n j) := by
3699    simp [hF]
3700  rw [← hF0, ← hint]
3701  have hFi : Integrable F := hFc.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
3702  have : F 0 - ∫ x, F x = ∫ x, (F 0 - F x) := by
3703    rw [integral_sub (integrable_const _) hFi]
3704    simp
3705  rw [this]
3706  have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := (volume : Measure (Torus d))) (f := fun x => F 0 - F x)
3707    (C := ε) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
3708  · simpa using this
3709  · rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]
3710    have h1 := h (Torus.rep x)
3711    rw [← hFmk, ← hFmk] at h1
3712    rw [Torus.mk_rep, mk_zero'] at h1
3713    rw [abs_sub_comm]
3714    exact h1

3717lemma lip_of_fderiv {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] {g : Euc d → E}
3718    (hg : Differentiable ℝ g) {B : ℝ} (hB : ∀ x, ‖fderiv ℝ g x‖ ≤ B) (u v : Euc d) :
3719    ‖g u - g v‖ ≤ B * ‖u - v‖ :=
3720  (convex_univ : Convex ℝ (Set.univ : Set (Euc d))).norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_fderiv_le
3721    (fun x _ => (hg x)) (fun x _ => hB x) (Set.mem_univ v) (Set.mem_univ u)
3722
3723lemma fderiv_lip {g : Euc d → ℝ} (hg : ContDiff ℝ 2 g) {M : ℝ}
3724    (hM : ∀ x, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 g x‖ ≤ M) (u v : Euc d) :
3725    ‖fderiv ℝ g u - fderiv ℝ g v‖ ≤ M * ‖u - v‖ := by
3726  have hd : Differentiable ℝ (fderiv ℝ g) :=
3727    ((hg.fderiv_right (m := 1) (by norm_num)).differentiable one_ne_zero)
3728  refine lip_of_fderiv hd (fun x => ?_) u v
3729  have := norm_iteratedFDeriv_fderiv (𝕜 := ℝ) (f := g) (x := x) (n := 1)
3730  rw [norm_iteratedFDeriv_one] at this
3731  rw [this]; exact hM x
3732
3733lemma le_sup_E {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] (j : ℕ) (φ : Torus d → E)
3734    (h : ContDiff ℝ j (Torus.lift φ)) (x : Euc d) :
3735    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) x‖ ≤ ⨆ x : Euc d, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) x‖ :=
3736  le_ciSup (bddAbove_norm_iteratedFDeriv_lift_E j φ h) x
3737
3738lemma le_cbNorm {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] (k j : ℕ) (hj : j ≤ k)
3739    (φ : Torus d → E) (hφ : ContDiff ℝ k (Torus.lift φ)) (x : Euc d) :
3740    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) x‖ ≤ Torus.cbNorm k φ := by
3741  have hj' : ContDiff ℝ j (Torus.lift φ) := hφ.of_le (by exact_mod_cast hj)
3742  refine (le_sup_E j φ hj' x).trans ?_
3743  unfold Torus.cbNorm
3744  refine Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => ⨆ x : Euc d, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (Torus.lift φ) x‖)
3745    (fun i _ => Real.iSup_nonneg fun _ => norm_nonneg _) (Finset.mem_range.mpr (by omega))
3746
3747lemma cbNorm_nonneg {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] (k : ℕ)
3748    (φ : Torus d → E) : 0 ≤ Torus.cbNorm k φ :=
3749  Finset.sum_nonneg fun _ _ => Real.iSup_nonneg fun _ => norm_nonneg _
3750
3751
3752/-- the scalar `∇ψ · a` on the torus -/
3753def hT (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (a : Torus d → Euc d) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
3754  ∑ i, Torus.partialDeriv i ψ x * a x i
3755
3756lemma lift_hT (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : Torus.IsCk 1 ψ) (a : Torus d → Euc d) (u : Euc d) :
3757    Torus.lift (hT ψ a) u = fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) u (Torus.lift a u) := by
3758  have e1 : Torus.lift (hT ψ a) u = hT ψ a (Torus.mk u) := rfl
3759  have e2 : Torus.lift a u = a (Torus.mk u) := rfl
3760  rw [e1, e2]
3761  simp only [hT, PDE.partialDeriv_mk _ ψ hψ]
3762  conv_rhs => rw [eq_sum_single (a (Torus.mk u))]
3763  simp only [map_sum, map_smul, smul_eq_mul]
3764  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3765  ring
3766
3767lemma continuous_comp_apply (a : Torus d → Euc d) (ha : Continuous a) (i : Fin d) :
3768    Continuous fun x => a x i :=
3769  (EuclideanSpace.proj i).continuous.comp ha
3770
3771lemma isCk_apply (a : Torus d → Euc d) (ha : Torus.IsCk 1 a) (i : Fin d) :
3772    Torus.IsCk 1 (fun x => a x i) :=
3773  (EuclideanSpace.proj (𝕜 := ℝ) i).contDiff.comp ha
3774
3775lemma partialDeriv_apply_eq_div (a : Torus d → Euc d) (ha : Torus.IsCk 1 a) (x : Torus d) :
3776    Torus.div a x = ∑ i, Torus.partialDeriv i (fun y => a y i) x := by
3777  unfold Torus.div Torus.partialDeriv
3778  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3779  have hda : DifferentiableAt ℝ (Torus.lift a) (Torus.rep x) :=
3780    (ha.differentiable one_ne_zero) _
3781  have : fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift fun y => a y i) (Torus.rep x) =
3782      (EuclideanSpace.proj (𝕜 := ℝ) i).comp (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift a) (Torus.rep x)) :=
3783    ((EuclideanSpace.proj (𝕜 := ℝ) i).hasFDerivAt.comp _ hda.hasFDerivAt).fderiv
3784  rw [this]
3785  rfl
3786
3787lemma integral_hT (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : Torus.IsCk 1 ψ) (a : Torus d → Euc d)
3788    (ha : Torus.IsCk 1 a) :
3789    ∫ x, hT ψ a x = -∫ x, ψ x * Torus.div a x := by
3790  have cψ : Continuous ψ := continuous_of_lift' hψ.continuous
3791  have ca : Continuous a := continuous_of_lift' ha.continuous
3792  have hint1 : ∀ i, Integrable fun x => Torus.partialDeriv i ψ x * a x i := fun i =>
3793    ((PDE.continuous_partialDeriv i ψ hψ).mul (continuous_comp_apply a ca i)).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
3794      (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
3795  have hint2 : ∀ i, Integrable fun x => ψ x * Torus.partialDeriv i (fun y => a y i) x := fun i =>
3796    (cψ.mul (PDE.continuous_partialDeriv i _ (isCk_apply a ha i))).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
3797      (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
3798  simp only [hT]
3799  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => hint1 i)]
3800  have : ∀ x, ψ x * Torus.div a x = ∑ i, ψ x * Torus.partialDeriv i (fun y => a y i) x := by
3801    intro x; rw [partialDeriv_apply_eq_div a ha x, Finset.mul_sum]
3802  simp only [this]
3803  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => hint2 i), ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
3804  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3805  exact PDE.integral_partialDeriv_mul i ψ (fun y => a y i) hψ (isCk_apply a ha i)
3806

3808lemma stat_quad2 (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : Torus.IsCk 2 ψ) :
3809    |((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, ψ (latticePoint n j) - ∫ x, ψ x| ≤
3810      (d * Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ / n) * (d / (2 * n)) := by
3811  have hψ' : ContDiff ℝ 2 (Torus.lift ψ) := hψ
3812  have hψ2 : ContDiff ℝ ((2 : ℕ) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift ψ) := by exact_mod_cast hψ'
3813  have hbd : ∀ x, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 (Torus.lift ψ) x‖ ≤ Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ :=
3814    fun x => le_cbNorm 2 2 le_rfl ψ hψ2 x
3815  have hM0 : 0 ≤ Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ := cbNorm_nonneg 2 ψ
3816  refine torus_quad n hn (continuous_of_lift' hψ'.continuous) fun y => ?_
3817  exact latAvg_C2 n hn (hψ'.differentiable (by norm_num)) hM0 (fderiv_lip hψ' hbd)
3818    (Torus.lift_periodic ψ) y
3819
3820lemma stat_quad1 (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (h : Torus d → ℝ) (hc : Continuous h) {L : ℝ} (hL0 : 0 ≤ L)
3821    (hL : ∀ u v, |Torus.lift h u - Torus.lift h v| ≤ L * ‖u - v‖) :
3822    |((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, h (latticePoint n j) - ∫ x, h x| ≤
3823      L * (d / (2 * n)) :=
3824  torus_quad n hn hc fun y => latAvg_lip n hn (Torus.lift_periodic h) hL0 hL y
3825
3826lemma taylor_avg (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) {κ : ℝ} (hκ : 0 < κ) {g : Euc d → ℝ}
3827    (hdiff : Differentiable ℝ g) {M : ℝ} (hM0 : 0 ≤ M)
3828    (hM : ∀ u v, ‖fderiv ℝ g u - fderiv ℝ g v‖ ≤ M * ‖u - v‖) (A : Euc d → Euc d) {B : ℝ}
3829    (hB : ∀ x, ‖A x‖ ≤ B) :
3830    |((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n,
3831      (g (zhat n j + κ⁻¹ • A (zhat n j)) - g (zhat n j) - κ⁻¹ * fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j) (A (zhat n j)))|
3832      ≤ M * (κ⁻¹ * B) ^ 2 := by
3833  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
3834  have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := by
3835    by_cases h : Nonempty (Euc d)
3836    · obtain ⟨x⟩ := h; exact (norm_nonneg _).trans (hB x)
3837    · exact absurd ⟨0⟩ h
3838  rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr hN)]
3839  have hs : |∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, (g (zhat n j + κ⁻¹ • A (zhat n j)) - g (zhat n j) -
3840      κ⁻¹ * fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j) (A (zhat n j)))| ≤ ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, M * (κ⁻¹ * B) ^ 2 := by
3841    refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans (Finset.sum_le_sum fun j _ => ?_)
3842    have := taylor2 hdiff hM0 hM (zhat n j) (κ⁻¹ • A (zhat n j))
3843    simp only [map_smul, smul_eq_mul] at this
3844    refine this.trans ?_
3845    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hM0
3846    rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr hκ)]
3847    exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hB _) (inv_nonneg.mpr hκ.le)) 2
3848  have hc : ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, M * (κ⁻¹ * B) ^ 2 = ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) * (M * (κ⁻¹ * B) ^ 2) := by
3849    simp
3850  rw [hc] at hs
3851  calc ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * |∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, (g (zhat n j + κ⁻¹ • A (zhat n j)) - g (zhat n j) -
3852      κ⁻¹ * fderiv ℝ g (zhat n j) (A (zhat n j)))|
3853      ≤ ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * (((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) * (M * (κ⁻¹ * B) ^ 2)) :=
3854        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hs (inv_nonneg.mpr hN.le)
3855    _ = M * (κ⁻¹ * B) ^ 2 := by field_simp
3856
3857lemma rpow_card (hd : 1 ≤ d) (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) :
3858    ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (-(2 : ℝ) / d) = ((n : ℝ) ^ 2)⁻¹ := by
3859  have hnpos : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3860  have hd0 : (d : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
3861  rw [Nat.cast_pow, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hnpos.le]
3862  have : (d : ℝ) * (-(2 : ℝ) / d) = -2 := by field_simp
3863  rw [this, Real.rpow_neg hnpos.le]
3864  norm_num
3865
3866
3867lemma pair_perturbed (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (κ : ℝ) (a : Torus d → Euc d) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) :
3868    pair (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) ψ =
3869      ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n,
3870        Torus.lift ψ (zhat n j + κ⁻¹ • Torus.lift a (zhat n j)) := by
3871  have hN : 0 < n ^ d := Nat.pow_pos (by omega : 0 < n)
3872  rw [pair_empirical hN]
3873  congr 1
3874  rw [← Equiv.sum_comp (finFunctionFinEquiv (m := n) (n := d)).symm]
3875  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
3876  show ψ (latticePoint n (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm j) +
3877    Torus.mk (κ⁻¹ • a (latticePoint n (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm j)))) = ψ (Torus.mk _)
3878  rw [mk_add', latticePoint_eq]
3879  rfl
3880
3881
3882lemma final_ineq {D P A κ n u v w : ℝ} (hD : 1 ≤ D) (hP : 0 ≤ P) (hκ : 0 < κ) (hn : 0 < n)
3883    (hu : u = κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2) (hv : v = κ * P / n ^ 2) (hw : w = P * A / n) :
3884    u + D ^ 2 / 2 * v + D * w ≤ (1 + D ^ 2) * (u + v) := by
3885  have hu0 : 0 ≤ u := by rw [hu]; positivity
3886  have hv0 : 0 ≤ v := by rw [hv]; positivity
3887  have hw' : w ≤ (u + v) / 2 := by
3888    have h1 : (u + v) / 2 - w = P * κ⁻¹ * (A - κ / n) ^ 2 / 2 := by
3889      rw [hu, hv, hw]; field_simp; ring
3890    have h2 : 0 ≤ P * κ⁻¹ * (A - κ / n) ^ 2 / 2 := by positivity
3891    linarith
3892  nlinarith [mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ D) hu0, mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ D) hv0,
3893    mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ D - 1) hu0, mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ D - 1) hv0,
3894    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hw' (by linarith : 0 ≤ D), mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ D) (by linarith : 0 ≤ D)) hu0,
3895    mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by linarith : 0 ≤ D) (by linarith : 0 ≤ D)) hv0]
3896
3897
3898lemma stat_main (hd : 1 ≤ d) (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (κ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ) (a : Torus d → Euc d)
3899    (ha : Torus.IsCk 1 a) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : Torus.IsCk 2 ψ) :
3900    |κ * (pair (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) ψ - ∫ x, ψ x) +
3901        ∫ x, ψ x * Torus.div a x| ≤
3902      (1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2) * (κ⁻¹ * Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ * Torus.cbNorm 1 a ^ 2 +
3903        κ * ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (-(2 : ℝ) / d) * Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ) := by
3904  have hnpos : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3905  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) := by positivity
3906  set P := Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ with hPdef
3907  set A := Torus.cbNorm 1 a with hAdef
3908  have hP0 : 0 ≤ P := cbNorm_nonneg 2 ψ
3909  have hA0 : 0 ≤ A := cbNorm_nonneg 1 a
3910  have hg : ContDiff ℝ 2 (Torus.lift ψ) := hψ
3911  have hg2 : ContDiff ℝ ((2 : ℕ) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift ψ) := by exact_mod_cast hg
3912  have ha1 : ContDiff ℝ 1 (Torus.lift a) := ha
3913  have ha1' : ContDiff ℝ ((1 : ℕ) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift a) := by exact_mod_cast ha1
3914  have hψ1 : Torus.IsCk 1 ψ := hψ.of_le (by norm_num)
3915  have hgd : Differentiable ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) := hg.differentiable (by norm_num)
3916  have had : Differentiable ℝ (Torus.lift a) := ha1.differentiable one_ne_zero
3917  have hM2 : ∀ x, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 (Torus.lift ψ) x‖ ≤ P := fun x => le_cbNorm 2 2 le_rfl ψ hg2 x
3918  have hM1 : ∀ x, ‖fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) x‖ ≤ P := fun x => by
3919    have := le_cbNorm 2 1 (by norm_num) ψ hg2 x
3920    rwa [norm_iteratedFDeriv_one] at this
3921  have hB0 : ∀ x, ‖Torus.lift a x‖ ≤ A := fun x => by
3922    have := le_cbNorm 1 0 (by norm_num) a ha1' x
3923    rwa [norm_iteratedFDeriv_zero] at this
3924  have hB1 : ∀ x, ‖fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift a) x‖ ≤ A := fun x => by
3925    have := le_cbNorm 1 1 le_rfl a ha1' x
3926    rwa [norm_iteratedFDeriv_one] at this
3927  have hMlip := fderiv_lip hg hM2
3928  have halip := lip_of_fderiv had hB1
3929  -- Taylor
3930  have ht := taylor_avg n hn hκ hgd hP0 hMlip (Torus.lift a) hB0
3931  -- quadrature of psi
3932  have hq1 := stat_quad2 n hn ψ hψ
3933  -- quadrature of hT
3934  have hcT : Continuous (hT ψ a) := by
3935    unfold hT
3936    refine continuous_finsetSum _ fun i _ => ?_
3937    exact (PDE.continuous_partialDeriv i ψ hψ1).mul (continuous_comp_apply a (continuous_of_lift' ha.continuous) i)
3938  have hL : ∀ u v, |Torus.lift (hT ψ a) u - Torus.lift (hT ψ a) v| ≤ (P * A + P * A) * ‖u - v‖ := by
3939    intro u v
3940    rw [lift_hT ψ hψ1 a u, lift_hT ψ hψ1 a v]
3941    have e : fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) u (Torus.lift a u) - fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) v (Torus.lift a v) =
3942        (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) u - fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) v) (Torus.lift a u) +
3943          fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) v (Torus.lift a u - Torus.lift a v) := by
3944      simp
3945    rw [e, ← Real.norm_eq_abs]
3946    refine (norm_add_le _ _).trans ?_
3947    have t1 : ‖(fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) u - fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) v) (Torus.lift a u)‖ ≤
3948        P * ‖u - v‖ * A :=
3949      (ContinuousLinearMap.le_opNorm _ _).trans
3950        (mul_le_mul (hMlip u v) (hB0 u) (norm_nonneg _) (by positivity))
3951    have t2 : ‖fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) v (Torus.lift a u - Torus.lift a v)‖ ≤ P * (A * ‖u - v‖) :=
3952      (ContinuousLinearMap.le_opNorm _ _).trans
3953        (mul_le_mul (hM1 v) (halip u v) (norm_nonneg _) hP0)
3954    nlinarith [t1, t2]
3955  have hq2 := stat_quad1 n hn (hT ψ a) hcT (by positivity) hL
3956  have hint := integral_hT ψ hψ1 a ha
3957  have hpair := pair_perturbed n hn κ a ψ
3958  have hhT : ∀ j : Fin d → Fin n,
3959      fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) (zhat n j) (Torus.lift a (zhat n j)) = hT ψ a (latticePoint n j) :=
3960    fun j => (lift_hT ψ hψ1 a (zhat n j)).symm
3961  have hlat : ∀ j : Fin d → Fin n, Torus.lift ψ (zhat n j) = ψ (latticePoint n j) := fun j => rfl
3962  set Sy := ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n,
3963    Torus.lift ψ (zhat n j + κ⁻¹ • Torus.lift a (zhat n j)) with hSy
3964  set Sp := ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, ψ (latticePoint n j) with hSp
3965  set Sh := ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, hT ψ a (latticePoint n j) with hSh
3966  have hR : ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n,
3967      (Torus.lift ψ (zhat n j + κ⁻¹ • Torus.lift a (zhat n j)) - Torus.lift ψ (zhat n j) -
3968        κ⁻¹ * fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift ψ) (zhat n j) (Torus.lift a (zhat n j))) =
3969      Sy - Sp - κ⁻¹ * Sh := by
3970    simp only [hhT, hlat, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hSy, hSp, hSh]
3971    ring
3972  rw [hR] at ht
3973  rw [hpair]
3974  have hdecomp : κ * (Sy - ∫ x, ψ x) + ∫ x, ψ x * Torus.div a x =
3975      κ * (Sp - ∫ x, ψ x) + (Sh - ∫ x, hT ψ a x) + κ * (Sy - Sp - κ⁻¹ * Sh) := by
3976    rw [hint]
3977    field_simp
3978    ring
3979  rw [hdecomp]
3980  have hκinv : κ * κ⁻¹ = 1 := mul_inv_cancel₀ hκ.ne'
3981  -- bounds
3982  have b1 : |κ * (Sp - ∫ x, ψ x)| ≤ κ * ((d * P / n) * (d / (2 * n))) := by
3983    rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos hκ]; exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hq1 hκ.le
3984  have b2 : |κ * (Sy - Sp - κ⁻¹ * Sh)| ≤ κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2 := by
3985    rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos hκ]
3986    calc κ * |Sy - Sp - κ⁻¹ * Sh| ≤ κ * (P * (κ⁻¹ * A) ^ 2) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ht hκ.le
3987      _ = κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2 := by field_simp
3988  have b3 : |Sh - ∫ x, hT ψ a x| ≤ (P * A + P * A) * (d / (2 * n)) := hq2
3989  have hfin := final_ineq (D := (d : ℝ)) (P := P) (A := A) (κ := κ) (n := (n : ℝ))
3990    (u := κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2) (v := κ * P / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) (w := P * A / n)
3991    (by exact_mod_cast hd) hP0 hκ hnpos rfl rfl rfl
3992  rw [rpow_card hd n hn]
3993  calc _ ≤ |κ * (Sp - ∫ x, ψ x)| + |Sh - ∫ x, hT ψ a x| + |κ * (Sy - Sp - κ⁻¹ * Sh)| := by
3994        refine (abs_add_le _ _).trans ?_
3995        gcongr
3996        exact abs_add_le _ _
3997    _ ≤ κ * ((d * P / n) * (d / (2 * n))) + (P * A + P * A) * (d / (2 * n)) + κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2 := by
3998        linarith
3999    _ = κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 * (κ * P / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) + d * (P * A / n) := by
4000        field_simp
4001        ring
4002    _ ≤ (1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2) * (κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2 + κ * P / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) := hfin
4003    _ = _ := by
4004        field_simp
4005
4006end InitialDataAux

4009/-- Example A.39 `M:B:ex:lattice` (source lines 11608-11611), statistic level: for
4010`a ∈ C^1(𝕋^d; ℝ^d)` (the source takes `a ∈ C^∞`), `κ > 0`, `N = n^d` and `ψ ∈ C²`,
4011`|⟨ψ, κ(μ_{Y_N} - 1)⟩ + ⟨ψ, Div a⟩| ≤ C(κ⁻¹ ‖ψ‖_{C²} ‖a‖²_{C¹} + κ N^{-2/d} ‖ψ‖_{C²})`
4012with `C = C(d)`.  (The `N^{-1/d} ‖ψ‖_{C²}‖a‖_{C¹}` quadrature error of the first-order term,
4013omitted in the source display, is absorbed by AM-GM.) -/
4014theorem perturbedLattice_stat (hd : 1 ≤ d) :
4015    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n → ∀ κ : ℝ, 0 < κ → ∀ a : Torus d → Euc d, Torus.IsCk 1 a →
4016      ∀ ψ : Torus d → ℝ, Torus.IsCk 2 ψ →
4017        |κ * (pair (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) ψ - ∫ x, ψ x) +
4018            ∫ x, ψ x * Torus.div a x| ≤
4019          C * (κ⁻¹ * Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ * Torus.cbNorm 1 a ^ 2 +
4020            κ * ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (-(2 : ℝ) / d) * Torus.cbNorm 2 ψ) := by
4021  exact ⟨1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2, fun n hn κ hκ a ha ψ hψ => stat_main hd n hn κ hκ a ha ψ hψ⟩

4025lemma bdd_funRealCoeff (f : Torus d → ℝ) (hf : Continuous f) :
4026    ∃ C, ∀ k, |Torus.funRealCoeff f k| ≤ C := by
4027  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := isCompact_univ.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hf.continuousOn
4028  refine ⟨Real.sqrt 2 * B, fun k => ?_⟩
4029  have := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := (volume : Measure (Torus d)))
4030    (f := fun x => Torus.realMode k x * f x) (C := Real.sqrt 2 * B)
4031    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
4032  · simpa [Torus.funRealCoeff] using this
4033  · rw [norm_mul]
4034    exact mul_le_mul (by simpa using Torus.abs_realMode_le k x) (hB x trivial) (norm_nonneg _)
4035      (by positivity)

4037lemma continuous_div (a : Torus d → Euc d) (ha : Torus.IsCk 1 a) : Continuous (Torus.div a) := by
4038  have : Torus.div a = fun x => ∑ i, Torus.partialDeriv i (fun y => a y i) x :=
4039    funext (partialDeriv_apply_eq_div a ha)
4040  rw [this]
4041  exact continuous_finsetSum _ fun i _ => PDE.continuous_partialDeriv i _ (isCk_apply a ha i)

4043lemma field_coord (γ : ℝ) (hγ : (d : ℝ) < 2 * γ) (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) (κ : ℝ)
4044    (a : Torus d → Euc d) (ha : Torus.IsCk 1 a) (k : NonzeroLattice d) :
4045    NegSobolev.coord (κ • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) volume -
4046        NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x)) k =
4047      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * (κ * (pair (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) (Torus.realMode k) -
4048        ∫ x, Torus.realMode k x) + ∫ x, Torus.realMode k x * Torus.div a x) := by
4049  have hN : 0 < n ^ d := Nat.pow_pos (by omega : 0 < n)
4050  have : IsProbabilityMeasure (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) := empirical_isProbabilityMeasure hN _
4051  set μ := empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a) with hμ
4052  have hb : ∃ C, ∀ k, |Torus.measureRealCoeff μ k - Torus.measureRealCoeff volume k| ≤ C := by
4053    refine ⟨Real.sqrt 2 * (μ Set.univ).toReal + Real.sqrt 2 * ((volume : Measure (Torus d)) Set.univ).toReal, fun k => ?_⟩
4054    exact (abs_sub _ _).trans (add_le_add (Torus.abs_measureRealCoeff_le μ k)
4055      (Torus.abs_measureRealCoeff_le volume k))
4056  have hmem1 := NegSobolev.memℓp_of_bounded hγ _ hb
4057  have hbf := bdd_funRealCoeff (fun x => -Torus.div a x) (continuous_div a ha).neg
4058  have hmem2 := NegSobolev.memℓp_of_bounded hγ _ hbf
4059  have h1 : NegSobolev.coord (NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ μ volume) k =
4060      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * (Torus.measureRealCoeff μ k - Torus.measureRealCoeff volume k) :=
4061    NegSobolev.coord_ofSeq_of_mem _ hmem1 k
4062  have h2 : NegSobolev.coord (NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x)) k =
4063      Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * Torus.funRealCoeff (fun x => -Torus.div a x) k :=
4064    NegSobolev.coord_ofSeq_of_mem _ hmem2 k
4065  have h3 : NegSobolev.coord (κ • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ μ volume -
4066        NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x)) k =
4067      κ * NegSobolev.coord (NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ μ volume) k -
4068        NegSobolev.coord (NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x)) k := rfl
4069  rw [h3, h1, h2]
4070  have e1 : Torus.measureRealCoeff μ k = pair μ (Torus.realMode k) := rfl
4071  have e2 : Torus.measureRealCoeff volume k = ∫ x, Torus.realMode k x := rfl
4072  have e3 : Torus.funRealCoeff (fun x => -Torus.div a x) k = -∫ x, Torus.realMode k x * Torus.div a x := by
4073    unfold Torus.funRealCoeff
4074    rw [← integral_neg]
4075    congr 1; funext x; ring
4076  rw [e1, e2, e3]
4077  ring
4078

4080lemma field_main (hd : 1 ≤ d) (γ : ℝ) (hγ : (d : ℝ) / 2 + 2 < γ) :
4081    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n → ∀ κ : ℝ, 0 < κ → ∀ a : Torus d → Euc d, Torus.IsCk 1 a →
4082      ‖κ • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) volume -
4083          NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x)‖ ≤
4084        C * (κ⁻¹ * Torus.cbNorm 1 a ^ 2 + κ * ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (-(2 : ℝ) / d)) := by
4085  have hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * (γ - 2) := by linarith
4086  have hγ' : (d : ℝ) < 2 * γ := by linarith
4087  obtain ⟨C₂, hC₂⟩ := Torus.cbNorm_realMode_le (d := d) 2
4088  have hsum := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) (γ - 2) hm
4089  set S := ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * (γ - 2))) with hS
4090  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := tsum_nonneg fun k => (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (Torus.jp_pos k.1) _).le
4091  refine ⟨(1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2) * |C₂| * Real.sqrt S, fun n hn κ hκ a ha => ?_⟩
4092  have hnpos : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
4093  set A := Torus.cbNorm 1 a with hA
4094  set Nr := ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (-(2 : ℝ) / d) with hNr
4095  have hNr0 : 0 ≤ Nr := Real.rpow_nonneg (by positivity) _
4096  set X := κ⁻¹ * A ^ 2 + κ * Nr with hX
4097  have hX0 : 0 ≤ X := by positivity
4098  set K := (1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2) * |C₂| with hK
4099  have hK0 : 0 ≤ K := by positivity
4100  obtain ⟨hF2, hFn⟩ := NegSobolev.coord_sq (κ • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) volume -
4101          NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x))
4102  have hcoord : ∀ k : NonzeroLattice d,
4103      NegSobolev.coord (κ • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) volume -
4104          NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x)) k ^ 2 ≤
4105        (K * X) ^ 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * (γ - 2))) := by
4106    intro k
4107    have hj := Torus.jp_pos k.1
4108    rw [field_coord γ hγ' n hn κ a ha k]
4109    set T := κ * (pair (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) (Torus.realMode k) -
4110        ∫ x, Torus.realMode k x) + ∫ x, Torus.realMode k x * Torus.div a x with hT'
4111    have h0 : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift (Torus.realMode k)) :=
4112      isSmooth_realMode k
4113    have hsm : Torus.IsCk 2 (Torus.realMode k) := h0.of_le (WithTop.coe_le_coe.mpr le_top)
4114    have hst := stat_main hd n hn κ hκ a ha (Torus.realMode k) hsm
4115    have hP := hC₂ k
4116    have hP0 := cbNorm_nonneg 2 (Torus.realMode k)
4117    set P := Torus.cbNorm 2 (Torus.realMode k) with hPd
4118    have hTb : |T| ≤ K * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2 * X := by
4119      refine hst.trans ?_
4120      calc (1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2) * (κ⁻¹ * P * A ^ 2 + κ * Nr * P) = (1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2) * (P * X) := by
4121            rw [hX]; ring
4122        _ ≤ (1 + (d : ℝ) ^ 2) * (|C₂| * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2 * X) := by
4123            refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ?_ hX0) (by positivity)
4124            exact hP.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_abs_self _) (by positivity))
4125        _ = K * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2 * X := by rw [hK]; ring
4126    have hT2 : T ^ 2 ≤ (K * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2 * X) ^ 2 := by
4127      rw [← sq_abs T]
4128      exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) hTb 2
4129    have hJ : (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) ^ 2 * (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2) ^ 2 =
4130        Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * (γ - 2))) := by
4131      rw [Torus.jp_rpow_sq]
4132      have : (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2) ^ 2 = Lattice.jp k.1 ^ ((4 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
4133        rw [Real.rpow_natCast]; ring
4134      rw [this, ← Real.rpow_add hj]
4135      congr 1; push_cast; ring
4136    calc (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ) * T) ^ 2 = (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) ^ 2 * T ^ 2 := by ring
4137      _ ≤ (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) ^ 2 * (K * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2 * X) ^ 2 :=
4138          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hT2 (by positivity)
4139      _ = (K * X) ^ 2 * ((Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-γ)) ^ 2 * (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ 2) ^ 2) := by ring
4140      _ = _ := by rw [hJ]
4141  have hle : ‖κ • NegSobolev.ofMeasureDiff γ (empirical (perturbedLattice n κ a)) volume -
4142          NegSobolev.ofFun γ (fun x => -Torus.div a x)‖ ^ 2 ≤ (K * X * Real.sqrt S) ^ 2 := by
4143    rw [hFn, mul_pow, Real.sq_sqrt hS0, hS, ← tsum_mul_left]
4144    exact hF2.tsum_le_tsum hcoord (hsum.mul_left _)
4145  have := (pow_le_pow_iff_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (by positivity) (two_ne_zero)).mp hle
4146  calc _ ≤ K * X * Real.sqrt S := this
4147    _ = _ := by rw [hK]; ring
4148
4149end InitialDataAux

4160  exact field_main hd γ hγ

4165lemma smooth_weighted_summable (m : ℝ) (φ : Torus d → ℝ) (hφ : Torus.IsSmooth φ) :
4166    Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff φ k ^ 2 := by
4167  set n : ℕ := ⌈m + (d : ℝ) / 2⌉₊ + 1 with hn
4168  have hnm : m + (d : ℝ) / 2 + 1 ≤ n := by
4169    have := Nat.le_ceil (m + (d : ℝ) / 2)
4170    rw [hn]; push_cast; linarith
4171  have h0 : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift φ) := hφ
4172  have hcd : ContDiff ℝ n (Torus.lift φ) := h0.of_le (by exact_mod_cast le_top)
4173  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := Torus.fcoef_pow_decay hcd
4174  have hcont : Continuous φ := continuous_of_lift' hcd.continuous
4175  have hint : Integrable φ :=
4176    hcont.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
4177  have hsum := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) ((n : ℝ) - m) (by linarith)
4178  refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos _).le _)
4179    (sq_nonneg _)) (fun k => ?_) (hsum.mul_left (2 * B ^ 2))
4180  have hj := Torus.jp_pos k.1
4181  have h1 := Torus.abs_funRealCoeff_le hint k
4182  have h2 : ‖Torus.fcoef φ k.1‖ ≤ B / Lattice.jp k.1 ^ n := by
4183    rw [le_div_iff₀ (pow_pos hj _)]; exact hB k
4184  have h3 : Torus.funRealCoeff φ k ^ 2 ≤ 2 * (B / Lattice.jp k.1 ^ n) ^ 2 := by
4185    rw [← sq_abs]
4186    calc |Torus.funRealCoeff φ k| ^ 2 ≤ (Real.sqrt 2 * ‖Torus.fcoef φ k.1‖) ^ 2 :=
4187          pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h1 2
4188      _ = 2 * ‖Torus.fcoef φ k.1‖ ^ 2 := by
4189          rw [mul_pow, Real.sq_sqrt (by norm_num)]
4190      _ ≤ _ := by gcongr
4191  have h4 : Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * (2 * (B / Lattice.jp k.1 ^ n) ^ 2) =
4192      2 * B ^ 2 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * ((n : ℝ) - m))) := by
4193    rw [show -(2 * ((n : ℝ) - m)) = 2 * m + -(2 * n) by ring, Real.rpow_add hj]
4194    have : Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * (n : ℝ))) = (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ n)⁻¹ ^ 2 := by
4195      rw [Real.rpow_neg hj.le, show 2 * (n : ℝ) = ((2 * n : ℕ) : ℝ) by push_cast; ring,
4196        Real.rpow_natCast, pow_mul', inv_pow]
4197    rw [this, div_eq_mul_inv, mul_pow, inv_pow]
4198    ring
4199  calc _ ≤ Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * (2 * (B / Lattice.jp k.1 ^ n) ^ 2) :=
4200        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 (Real.rpow_nonneg hj.le _)
4201    _ = _ := h4

4204lemma wvec_mem (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) (t : Torus d) :
4205    Memℓp (fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * Torus.realMode k t) 2 := by
4206  rw [Torus.memℓp_two_iff]
4207  have hs := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) m hm
4208  refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => sq_nonneg _) (fun k => ?_) (hs.mul_left 2)
4209  rw [mul_pow, Torus.jp_rpow_sq, show 2 * (-m) = -(2 * m) by ring]
4210  have := Torus.abs_realMode_le k t
4211  have h2 : Torus.realMode k t ^ 2 ≤ 2 := by
4212    rw [← sq_abs]
4213    calc |Torus.realMode k t| ^ 2 ≤ Real.sqrt 2 ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) this 2
4214      _ = 2 := Real.sq_sqrt (by norm_num)
4215  nlinarith [Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos k.1).le (-(2 * m))]

4224lemma norm_wvec_sq_le (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) (t : Torus d) :
4225    ‖wvec m hm t‖ ^ 2 ≤ 2 * ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m)) := by
4226  have hs := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) m hm
4227  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := NegSobolev.coord_sq (wvec m hm t)
4228  rw [h2, ← tsum_mul_left]
4229  refine h1.tsum_le_tsum (fun k => ?_) (hs.mul_left 2)
4230  rw [coord_wvec, mul_pow, Torus.jp_rpow_sq, show 2 * (-m) = -(2 * m) by ring]
4231  have := Torus.abs_realMode_le k t
4232  have h2 : Torus.realMode k t ^ 2 ≤ 2 := by
4233    rw [← sq_abs]
4234    calc |Torus.realMode k t| ^ 2 ≤ Real.sqrt 2 ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) this 2
4235      _ = 2 := Real.sq_sqrt (by norm_num)
4236  nlinarith [Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos k.1).le (-(2 * m))]

4238lemma continuous_wvec (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) : Continuous (wvec m hm) := by
4239  have hs := summable_jp_rpow (d := d) m hm
4240  refine continuous_iff_continuousAt.mpr fun s => ?_
4241  set g : Torus d → ℝ := fun t => ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d,
4242    (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * (Torus.realMode k t - Torus.realMode k s)) ^ 2 with hg
4243  have hcont : ∀ k : NonzeroLattice d, Continuous fun t : Torus d =>
4244      (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * (Torus.realMode k t - Torus.realMode k s)) ^ 2 := fun k =>
4245    (continuous_const.mul ((continuous_realMode k).sub continuous_const)).pow 2
4246  have hbnd : ∀ (k : NonzeroLattice d) (t : Torus d),
4247      ‖(Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * (Torus.realMode k t - Torus.realMode k s)) ^ 2‖ ≤
4248        8 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m)) := by
4249    intro k t
4250    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _), mul_pow, Torus.jp_rpow_sq,
4251      show 2 * (-m) = -(2 * m) by ring]
4252    have h1 := Torus.abs_realMode_le k t
4253    have h2 := Torus.abs_realMode_le k s
4254    have h3 : (Torus.realMode k t - Torus.realMode k s) ^ 2 ≤ 8 := by
4255      rw [← sq_abs]
4256      have : |Torus.realMode k t - Torus.realMode k s| ≤ 2 * Real.sqrt 2 := by
4257        rw [abs_le] at *; constructor <;> linarith [h1.1, h1.2, h2.1, h2.2]
4258      calc _ ≤ (2 * Real.sqrt 2) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) this 2
4259        _ = 8 := by rw [mul_pow, Real.sq_sqrt (by norm_num)]; norm_num
4260    nlinarith [Real.rpow_nonneg (Torus.jp_pos k.1).le (-(2 * m))]
4261  have hgc : Continuous g :=
4262    continuous_tsum (f := fun (k : NonzeroLattice d) (t : Torus d) =>
4263      (Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * (Torus.realMode k t - Torus.realMode k s)) ^ 2)
4264      (u := fun k => 8 * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m))) hcont (hs.mul_left 8) hbnd
4265  have hnorm : ∀ t, ‖wvec m hm t - wvec m hm s‖ = Real.sqrt (g t) := by
4266    intro t
4267    obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := NegSobolev.coord_sq (wvec m hm t - wvec m hm s)
4268    rw [← Real.sqrt_sq (norm_nonneg _), h2]
4269    congr 1
4270    refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
4271    have e : NegSobolev.coord (wvec m hm t - wvec m hm s) k =
4272        NegSobolev.coord (wvec m hm t) k - NegSobolev.coord (wvec m hm s) k := rfl
4273    rw [e, coord_wvec, coord_wvec]
4274    ring
4275  have hg0 : g s = 0 := by simp [hg]
4276  rw [ContinuousAt, tendsto_iff_norm_sub_tendsto_zero]
4277  simp_rw [hnorm]
4278  have := (Real.continuous_sqrt.comp hgc).continuousAt (x := s)
4279  simpa [Function.comp_def, hg0] using this.tendsto

4282lemma smooth_contDiff (φ : Torus d → ℝ) (hφ : Torus.IsSmooth φ) (n : ℕ) :
4283    ContDiff ℝ n (Torus.lift φ) := by
4284  have h0 : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift φ) := hφ
4285  exact h0.of_le (by exact_mod_cast le_top)
4286
4287lemma inner_yvec_wvec (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m) (φ : Torus d → ℝ) (hφ : Torus.IsSmooth φ)
4288    (t : Torus d) :
4289    inner ℝ (yvec m φ (smooth_weighted_summable m φ hφ)) (wvec m hm t) = φ t - Torus.mean φ := by
4290  have hd := smooth_contDiff φ hφ
4291  have hc : Continuous φ := continuous_of_lift' (hd 0).continuous
4292  have hsum := Torus.hasSum_realExpansion hc (Torus.summable_fcoef_of_contDiff hd) t
4293  have := lp.inner_eq_tsum (𝕜 := ℝ) (G := fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ)
4294    (yvec m φ (smooth_weighted_summable m φ hφ) : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
4295    (wvec m hm t : lp (fun _ : NonzeroLattice d => ℝ) 2)
4296  refine this.trans (Eq.trans (tsum_congr fun k => ?_) hsum.tsum_eq)
4297  simp only [yvec, wvec, RCLike.inner_apply, conj_trivial]
4298  have e : Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-m) * Lattice.jp k.1 ^ m = 1 := by
4299    rw [← Real.rpow_add (Torus.jp_pos k.1)]; simp
4300  linear_combination (Torus.funRealCoeff φ k * Torus.realMode k t) * e
4301
4302lemma integrable_cont (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {f : Torus d → ℝ}
4303    (hf : Continuous f) : Integrable f μ :=
4304  hf.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
4305
4306lemma iid_cov_formula (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {φ ψ : Torus d → ℝ}
4307    (hφ : Continuous φ) (hψ : Continuous ψ) :
4308    ∫ t, (φ t - pair μ φ) * (ψ t - pair μ ψ) ∂μ = iidCov μ φ ψ := by
4309  have i1 : Integrable (fun t => φ t * ψ t) μ := integrable_cont μ (hφ.mul hψ)
4310  have i2 := integrable_cont μ hφ
4311  have i3 := integrable_cont μ hψ
4312  have e : ∀ t, (φ t - pair μ φ) * (ψ t - pair μ ψ) =
4313      φ t * ψ t - pair μ ψ * φ t - pair μ φ * ψ t + pair μ φ * pair μ ψ := fun t => by ring
4314  simp_rw [e]
4315  have j1 : Integrable (fun t => φ t * ψ t - pair μ ψ * φ t) μ := i1.sub (i2.const_mul _)
4316  have j2 : Integrable (fun t => φ t * ψ t - pair μ ψ * φ t - pair μ φ * ψ t) μ :=
4317    j1.sub (i3.const_mul _)
4318  rw [integral_add j2 (integrable_const _), integral_sub j1 (i3.const_mul _),
4319    integral_sub i1 (i2.const_mul _), integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul]
4320  simp only [integral_const, probReal_univ, smul_eq_mul, one_mul]
4321  unfold iidCov pair
4322  ring
4323
4324end InitialDataAux

4326open InitialDataAux in
4327theorem exists_iid_gaussian_field_proof (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) / 2 < m) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
4328    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] :
4329    ∃ ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m), IsProbabilityMeasure ν ∧
4330      IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m ν (iidCov μ) := by
4331  have := negSobolev_secondCountable d m
4332  have hm' : (d : ℝ) < 2 * m := by linarith
4333  have hwc := continuous_wvec m hm'
4334  set S := ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (-(2 * m)) with hS
4335  have hmemw : MemLp (wvec m hm') 2 μ := by
4336    refine MemLp.of_bound hwc.aestronglyMeasurable (Real.sqrt (2 * S)) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t => ?_)
4337    exact Real.le_sqrt_of_sq_le (by simpa using norm_wvec_sq_le m hm' t)
4338  have hwi : Integrable (wvec m hm') μ := hmemw.integrable one_le_two
4339  set mbar : NegSobolev d m := ∫ t, wvec m hm' t ∂μ with hmbar
4340  set X : Torus d → NegSobolev d m := fun t => wvec m hm' t - mbar with hX
4341  have hXc : Continuous X := hwc.sub continuous_const
4342  have hXm : MemLp X 2 μ := hmemw.sub (memLp_const _)
4343  have hX0 : ∫ t, X t ∂μ = 0 := by
4344    simp only [hX]
4345    rw [integral_sub hwi (integrable_const _)]
4346    simp [hmbar]
4347  obtain ⟨hC, -⟩ := isTraceClassCov_covarianceBilin μ X hXm hX0
4348  obtain ⟨γ, hγp, hγg, hγc⟩ := exists_centeredGaussian_of_traceClass _ hC
4349  refine ⟨γ, hγp, hγg, ?_⟩
4350  intro φ ψ hφ hψ
4351  have hφs := smooth_weighted_summable m φ hφ
4352  have hψs := smooth_weighted_summable m ψ hψ
4353  simp_rw [eval_eq_inner m φ hφs, eval_eq_inner m ψ hψs]
4354  set a := yvec m φ hφs
4355  set b := yvec m ψ hψs
4356  have : IsGaussian γ := hγg.1
4357  have hmemγ : MemLp id 2 γ := IsGaussian.memLp_two_id
4358  have hγ0 : γ[id] = 0 := gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hγg
4359  have h1 : ∫ F, inner ℝ a F * inner ℝ b F ∂γ = covarianceBilin γ a b := by
4360    rw [covarianceBilin_apply hmemγ]
4361    simp only [hγ0, sub_zero]
4362  rw [h1, hγc a b]
4363  -- covariance of the law of X
4364  have hXae : AEMeasurable X μ := hXc.aemeasurable
4365  have hmemlaw : MemLp id 2 (law μ X) :=
4366    (memLp_map_measure_iff aestronglyMeasurable_id hXae).mpr hXm
4367  rw [covarianceBilin_apply hmemlaw]
4368  have hlaw0 : (law μ X)[id] = 0 := by
4369    show ∫ z, id z ∂(μ.map X) = 0
4370    rw [integral_map hXae aestronglyMeasurable_id]
4371    exact hX0
4372  rw [hlaw0]
4373  simp only [sub_zero]
4374  show ∫ z, inner ℝ a z * inner ℝ b z ∂(μ.map X) = _
4375  rw [integral_map hXae (by fun_prop)]
4376  -- pointwise values
4377  have hmean_inner : ∀ (f : Torus d → ℝ) (hf : Torus.IsSmooth f)
4378      (hh : Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff f k ^ 2),
4379      inner ℝ (yvec m f hh) mbar = pair μ f - Torus.mean f := by
4380    intro f hf hh
4381    rw [hmbar, ← integral_inner hwi]
4382    simp_rw [inner_yvec_wvec m hm' f hf]
4383    have hfc : Continuous f := continuous_of_lift' ((smooth_contDiff f hf 0).continuous)
4384    rw [integral_sub (integrable_cont μ hfc) (integrable_const _)]
4385    simp [pair]
4386  have hpt : ∀ (f : Torus d → ℝ) (hf : Torus.IsSmooth f)
4387      (hh : Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d => Lattice.jp k.1 ^ (2 * m) * Torus.funRealCoeff f k ^ 2) t,
4388      inner ℝ (yvec m f hh) (X t) = f t - pair μ f := by
4389    intro f hf hh t
4390    simp only [hX, inner_sub_right]
4391    rw [inner_yvec_wvec m hm' f hf t, hmean_inner f hf hh]
4392    ring
4393  have hφc : Continuous φ := continuous_of_lift' ((smooth_contDiff φ hφ 0).continuous)
4394  have hψc : Continuous ψ := continuous_of_lift' ((smooth_contDiff ψ hψ 0).continuous)
4395  have := iid_cov_formula μ hφc hψc
4396  rw [← this]
4397  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t => ?_)
4398  show inner ℝ (yvec m φ hφs) (X t) * inner ℝ (yvec m ψ hψs) (X t) = _
4399  rw [hpt φ hφ hφs t, hpt ψ hψ hψs t]
4400
4401open InitialDataAux in
4402/-- Example A.17 `M:B:ex:iid` (source lines 10469-10472): for `m > d/2` and every probability
4403measure `μ°` there is a centered Gaussian law on `E_m = H^{-m}_0` with the bridge covariance
4404`μ°(ψφ) - μ°(ψ)μ°(φ)`. -/
4405theorem exists_iid_gaussian_field (m : ℝ) (hm : (d : ℝ) / 2 < m) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
4406    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] :
4407    ∃ ν : Measure (NegSobolev d m), IsProbabilityMeasure ν ∧
4408      IsCenteredGaussianWithCov m ν (iidCov μ) := by
4409  exact exists_iid_gaussian_field_proof m hm μ
4410
4411/-! ### Example A.39: energy of the perturbed lattice (route of the proof)
4412
4413The statement is provable as stated for every `d ≥ 1` (`N = n^d ≥ 4`, `s < d`, so
4414`g = periodicRiesz d s` is integrable with mean zero, `∫ g(x - y) dy = 0`, hence
4415`F_N(Y,1) = (2N²)⁻¹ ∑_{i≠j} g(Y_i - Y_j)`; all terms have genuine values, no junk integral is
4416used).  For `d = 1` the kernel inputs (smoothness off the origin, the gradient identity, the local
4417singularity, the mean-zero property) are those of `HRS/Kernel/D1.lean`; the lower bound for
4418`g_T(0)` in `s = 0` is uniform in `d ≥ 1`.  Let `Z = Λ_N`, `Y_i = Z_i + κ⁻¹ a(Z_i)`.
4419
44201. *Perturbation* (`perturb_pair_sum`).  With `z = Z_i - Z_j`, `δ = κ⁻¹(a(Z_i) - a(Z_j))`,
4421   `|δ| ≤ κ⁻¹ L |z|_T ≤ |z|_T/2` for `κ ≥ 2L`, the second-order Taylor expansion of `g`
4422   along the segment `[z, z+δ]` (which avoids the lattice) gives
4423   `g(z+δ) = g(z) + ∇g(z)·δ + R`, `|R| ≤ C |δ|² (|z|^{-s-2} + 1) ≤ C' κ⁻² (|z|^{-s} + 1)`
4424   (`taylor_gt`: near `0` use `g = |x|^{-s} + r` with `r` smooth, far from the lattice use a
4425   compactness bound on `D²g`).  The first-order terms cancel *exactly* by the lattice symmetry
4426   `z_j ↦ 2 z_i - z_j` and the oddness of `∇g` (`first_order_zero`).  Finally
4427   `|z|^{-s} ≤ g(z) + K`, so `∑_{i≠j}|R_ij| ≤ C κ⁻² N²`.
44282. *Perfect lattice* (`lattice_pair_sum`), `ε = n⁻²`, `g = g_ε + f_ε` (truncation of
4429   `HRS/Energy/Coercivity.lean`): `∑_{i≠j} g(Z_ij) = N² ∑_{k ∈ nℤ^d} ĝ_ε(k) - N g_ε(0) +
4430   ∑_{i≠j} f_ε(Z_ij)`.  The first term is `≤ C N n^s` because `ĝ_ε(nm) ≲ n^{s-d} e^{-|m|²/2}`;
4431   the third is `≤ C N n^s` by the Gaussian bound on the periodic heat kernel and an injective
4432   count of the lattice points (`row_pointwise`, `nearF_row_le`); `g_ε(0) ≥ 0`, and for `s = 0`
4433   `g_ε(0) ≥ log n - C`, which cancels the logarithmic term of `o_N`.
44343. Assembly (`perturbedLattice_energy_aux`): `n^s / N = N^{s/d-1}` and
4435   `log N / (2 d N) = log n / (2 N)`.
4436-/
4437
4438namespace LatticeEnergyAux
4439
4440open Set
4441
4442local notation "∞'" => ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞)

4444/-- one-dimensional Taylor remainder bound from a second-derivative bound -/
4445theorem taylor1 {φ ψ χ : ℝ → ℝ} {M : ℝ}
4446    (hφ : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, HasDerivAt φ (ψ t) t)
4447    (hψ : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, HasDerivAt ψ (χ t) t)
4448    (hχ : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, |χ t| ≤ M) :
4449    |φ 1 - φ 0 - ψ 0| ≤ M := by
4450  have h0 : (0:ℝ) ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1 := ⟨le_rfl, zero_le_one⟩
4451  have hψb : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, |ψ t - ψ 0| ≤ M := by
4452    intro t ht
4453    have := Convex.norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_hasDerivWithin_le (f := ψ) (f' := χ) (s := Icc (0:ℝ) 1)
4454      (C := M) (fun x hx => (hψ x hx).hasDerivWithinAt) (fun x hx => by
4455        simpa [Real.norm_eq_abs] using hχ x hx) (convex_Icc 0 1) h0 ht
4456    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
4457    have h2 : |t - 0| ≤ 1 := by
4458      rw [sub_zero, abs_of_nonneg ht.1]; exact ht.2
4459    have hM : 0 ≤ M := (abs_nonneg _).trans (hχ 0 h0)
4460    calc |ψ t - ψ 0| ≤ M * ‖t - 0‖ := by simpa [Real.norm_eq_abs] using this
4461      _ ≤ M * 1 := by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hM
4462      _ = M := mul_one M
4463  have hE : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, HasDerivAt (fun t => φ t - φ 0 - t * ψ 0) (ψ t - ψ 0) t := by
4464    intro t ht
4465    have h1 := (hφ t ht).sub_const (φ 0)
4466    have h2 := (hasDerivAt_id t).mul_const (ψ 0)
4467    have h3 := h1.sub h2
4468    convert h3 using 1
4469    · funext x; simp
4470    · simp
4471  have := Convex.norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_hasDerivWithin_le (f := fun t => φ t - φ 0 - t * ψ 0)
4472    (f' := fun t => ψ t - ψ 0) (s := Icc (0:ℝ) 1) (C := M)
4473    (fun x hx => (hE x hx).hasDerivWithinAt) (fun x hx => by
4474      simpa [Real.norm_eq_abs] using hψb x hx) (convex_Icc 0 1) h0 (⟨zero_le_one, le_rfl⟩ : (1:ℝ) ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1)
4475  simpa [Real.norm_eq_abs] using this
4476
4477theorem hasDerivAt_line (y δ : Euc d) (t : ℝ) :
4478    HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => y + t • δ) δ t := by
4479  simpa using ((hasDerivAt_id t).smul_const δ).const_add y
4480
4481/-- second-order Taylor bound along a segment, for a function that is `C²` near the segment -/
4482theorem taylor2 {h : Euc d → ℝ} {y δ : Euc d} {M : ℝ}
4483    (hc : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, ContDiffAt ℝ 2 h (y + t • δ))
4484    (hM : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ h) (y + t • δ)‖ ≤ M) :
4485    |h (y + δ) - h y - fderiv ℝ h y δ| ≤ M * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4486  have h0 : (0:ℝ) ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1 := ⟨le_rfl, zero_le_one⟩
4487  have key := taylor1 (φ := fun t : ℝ => h (y + t • δ))
4488    (ψ := fun t : ℝ => fderiv ℝ h (y + t • δ) δ)
4489    (χ := fun t : ℝ => fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ h) (y + t • δ) δ δ) (M := M * ‖δ‖ ^ 2)
4490    (fun t ht => ?_) (fun t ht => ?_) (fun t ht => ?_)
4491  · simpa using key
4492  · have hd : DifferentiableAt ℝ h (y + t • δ) := ((hc t ht).differentiableAt (by norm_num))
4493    exact hd.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt t (hasDerivAt_line y δ t)
4494  · have hd : DifferentiableAt ℝ (fderiv ℝ h) (y + t • δ) :=
4495      ((hc t ht).fderiv_right (m := 1) (by norm_num)).differentiableAt (by norm_num)
4496    have h1 := hd.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt t (hasDerivAt_line y δ t)
4497    have h2 := (ContinuousLinearMap.apply ℝ ℝ δ).hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt t h1
4498    exact h2
4499  · calc |fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ h) (y + t • δ) δ δ|
4500        ≤ ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ h) (y + t • δ)‖ * ‖δ‖ * ‖δ‖ := by
4501          rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact ContinuousLinearMap.le_opNorm₂ _ _ _
4502      _ ≤ M * ‖δ‖ * ‖δ‖ := by gcongr; exact hM t ht
4503      _ = M * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by ring

4505theorem fderiv_periodic {F : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace ℝ F] (f : Euc d → F)
4506    (hf : ∀ x (m : Lattice d), f (x + Lattice.toEuc m) = f x) (x : Euc d) (m : Lattice d) :
4507    fderiv ℝ f (x + Lattice.toEuc m) = fderiv ℝ f x := by
4508  rw [← fderiv_comp_add_right]
4509  congr 1
4510  funext z
4511  exact hf z m

4515    φ (Torus.mk (x + Lattice.toEuc m)) = φ (Torus.mk x) := by
4516  rw [KernelGrad.mk_add, KernelGrad.mk_toEuc, add_zero]

4518/-- every point of `ℝ^d` is within the box `[-1/2,1/2]^d` of a lattice point -/
4519theorem exists_box_rep (p : Euc d) : ∃ m : Lattice d, ∀ i, |(p - Lattice.toEuc m) i| ≤ 1 / 2 := by
4520  refine ⟨fun i => round (p i), fun i => ?_⟩
4521  simpa [Lattice.toEuc] using abs_sub_round (p i)

4523/-- a continuous function on `ℝ^d` that is lattice periodic is bounded -/
4524theorem exists_bound_periodic {F : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup F] {f : Euc d → F}
4525    (hc : Continuous f) (hf : ∀ x (m : Lattice d), f (x + Lattice.toEuc m) = f x) :
4526    ∃ M : ℝ, ∀ x, ‖f x‖ ≤ M := by
4527  have hK : IsCompact {v : Euc d | ∀ i, |v i| ≤ 1 / 2} := by
4528    refine (isCompact_closedBall (0 : Euc d) d).of_isClosed_subset ?_ ?_
4529    · have : {v : Euc d | ∀ i, |v i| ≤ 1 / 2} = ⋂ i, {v : Euc d | |v i| ≤ 1 / 2} := by
4530        ext v; simp
4531      rw [this]
4532      exact isClosed_iInter fun i => isClosed_le (by fun_prop) continuous_const
4533    · intro v hv
4534      rw [mem_closedBall_zero_iff]
4535      exact KernelGrad.norm_le_card_of_box hv
4536  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := hK.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hc.continuousOn
4537  refine ⟨M, fun x => ?_⟩
4538  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := exists_box_rep x
4539  have := hM _ hm
4540  have e : f x = f (x - Lattice.toEuc m) := by
4541    have := hf (x - Lattice.toEuc m) m
4542    rwa [sub_add_cancel] at this
4543  rwa [e]

4545/-- the periodic Riesz kernel lifted to `ℝ^d` -/
4546def gt (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : Euc d → ℝ := fun y => periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk y)

4548theorem gt_periodic (x : Euc d) (m : Lattice d) {s : ℝ} :
4549    gt d s (x + Lattice.toEuc m) = gt d s x := lift_periodic (periodicRiesz d s) x m

4551theorem mk_ne_zero_of_far {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r) {p : Euc d}
4552    (hp : ∀ m : Lattice d, r ≤ ‖p - Lattice.toEuc m‖) : Torus.mk p ≠ 0 := by
4553  intro h
4554  have h0 : Torus.mk p = Torus.mk (0 : Euc d) := by
4555    rw [h]; funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
4556  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := KernelGrad.exists_lattice_of_mk_eq h0
4557  have := hp m
4558  rw [hm] at this
4559  simp at this
4560  linarith
4561
4562theorem bounded_far {F : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup F] {f : Euc d → F}
4563    (hper : ∀ x (m : Lattice d), f (x + Lattice.toEuc m) = f x)
4564    (hc : ContinuousOn f {y : Euc d | Torus.mk y ≠ 0}) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r) :
4565    ∃ M : ℝ, ∀ p : Euc d, (∀ m : Lattice d, r ≤ ‖p - Lattice.toEuc m‖) → ‖f p‖ ≤ M := by
4566  have hK : IsCompact {v : Euc d | (∀ i, |v i| ≤ 1 / 2) ∧ r ≤ ‖v‖} := by
4567    refine (isCompact_closedBall (0 : Euc d) d).of_isClosed_subset ?_ ?_
4568    · have : {v : Euc d | (∀ i, |v i| ≤ 1 / 2) ∧ r ≤ ‖v‖} =
4569          (⋂ i, {v : Euc d | |v i| ≤ 1 / 2}) ∩ {v : Euc d | r ≤ ‖v‖} := by
4570        ext v; simp
4571      rw [this]
4572      exact (isClosed_iInter fun i => isClosed_le (by fun_prop) continuous_const).inter
4573        (isClosed_le continuous_const continuous_norm)
4574    · intro v hv
4575      rw [mem_closedBall_zero_iff]
4576      exact KernelGrad.norm_le_card_of_box hv.1
4577  have hKU : {v : Euc d | (∀ i, |v i| ≤ 1 / 2) ∧ r ≤ ‖v‖} ⊆ {y : Euc d | Torus.mk y ≠ 0} := by
4578    intro v hv
4579    refine mk_ne_zero_of_far hr (fun m => ?_)
4580    exact hv.2.trans (KernelGrad.norm_sub_toEuc_ge_of_box hv.1 m)
4581  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := hK.exists_bound_of_continuousOn (hc.mono hKU)
4582  refine ⟨M, fun p hp => ?_⟩
4583  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := exists_box_rep p
4584  have hv : p - Lattice.toEuc m ∈ {v : Euc d | (∀ i, |v i| ≤ 1 / 2) ∧ r ≤ ‖v‖} := ⟨hm, hp m⟩
4585  have := hper (p - Lattice.toEuc m) m
4586  rw [sub_add_cancel] at this
4587  rw [this]
4588  exact hM _ hv
