4590/-- `d ≥ 1` version of `KernelGrad.contDiffAt_periodicRiesz` (which is stated for `d ≥ 2`): the
4591lift of the periodic Riesz kernel is smooth away from the lattice, in every dimension `d ≥ 1`. -/
4592theorem contDiffAt_periodicRiesz_of_one_le {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
4593    {y0 : Euc d} (hy0 : Torus.mk y0 ≠ 0) :
4594    ContDiffAt ℝ ∞' (fun y : Euc d => periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk y)) y0 :=
4595  KernelD1Aux.contDiffAt_lift_g hd hs0 hsd y0 (KernelGrad.wsq_pos_of_mk_ne_zero hy0)
4596
4597/-- `d ≥ 1` version of `KernelGrad.contDiffOn_periodicRiesz`. -/
4598theorem contDiffOn_periodicRiesz_of_one_le {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
4599    ContDiffOn ℝ ∞' (fun y : Euc d => periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk y))
4600      {y : Euc d | Torus.mk y ≠ 0} :=
4601  fun _ hy => (contDiffAt_periodicRiesz_of_one_le hd hs0 hsd hy).contDiffWithinAt
4602
4603theorem hess_far {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r) :
4604    ∃ M : ℝ, ∀ p : Euc d, (∀ m : Lattice d, r ≤ ‖p - Lattice.toEuc m‖) →
4605      ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (gt d s)) p‖ ≤ M := by
4606  have hU := KernelGrad.isOpen_mk_ne_zero (d := d)
4607  have h0 : ContDiffOn ℝ 3 (gt d s) {y : Euc d | Torus.mk y ≠ 0} :=
4608    (contDiffOn_periodicRiesz_of_one_le hd hs0 hsd (s := s)).of_le (by simp)
4609  have h1 := h0.fderiv_of_isOpen hU (m := 2) (by norm_num)
4610  have h2 := h1.fderiv_of_isOpen hU (m := 1) (by norm_num)
4611  have hper1 : ∀ x (m : Lattice d), fderiv ℝ (gt d s) (x + Lattice.toEuc m) = fderiv ℝ (gt d s) x :=
4612    fderiv_periodic (gt d s) (fun x m => gt_periodic x m)
4613  exact bounded_far (fun x m => fderiv_periodic (fderiv ℝ (gt d s)) hper1 x m) h2.continuousOn hr
4614
4615theorem gt_bounded_far {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r) :
4616    ∃ M : ℝ, ∀ p : Euc d, (∀ m : Lattice d, r ≤ ‖p - Lattice.toEuc m‖) → ‖gt d s p‖ ≤ M :=
4617  bounded_far (fun x m => gt_periodic x m)
4618    ((contDiffOn_periodicRiesz_of_one_le hd hs0 hsd (s := s)).continuousOn) hr
4619
4620theorem hasDerivAt_normline (y δ : Euc d) (t : ℝ) (hp : y + t • δ ≠ 0) :
4621    HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => ‖y + t • δ‖) (inner ℝ (y + t • δ) δ / ‖y + t • δ‖) t := by
4622  have h1 := (hasDerivAt_line y δ t).norm_sq
4623  have hne : ‖y + t • δ‖ ^ 2 ≠ 0 := by positivity
4624  have h2 := h1.sqrt hne
4625  have hq : 0 < ‖y + t • δ‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hp
4626  have e : (fun t : ℝ => Real.sqrt (‖y + t • δ‖ ^ 2)) = fun t : ℝ => ‖y + t • δ‖ := by
4627    funext t; exact Real.sqrt_sq (norm_nonneg _)
4628  rw [e, Real.sqrt_sq hq.le] at h2
4629  convert h2 using 1
4630  field_simp
4631
4632/-- the radial singular profile: `t^{-s}` for `s > 0`, `-log t` for `s = 0` -/
4633def sig (s t : ℝ) : ℝ := if s = 0 then -Real.log t else t ^ (-s)
4634
4635/-- normalising constant for the derivative of the radial profile -/
4636def sig0 (s : ℝ) : ℝ := if s = 0 then 1 else s
4637
4638theorem sig0_nonneg {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) : 0 ≤ sig0 s := by
4639  unfold sig0; split_ifs <;> linarith
4640
4641theorem hasDerivAt_sig {s : ℝ} {q : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) :
4642    HasDerivAt (sig s) (-(sig0 s) * q ^ (-(s + 1))) q := by
4643  unfold sig sig0
4644  by_cases hs : s = 0
4645  · subst hs
4646    simp only [ite_true]
4647    have := (Real.hasDerivAt_log hq.ne').neg
4648    convert this using 1
4649    simp [Real.rpow_neg_one]
4650  · simp only [hs, ite_false]
4651    have := Real.hasDerivAt_rpow_const (x := q) (p := -s) (Or.inl hq.ne')
4652    convert this using 1
4653    ring_nf
4654
4655theorem hasDerivAt_sigLine {s : ℝ} (y δ : Euc d) (t : ℝ) (hp : y + t • δ ≠ 0) :
4656    HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => sig s ‖y + t • δ‖)
4657      (-(sig0 s) * ‖y + t • δ‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * inner ℝ (y + t • δ) δ) t := by
4658  have hq : 0 < ‖y + t • δ‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hp
4659  have h := (hasDerivAt_sig (s := s) hq).comp t (hasDerivAt_normline y δ t hp)
4660  refine h.congr_deriv ?_
4661  have : ‖y + t • δ‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) = ‖y + t • δ‖ ^ (-(s + 1)) / ‖y + t • δ‖ := by
4662    rw [show -(s + 2) = -(s + 1) - 1 by ring, Real.rpow_sub hq, Real.rpow_one]
4663  rw [this]
4664  ring
4665
4666/-- the second derivative of the radial profile along a line -/
4667def sigChi (s : ℝ) (p δ : Euc d) : ℝ :=
4668  (-(sig0 s) * (inner ℝ p δ / ‖p‖ * (-(s + 2)) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2) - 1))) * inner ℝ p δ +
4669    (-(sig0 s) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2))) * inner ℝ δ δ
4670
4671theorem hasDerivAt_sigLine2 {s : ℝ} (y δ : Euc d) (t : ℝ) (hp : y + t • δ ≠ 0) :
4672    HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => -(sig0 s) * ‖y + t • δ‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * inner ℝ (y + t • δ) δ)
4673      (sigChi s (y + t • δ) δ) t := by
4674  have hq : 0 < ‖y + t • δ‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hp
4675  have h1 := (hasDerivAt_normline y δ t hp).rpow_const (p := -(s + 2)) (Or.inl hq.ne')
4676  have h2 := (hasDerivAt_line y δ t).inner ℝ (hasDerivAt_const t δ)
4677  have h3 := (h1.const_mul (-(sig0 s))).mul h2
4678  refine h3.congr_deriv ?_
4679  unfold sigChi
4680  simp
4681
4682theorem abs_sigChi_le {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) {p : Euc d} (δ : Euc d) {ρ : ℝ} (hρ : 0 < ρ)
4683    (hpρ : ρ ≤ ‖p‖) :
4684    |sigChi s p δ| ≤ sig0 s * (s + 3) * ρ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4685  have hq : 0 < ‖p‖ := lt_of_lt_of_le hρ hpρ
4686  have hσ := sig0_nonneg hs0
4687  have hc : |inner ℝ p δ| ≤ ‖p‖ * ‖δ‖ := abs_real_inner_le_norm p δ
4688  have hc2 : (inner ℝ p δ) ^ 2 ≤ ‖p‖ ^ 2 * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4689    calc (inner ℝ p δ) ^ 2 = |inner ℝ p δ| ^ 2 := (sq_abs _).symm
4690      _ ≤ (‖p‖ * ‖δ‖) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) hc 2
4691      _ = _ := by ring
4692  have hqp : ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) ≤ ρ ^ (-(s + 2)) :=
4693    Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hρ hpρ (by linarith)
4694  have hqp0 : 0 < ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hq _
4695  have hq1 : ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2) - 1) = ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) / ‖p‖ := by
4696    rw [Real.rpow_sub hq, Real.rpow_one]
4697  have hid : inner ℝ δ δ = ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq δ
4698  -- first term
4699  have hT1 : |(-(sig0 s) * (inner ℝ p δ / ‖p‖ * (-(s + 2)) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2) - 1))) * inner ℝ p δ| ≤
4700      sig0 s * (s + 2) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4701    have e : (-(sig0 s) * (inner ℝ p δ / ‖p‖ * (-(s + 2)) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2) - 1))) * inner ℝ p δ =
4702        sig0 s * (s + 2) * (‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) / ‖p‖ ^ 2) * (inner ℝ p δ) ^ 2 := by
4703      rw [hq1]; field_simp
4704    rw [e, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
4705    have : ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) / ‖p‖ ^ 2 * (inner ℝ p δ) ^ 2 ≤ ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4706      rw [div_mul_eq_mul_div, div_le_iff₀ (by positivity)]
4707      calc ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * (inner ℝ p δ) ^ 2 ≤ ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * (‖p‖ ^ 2 * ‖δ‖ ^ 2) :=
4708            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hc2 hqp0.le
4709        _ = _ := by ring
4710    calc sig0 s * (s + 2) * (‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) / ‖p‖ ^ 2) * (inner ℝ p δ) ^ 2
4711        = sig0 s * (s + 2) * (‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) / ‖p‖ ^ 2 * (inner ℝ p δ) ^ 2) := by ring
4712      _ ≤ sig0 s * (s + 2) * (‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2) :=
4713          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this (by positivity)
4714      _ = _ := by ring
4715  have hT2 : |(-(sig0 s) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2))) * inner ℝ δ δ| = sig0 s * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4716    rw [hid, abs_of_nonpos (by nlinarith [mul_nonneg hσ hqp0.le, sq_nonneg ‖δ‖])]
4717    ring
4718  unfold sigChi
4719  calc _ ≤ |(-(sig0 s) * (inner ℝ p δ / ‖p‖ * (-(s + 2)) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2) - 1))) * inner ℝ p δ| +
4720        |(-(sig0 s) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2))) * inner ℝ δ δ| := abs_add_le _ _
4721    _ ≤ sig0 s * (s + 2) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 + sig0 s * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4722        rw [hT2]; linarith
4723    _ = sig0 s * (s + 3) * ‖p‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by ring
4724    _ ≤ _ := by
4725        have : 0 ≤ sig0 s * (s + 3) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by positivity
4726        nlinarith
4727
4728theorem hasDerivAt_c2line {h : Euc d → ℝ} {y δ : Euc d} {t : ℝ}
4729    (hc : ContDiffAt ℝ 2 h (y + t • δ)) :
4730    HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => h (y + t • δ)) (fderiv ℝ h (y + t • δ) δ) t ∧
4731    HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => fderiv ℝ h (y + t • δ) δ) (fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ h) (y + t • δ) δ δ) t := by
4732  constructor
4733  · have hd : DifferentiableAt ℝ h (y + t • δ) := hc.differentiableAt (by norm_num)
4734    exact hd.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt t (hasDerivAt_line y δ t)
4735  · have hd : DifferentiableAt ℝ (fderiv ℝ h) (y + t • δ) :=
4736      (hc.fderiv_right (m := 1) (by norm_num)).differentiableAt (by norm_num)
4737    have h1 := hd.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt t (hasDerivAt_line y δ t)
4738    have h2 := (ContinuousLinearMap.apply ℝ ℝ δ).hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt t h1
4739    exact h2

4741theorem mk_ne_zero_of_box {y : Euc d} (hy : ∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1 / 2) (hy0 : y ≠ 0) : Torus.mk y ≠ 0 := by
4742  have hr : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hy0
4743  exact mk_ne_zero_of_far hr (fun m => KernelGrad.norm_sub_toEuc_ge_of_box hy m)

4745theorem local_sing {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
4746    ∃ (r : Torus d → ℝ) (δ₀ : ℝ), 0 < δ₀ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧
4747      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ₀ → x ≠ 0 → gt d s x = sig s ‖x‖ + Torus.lift r x := by
4748  rcases Nat.eq_or_lt_of_le hd with hd1 | hd2
4749  · -- `d = 1`: the local singularity comes from `HRS/Kernel/D1.lean`
4750    subst hd1
4751    have hs1 : s < 1 := by simpa using hsd
4752    by_cases hs : s = 0
4753    · subst hs
4754      obtain ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, h⟩ := periodicLog_local_singularity_d1
4755      refine ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, fun x hx hx0 => ?_⟩
4756      simp only [gt, sig, ite_true, Torus.lift, Function.comp]
4757      exact h x hx hx0
4758    · have hspos : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm hs)
4759      obtain ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, h⟩ := periodicRiesz_local_singularity_d1 hspos hs1
4760      refine ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, fun x hx hx0 => ?_⟩
4761      simp only [gt, sig, hs, ite_false, Torus.lift, Function.comp]
4762      exact h x hx hx0
4763  have hd : 2 ≤ d := hd2
4764  by_cases hs : s = 0
4765  · subst hs
4766    obtain ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, h⟩ := periodicLog_local_singularity (d := d) hd
4767    refine ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, fun x hx hx0 => ?_⟩
4768    simp only [gt, sig, ite_true, Torus.lift, Function.comp]
4769    exact h x hx hx0
4770  · have hspos : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm hs)
4771    obtain ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, h⟩ := periodicRiesz_local_singularity (d := d) hd hspos hsd
4772    refine ⟨r, δ₀, h0, hr, fun x hx hx0 => ?_⟩
4773    simp only [gt, sig, hs, ite_false, Torus.lift, Function.comp]
4774    exact h x hx hx0
4775
4776theorem hess_smooth_bound {r : Torus d → ℝ} (hr : Torus.IsSmooth r) :
4777    ∃ M : ℝ, ∀ p : Euc d, ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r)) p‖ ≤ M := by
4778  have hr' : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (Torus.lift r) := hr
4779  have h3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 (Torus.lift r) := hr'.of_le (by simp)
4780  have h1 := h3.fderiv_right (m := 2) (by norm_num)
4781  have h2 := h1.fderiv_right (m := 1) (by norm_num)
4782  have hper1 : ∀ x (m : Lattice d), fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r) (x + Lattice.toEuc m) =
4783      fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r) x :=
4784    fderiv_periodic (Torus.lift r) (fun x m => lift_periodic r x m)
4785  refine exists_bound_periodic h2.continuous ?_
4786  intro x m
4787  exact fderiv_periodic (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r)) hper1 x m
4788
4789theorem norm_line_bounds {y δ : Euc d} (hδ : ‖δ‖ ≤ ‖y‖ / 2) {t : ℝ} (ht : t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1) :
4790    ‖y‖ / 2 ≤ ‖y + t • δ‖ ∧ ‖y + t • δ‖ ≤ 3 * ‖y‖ / 2 := by
4791  have h1 : ‖t • δ‖ ≤ ‖δ‖ := by
4792    rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg ht.1]
4793    exact mul_le_of_le_one_left (norm_nonneg _) ht.2
4794  constructor
4795  · have : ‖y‖ ≤ ‖y + t • δ‖ + ‖t • δ‖ := by
4796      have := norm_sub_le (y + t • δ) (t • δ)
4797      rwa [add_sub_cancel_right] at this
4798    linarith
4799  · have := norm_add_le y (t • δ)
4800    linarith
4801
4802theorem far_cond {y δ : Euc d} (hy : ∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1 / 2) (hδ : ‖δ‖ ≤ ‖y‖ / 2) {t : ℝ}
4803    (ht : t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1) (m : Lattice d) : ‖y‖ / 2 ≤ ‖y + t • δ - Lattice.toEuc m‖ := by
4804  have h1 : ‖t • δ‖ ≤ ‖δ‖ := by
4805    rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg ht.1]
4806    exact mul_le_of_le_one_left (norm_nonneg _) ht.2
4807  have h2 := KernelGrad.norm_sub_toEuc_ge_of_box hy m
4808  have h3 : ‖y - Lattice.toEuc m‖ ≤ ‖y + t • δ - Lattice.toEuc m‖ + ‖t • δ‖ := by
4809    have := norm_sub_le (y + t • δ - Lattice.toEuc m) (t • δ)
4810    rwa [show y + t • δ - Lattice.toEuc m - t • δ = y - Lattice.toEuc m by abel] at this
4811  linarith

4813theorem taylor_gt {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
4814    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ y δ : Euc d, (∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1 / 2) → y ≠ 0 → ‖δ‖ ≤ ‖y‖ / 2 →
4815      |gt d s (y + δ) - gt d s y - fderiv ℝ (gt d s) y δ| ≤
4816        C * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 * (‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1) := by
4817  obtain ⟨r, δ₀, hδ₀, hr, hrep⟩ := local_sing (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
4818  obtain ⟨Mr, hMr⟩ := hess_smooth_bound hr
4819  obtain ⟨Mf, hMf⟩ := hess_far hd hs0 hsd (r := δ₀ / 4) (by positivity)
4820  have hσ := sig0_nonneg hs0
4821  refine ⟨sig0 s * (s + 3) * 2 ^ (s + 2) + |Mr| + |Mf|, by positivity, fun y δ hy hy0 hδ => ?_⟩
4822  have hyn : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hy0
4823  have hyp : 0 ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) := Real.rpow_nonneg hyn.le _
4824  have hmk := mk_ne_zero_of_box hy hy0
4825  have hgy2 : ContDiffAt ℝ 2 (gt d s) y :=
4826    (contDiffAt_periodicRiesz_of_one_le hd hs0 hsd (y0 := y) hmk).of_le (by simp)
4827  have hgy : DifferentiableAt ℝ (gt d s) y := hgy2.differentiableAt (by norm_num)
4828  by_cases hnear : ‖y‖ < δ₀ / 2
4829  · -- near the singularity
4830    set W : Set ℝ := {t : ℝ | 0 < ‖y + t • δ‖ ∧ ‖y + t • δ‖ < δ₀} with hW
4831    have hWo : IsOpen W := by
4832      have hc : Continuous fun t : ℝ => ‖y + t • δ‖ := by fun_prop
4833      exact (isOpen_lt continuous_const hc).inter (isOpen_lt hc continuous_const)
4834    have hWI : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, t ∈ W := by
4835      intro t ht
4836      obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := norm_line_bounds hδ ht
4837      exact ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩
4838    have hline : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, y + t • δ ≠ 0 := fun t ht h => by
4839      have := (hWI t ht).1; rw [h] at this; simp at this
4840    have hφ : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => gt d s (y + t • δ))
4841        (-(sig0 s) * ‖y + t • δ‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * inner ℝ (y + t • δ) δ +
4842          fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r) (y + t • δ) δ) t := by
4843      intro t ht
4844      have hrc : ContDiffAt ℝ 2 (Torus.lift r) (y + t • δ) := by
4845        have hr' : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (Torus.lift r) := hr
4846        exact (hr'.of_le (by simp)).contDiffAt
4847      have h1 := hasDerivAt_sigLine (s := s) y δ t (hline t ht)
4848      have h2 := (hasDerivAt_c2line (y := y) (δ := δ) hrc).1
4849      refine (h1.add h2).congr_of_eventuallyEq ?_
4850      filter_upwards [hWo.mem_nhds (hWI t ht)] with u hu
4851      have := hrep (y + u • δ) hu.2 (norm_pos_iff.mp hu.1)
4852      simpa using this
4853    have hψ : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, HasDerivAt
4854        (fun t : ℝ => -(sig0 s) * ‖y + t • δ‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) * inner ℝ (y + t • δ) δ +
4855          fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r) (y + t • δ) δ)
4856        (sigChi s (y + t • δ) δ + fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r)) (y + t • δ) δ δ) t := by
4857      intro t ht
4858      have hrc : ContDiffAt ℝ 2 (Torus.lift r) (y + t • δ) := by
4859        have hr' : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (Torus.lift r) := hr
4860        exact (hr'.of_le (by simp)).contDiffAt
4861      exact (hasDerivAt_sigLine2 (s := s) y δ t (hline t ht)).add
4862        (hasDerivAt_c2line (y := y) (δ := δ) hrc).2
4863    have hχ : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1,
4864        |sigChi s (y + t • δ) δ + fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r)) (y + t • δ) δ δ| ≤
4865          (sig0 s * (s + 3) * (‖y‖ / 2) ^ (-(s + 2)) + Mr) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4866      intro t ht
4867      obtain ⟨h1, -⟩ := norm_line_bounds hδ ht
4868      have hA := abs_sigChi_le (s := s) hs0 δ (by positivity : 0 < ‖y‖ / 2) h1
4869      have hB : |fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r)) (y + t • δ) δ δ| ≤ Mr * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by
4870        calc _ ≤ ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift r)) (y + t • δ)‖ * ‖δ‖ * ‖δ‖ := by
4871              rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact ContinuousLinearMap.le_opNorm₂ _ _ _
4872          _ ≤ Mr * ‖δ‖ * ‖δ‖ := by gcongr; exact hMr _
4873          _ = _ := by ring
4874      calc _ ≤ _ := abs_add_le _ _
4875        _ ≤ _ := add_le_add hA hB
4876        _ = _ := by ring
4877    have key := taylor1 hφ hψ hχ
4878    simp only [one_smul, zero_smul, add_zero] at key
4879    -- identify the first-order term
4880    have hder : HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => gt d s (y + t • δ)) (fderiv ℝ (gt d s) y δ) 0 := by
4881      have hg0 : DifferentiableAt ℝ (gt d s) (y + (0:ℝ) • δ) := by simpa using hgy
4882      have := hg0.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt (0:ℝ) (hasDerivAt_line y δ 0)
4883      simp only [zero_smul, add_zero] at this
4884      exact this
4885    have hψ0 := hφ 0 ⟨le_rfl, zero_le_one⟩
4886    simp only [zero_smul, add_zero] at hψ0
4887    have heq := hψ0.unique hder
4888    rw [heq] at key
4889    have e2 : (‖y‖ / 2) ^ (-(s + 2)) = 2 ^ (s + 2) * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) := by
4890      rw [Real.div_rpow hyn.le (by norm_num), Real.rpow_neg (by norm_num : (0:ℝ) ≤ 2),
4891        div_inv_eq_mul, mul_comm]
4892    rw [e2] at key
4893    refine key.trans ?_
4894    have hd2 : 0 ≤ ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by positivity
4895    have h2p : 0 ≤ (2:ℝ) ^ (s + 2) := by positivity
4896    have hK0 : 0 ≤ sig0 s * (s + 3) * 2 ^ (s + 2) := by positivity
4897    have hle : sig0 s * (s + 3) * (2 ^ (s + 2) * ‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2))) + Mr ≤
4898        (sig0 s * (s + 3) * 2 ^ (s + 2) + |Mr| + |Mf|) * (‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1) := by
4899      nlinarith [mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg Mr) hyp, mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg Mf) hyp,
4900        abs_nonneg Mf, neg_abs_le Mr, le_abs_self Mr]
4901    calc _ ≤ ((sig0 s * (s + 3) * 2 ^ (s + 2) + |Mr| + |Mf|) * (‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 :=
4902          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hle hd2
4903      _ = _ := by ring
4904  · -- away from the singularity
4905    have hnear' : δ₀ / 2 ≤ ‖y‖ := not_lt.mp hnear
4906    have hcd : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, ContDiffAt ℝ 2 (gt d s) (y + t • δ) := by
4907      intro t ht
4908      have hmk' : Torus.mk (y + t • δ) ≠ 0 := by
4909        refine mk_ne_zero_of_far (r := δ₀ / 4) (by positivity) (fun m => ?_)
4910        have := far_cond hy hδ ht m
4911        linarith
4912      exact (contDiffAt_periodicRiesz_of_one_le hd hs0 hsd hmk').of_le (by simp)
4913    have hM : ∀ t ∈ Icc (0:ℝ) 1, ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (gt d s)) (y + t • δ)‖ ≤ Mf := by
4914      intro t ht
4915      refine hMf _ (fun m => ?_)
4916      have := far_cond hy hδ ht m
4917      linarith
4918    have key := taylor2 hcd hM
4919    refine key.trans ?_
4920    have hd2 : 0 ≤ ‖δ‖ ^ 2 := by positivity
4921    have hK0 : 0 ≤ sig0 s * (s + 3) * 2 ^ (s + 2) := by positivity
4922    have hle : Mf ≤ (sig0 s * (s + 3) * 2 ^ (s + 2) + |Mr| + |Mf|) * (‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1) := by
4923      nlinarith [mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg Mr) hyp, mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg Mf) hyp,
4924        mul_nonneg hK0 hyp, abs_nonneg Mr, le_abs_self Mf]
4925    calc _ ≤ ((sig0 s * (s + 3) * 2 ^ (s + 2) + |Mr| + |Mf|) * (‖y‖ ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1)) * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 :=
4926          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hle hd2
4927      _ = _ := by ring

4929/-- the torus distance to the origin -/
4930def rho (z : Torus d) : ℝ := ‖KernelGrad.centeredRep z‖
4931
4932theorem rho_nonneg (z : Torus d) : 0 ≤ rho z := norm_nonneg _
4933
4934theorem rho_le (z : Torus d) : rho z ≤ d := KernelGrad.norm_le_card_of_box (KernelGrad.abs_centeredRep_le z)
4935
4936theorem centeredRep_ne_zero {z : Torus d} (hz : z ≠ 0) : KernelGrad.centeredRep z ≠ 0 := by
4937  intro h
4938  apply hz
4939  rw [← KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep z, h]
4940  funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
4941
4942theorem rho_pos {z : Torus d} (hz : z ≠ 0) : 0 < rho z :=
4943  norm_pos_iff.mpr (centeredRep_ne_zero hz)
4944
4945theorem rho_neg (z : Torus d) : rho (-z) = rho z := by
4946  have h := KernelGrad.tdist_neg z
4947  rw [KernelGrad.tdist_zero_right, KernelGrad.tdist_zero_right] at h
4948  exact h
4949
4950theorem fderiv_gt_apply {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {y : Euc d}
4951    (hy : Torus.mk y ≠ 0) (δ : Euc d) :
4952    fderiv ℝ (gt d s) y δ = inner ℝ (periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk y)) δ := by
4953  rcases Nat.eq_or_lt_of_le hd with hd1 | hd2
4954  · -- `d = 1`: reduce to the one-variable derivative `KernelD1Aux.hasDerivAt_g_ev`
4955    subst hd1
4956    have hs1 : s < 1 := by simpa using hsd
4957    have hoff := KernelD1Aux.off_int_of_mk_ne hy
4958    have hder := KernelD1Aux.hasDerivAt_g_ev hs0 hs1 hoff
4959    have hdiff : DifferentiableAt ℝ (gt 1 s) y :=
4960      (contDiffAt_periodicRiesz_of_one_le le_rfl hs0 hsd hy).differentiableAt (by simp)
4961    have hline : HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => gt 1 s (y + t • δ)) (fderiv ℝ (gt 1 s) y δ) 0 := by
4962      have hd0 : DifferentiableAt ℝ (gt 1 s) (y + (0 : ℝ) • δ) := by simpa using hdiff
4963      have := hd0.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt (0 : ℝ) (hasDerivAt_line y δ 0)
4964      simp only [zero_smul, add_zero] at this
4965      exact this
4966    have hlin : (fun t : ℝ => gt 1 s (y + t • δ)) =
4967        (fun u : ℝ => periodicRiesz 1 s (Torus.mk (KernelD1Aux.ev u))) ∘
4968          fun t : ℝ => y 0 + t * δ 0 := by
4969      funext t
4970      have e : y + t • δ = KernelD1Aux.ev (y 0 + t * δ 0) := by
4971        have := KernelD1Aux.ev_eq (y + t • δ)
4972        simpa using this
4973      simp only [Function.comp, gt]
4974      rw [e]
4975    have hcomp : HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => gt 1 s (y + t • δ))
4976        ((periodicRieszGrad 1 s (Torus.mk (KernelD1Aux.ev (y 0)))) 0 * δ 0) 0 := by
4977      rw [hlin]
4978      have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun t : ℝ => y 0 + t * δ 0) (δ 0) 0 := by
4979        simpa using ((hasDerivAt_id (0 : ℝ)).mul_const (δ 0)).const_add (y 0)
4980      have h2 : HasDerivAt (fun u : ℝ => periodicRiesz 1 s (Torus.mk (KernelD1Aux.ev u)))
4981          ((periodicRieszGrad 1 s (Torus.mk (KernelD1Aux.ev (y 0)))) 0) (y 0 + 0 * δ 0) := by
4982        simpa using hder
4983      have := h2.comp (0 : ℝ) h1
4984      simpa [mul_comm] using this
4985    have e := hline.unique hcomp
4986    rw [e, ← KernelD1Aux.ev_eq y]
4987    simp [PiLp.inner_apply, mul_comm]
4988  have hd' : 2 ≤ d := hd2
4989  have h := KernelGrad.lift_periodicRieszGrad_eq_gradient hd' hs0 hsd hy
4990  have h2 : periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk y) =
4991      (InnerProductSpace.toDual ℝ (Euc d)).symm (fderiv ℝ (gt d s) y) := h
4992  rw [h2, InnerProductSpace.toDual_symm_apply]
4993
4994theorem taylor_torus {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
4995    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ (z : Torus d) (δ : Euc d), z ≠ 0 → ‖δ‖ ≤ rho z / 2 →
4996      |periodicRiesz d s (z + Torus.mk δ) - periodicRiesz d s z -
4997          inner ℝ (periodicRieszGrad d s z) δ| ≤
4998        C * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 * (rho z ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1) := by
4999  obtain ⟨C, hC0, hC⟩ := taylor_gt (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
5000  refine ⟨C, hC0, fun z δ hz hδ => ?_⟩
5001  have hy0 := centeredRep_ne_zero hz
5002  have hbox := KernelGrad.abs_centeredRep_le z
5003  have hmk := KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep z
5004  have := hC (KernelGrad.centeredRep z) δ hbox hy0 hδ
5005  have hmk0 : Torus.mk (KernelGrad.centeredRep z) ≠ 0 := by rwa [hmk]
5006  rw [fderiv_gt_apply hd hs0 hsd hmk0, hmk] at this
5007  simp only [gt, KernelGrad.mk_add, hmk] at this
5008  exact this

5010theorem lipschitz_a {a : Torus d → Euc d} (ha : Torus.IsSmooth a) :
5011    ∃ L : ℝ, 0 ≤ L ∧ ∀ x y : Torus d, ‖a x - a y‖ ≤ L * rho (x - y) := by
5012  have ha' : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (Torus.lift a) := ha
5013  have hc : Continuous (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift a)) := ha'.continuous_fderiv (by simp)
5014  have hper : ∀ x (m : Lattice d), fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift a) (x + Lattice.toEuc m) =
5015      fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift a) x :=
5016    fderiv_periodic (Torus.lift a) (fun x m => lift_periodic a x m)
5017  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := exists_bound_periodic hc hper
5018  have hL0 : 0 ≤ L := (norm_nonneg _).trans (hL 0)
5019  refine ⟨L, hL0, fun x y => ?_⟩
5020  set p := KernelGrad.centeredRep x with hp
5021  set c := KernelGrad.centeredRep (x - y) with hcdef
5022  have hq : Torus.mk (p - c) = y := by
5023    rw [KernelGrad.mk_sub, hp, hcdef, KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep, KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep, sub_sub_cancel]
5024  have hpx : Torus.mk p = x := KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep x
5025  have key := Convex.norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_fderiv_le (f := Torus.lift a) (s := Set.univ)
5026    (C := L) (fun z _ => (ha'.differentiable (by simp)) z) (fun z _ => hL z) convex_univ
5027    (Set.mem_univ (p - c)) (Set.mem_univ p)
5028  have e1 : Torus.lift a p = a x := by simp [Torus.lift, hpx]
5029  have e2 : Torus.lift a (p - c) = a y := by simp [Torus.lift, hq]
5030  rw [e1, e2, sub_sub_cancel] at key
5031  simpa [rho, hcdef] using key
5032
5033theorem rpow_neg_le_add {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
5034    ∃ K : ℝ, 0 ≤ K ∧ ∀ z : Torus d, z ≠ 0 → rho z ^ (-s) ≤ periodicRiesz d s z + K := by
5035  obtain ⟨r, δ₀, hδ₀, hr, hrep⟩ := local_sing (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
5036  have hr' : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (Torus.lift r) := hr
5037  obtain ⟨R, hR⟩ := exists_bound_periodic hr'.continuous (fun x m => lift_periodic r x m)
5038  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := gt_bounded_far hd hs0 hsd (r := δ₀ / 2) (by positivity)
5039  have hR0 : 0 ≤ R := (norm_nonneg _).trans (hR 0)
5040  have hδ2 : 0 < δ₀ / 2 := by positivity
5041  refine ⟨R + |Real.log (δ₀ / 2)| + 1 + (δ₀ / 2) ^ (-s) + |B|, by positivity, fun z hz => ?_⟩
5042  set y := KernelGrad.centeredRep z with hy
5043  have hy0 : y ≠ 0 := centeredRep_ne_zero hz
5044  have hbox := KernelGrad.abs_centeredRep_le z
5045  have hmk : Torus.mk y = z := KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep z
5046  have hgz : periodicRiesz d s z = gt d s y := by simp [gt, hmk]
5047  have hρ : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hy0
5048  have hrho : rho z = ‖y‖ := rfl
5049  rw [hgz, hrho]
5050  have hp0 : 0 ≤ (δ₀ / 2) ^ (-s) := Real.rpow_nonneg hδ2.le _
5051  by_cases hnear : ‖y‖ < δ₀ / 2
5052  · have h1 := hrep y (by linarith) hy0
5053    have hr1 := (abs_le.mp (by simpa using hR y : |Torus.lift r y| ≤ R))
5054    by_cases hs : s = 0
5055    · subst hs
5056      simp only [sig, ite_true] at h1
5057      rw [h1]
5058      simp only [neg_zero, Real.rpow_zero]
5059      have hlog : Real.log ‖y‖ ≤ Real.log (δ₀ / 2) := Real.log_le_log hρ hnear.le
5060      have := neg_abs_le (Real.log (δ₀ / 2))
5061      have := le_abs_self (Real.log (δ₀ / 2))
5062      have := abs_nonneg B
5063      linarith [hr1.1, hr1.2]
5064    · rw [h1]
5065      simp only [sig, hs, ite_false]
5066      have := abs_nonneg B
5067      have := abs_nonneg (Real.log (δ₀ / 2))
5068      linarith [hr1.1, hr1.2]
5069  · have hfar : δ₀ / 2 ≤ ‖y‖ := not_lt.mp hnear
5070    have hb := hB y (fun m => hfar.trans (KernelGrad.norm_sub_toEuc_ge_of_box hbox m))
5071    have hb' := (abs_le.mp (by simpa using hb : |gt d s y| ≤ B))
5072    have hpow : ‖y‖ ^ (-s) ≤ (δ₀ / 2) ^ (-s) :=
5073      Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hδ2 hfar (by linarith)
5074    have := abs_nonneg (Real.log (δ₀ / 2))
5075    have := le_abs_self B
5076    linarith [hb'.1]
5077

5079/-- two lattice-type circle points coincide iff the integers agree mod `n` -/
5080theorem circle_eq_iff {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (p q : ℤ) :
5081    (((p : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = (((q : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) ↔ (n : ℤ) ∣ p - q := by
5082  have hn' : (n : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5083  rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← AddCircle.coe_sub, AddCircle.coe_eq_zero_iff]
5084  constructor
5085  · rintro ⟨m, hm⟩
5086    refine ⟨m, ?_⟩
5087    have : ((p - q : ℤ) : ℝ) = (n : ℝ) * m := by
5088      push_cast
5089      field_simp at hm ⊢
5090      simp only [zsmul_eq_mul, mul_one] at hm
5091      linarith
5092    have h : ((p - q : ℤ) : ℝ) = ((n * m : ℤ) : ℝ) := by push_cast at this ⊢; linarith
5093    exact_mod_cast h
5094  · rintro ⟨m, hm⟩
5095    refine ⟨m, ?_⟩
5096    have : ((p - q : ℤ) : ℝ) = (n : ℝ) * m := by exact_mod_cast hm
5097    simp only [zsmul_eq_mul, mul_one]
5098    push_cast at this
5099    field_simp
5100    linarith
5101
5102theorem latticePoint_apply (n : ℕ) (j : Fin d → Fin n) (c : Fin d) :
5103    latticePoint n j c = ((((((j c : ℕ) : ℤ)) : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := by
5104  simp [latticePoint]
5105
5106theorem latticePoint_injective {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) :
5107    Function.Injective (latticePoint (d := d) n) := by
5108  intro j j' h
5109  funext c
5110  have h1 := congrFun h c
5111  rw [latticePoint_apply, latticePoint_apply, circle_eq_iff hn] at h1
5112  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := h1
5113  have h2 := (j c).2
5114  have h3 := (j' c).2
5115  apply Fin.ext
5116  have h4 : ((j c : ℕ) : ℤ) - (j' c : ℕ) = n * t := ht
5117  have hn' : (0 : ℤ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5118  have h5 : (t = 0) := by
5119    by_contra hne
5120    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hne with h6 | h6
5121    · nlinarith
5122    · nlinarith
5123  rw [h5] at h4
5124  omega
5125
5126theorem latticePoint_reflect {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (i j : Fin d → Fin n) :
5127    ∃ k : Fin d → Fin n, latticePoint n k = latticePoint n i + latticePoint n i - latticePoint n j := by
5128  have hn' : (0 : ℤ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5129  have key : ∀ c : Fin d, ∃ r : Fin n, latticePoint n (fun _ => r) c =
5130      latticePoint n i c + latticePoint n i c - latticePoint n j c := by
5131    intro c
5132    set x : ℤ := 2 * ((i c : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((j c : ℕ) : ℤ) with hx
5133    have hr0 : 0 ≤ x % n := Int.emod_nonneg _ hn'.ne'
5134    have hr1 : x % n < n := Int.emod_lt_of_pos _ hn'
5135    refine ⟨⟨(x % n).toNat, by omega⟩, ?_⟩
5136    rw [latticePoint_apply]
5137    have e1 : latticePoint n i c + latticePoint n i c - latticePoint n j c =
5138        (((x : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := by
5139      rw [latticePoint_apply, latticePoint_apply]
5140      rw [← AddCircle.coe_add, ← AddCircle.coe_sub]
5141      congr 1
5142      simp only [hx]; push_cast; ring
5143    rw [e1, circle_eq_iff hn]
5144    simp only [Fin.val_mk]
5145    refine ⟨-(x / n), ?_⟩
5146    have := Int.emod_def x n
5147    have h2 : (((x % n).toNat : ℕ) : ℤ) = x % n := Int.toNat_of_nonneg hr0
5148    rw [h2]
5149    linarith
5150  choose r hr using key
5151  refine ⟨fun c => r c, ?_⟩
5152  funext c
5153  have := hr c
5154  rw [latticePoint_apply] at this ⊢
5155  simpa [latticePoint_apply] using this

5157open Complex in
5158theorem sum_exp_fin {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (m : ℤ) :
5159    ∑ a : Fin n, Complex.exp (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I * (m : ℤ) * ((((a : ℕ) : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : ℂ)) =
5160      if (n : ℤ) ∣ m then (n : ℂ) else 0 := by
5161  have hn' : (n : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5162  set ζ : ℂ := Complex.exp (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I * (m : ℤ) / n) with hζ
5163  have hterm : ∀ a : Fin n, Complex.exp (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I * (m : ℤ) * ((((a : ℕ) : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : ℂ)) =
5164      ζ ^ (a : ℕ) := by
5165    intro a
5166    rw [hζ, ← Complex.exp_nat_mul]
5167    congr 1
5168    push_cast
5169    field_simp
5170  simp_rw [hterm]
5171  rw [Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun a => ζ ^ a) n]
5172  split_ifs with hdiv
5173  · obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hdiv
5174    have : ζ = 1 := by
5175      rw [hζ, Complex.exp_eq_one_iff]
5176      refine ⟨t, ?_⟩
5177      rw [ht]; push_cast; field_simp
5178    rw [this]; simp
5179  · have hne : ζ ≠ 1 := by
5180      intro h
5181      rw [hζ, Complex.exp_eq_one_iff] at h
5182      obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := h
5183      apply hdiv
5184      refine ⟨t, ?_⟩
5185      have : (m : ℂ) = n * t := by
5186        field_simp at ht
5187        exact ht
5188      exact_mod_cast this
5189    have hpow : ζ ^ n = 1 := by
5190      rw [hζ, ← Complex.exp_nat_mul]
5191      have : (n : ℂ) * (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I * (m : ℤ) / n) = (m : ℤ) * (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I) := by
5192        field_simp
5193      rw [this, Complex.exp_int_mul_two_pi_mul_I]
5194    rw [geom_sum_eq hne, hpow]; simp
5195
5196theorem sum_mode_lattice {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (k : Lattice d) :
5197    ∑ j : Fin d → Fin n, Torus.mode k (latticePoint n j) =
5198      if (∀ c, (n : ℤ) ∣ k c) then ((n : ℂ) ^ d) else 0 := by
5199  have h1 : ∀ j : Fin d → Fin n, Torus.mode k (latticePoint n j) =
5200      ∏ c, Complex.exp (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I * (k c : ℤ) * ((((j c : ℕ) : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : ℂ)) := by
5201    intro j
5202    exact mode_eq_prod k (fun c => (((j c : ℕ) : ℝ) / n : ℝ))
5203  simp_rw [h1]
5204  have h2 := Finset.prod_univ_sum (fun _ : Fin d => (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin n)))
5205    (fun c a => Complex.exp (2 * Real.pi * Complex.I * (k c : ℤ) * ((((a : ℕ) : ℝ) / n : ℝ) : ℂ)))
5206  rw [Fintype.piFinset_univ] at h2
5207  rw [← h2]
5208  simp_rw [sum_exp_fin hn]
5209  rw [Finset.prod_ite_zero]
5210  simp

5212/-- the lattice configuration `Λ_N`, `N = n^d` -/
5213def latCfg (n : ℕ) : Config d (n ^ d) := fun j => latticePoint n (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm j)
5214
5215theorem latCfg_injective {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) : Function.Injective (latCfg (d := d) n) := by
5216  intro i j h
5217  have := latticePoint_injective hn h
5218  exact finFunctionFinEquiv.symm.injective this
5219
5220theorem latCfg_reflect {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (i j : Fin (n ^ d)) :
5221    ∃ k : Fin (n ^ d), latCfg n k = latCfg n i + latCfg n i - latCfg n j := by
5222  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := latticePoint_reflect hn (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm i) (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm j)
5223  refine ⟨finFunctionFinEquiv k, ?_⟩
5224  simpa [latCfg] using hk

5229  exact Equiv.sum_comp finFunctionFinEquiv.symm (fun j => Torus.mode k (latticePoint n j))

5232theorem gT_lattice_sum {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε)
5233    {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) :
5234    (1 / ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) =
5235      ∑' k : Lattice d, cT d s ε k * (if (∀ c, (n : ℤ) ∣ k c) then 1 else 0) := by
5236  haveI : (volume : Measure (Torus d)).IsNegInvariant :=
5237    MeasureTheory.Measure.pi.isNegInvariant (fun _ : Fin d => (volume : Measure UnitAddCircle))
5238  have hN : 0 < n ^ d := by positivity
5239  have hc : Summable fun k => |cT d s ε k| := (summable_cT hd hs0 hsd hε).abs
5240  have hQ := Q_eq_tsum hN hc (latCfg n) (volume : Measure (Torus d))
5241  have hI : ∀ x : Torus d, ∫ y, trigSum (cT d s ε) (x - y) ∂(volume : Measure (Torus d)) = 0 := by
5242    intro x
5243    have := integral_sub_left_eq_self (fun z => gT d s ε z) (volume : Measure (Torus d)) x
5244    have h0 := integral_gT hd hs0 hsd hε (s := s) (d := d)
5245    exact this.trans h0
5246  simp only [hI, integral_zero, Finset.sum_const_zero, mul_zero, sub_zero, add_zero] at hQ
5247  refine Eq.trans hQ (tsum_congr fun k => ?_)
5248  by_cases hk : k = 0
5249  · simp [cT, hk]
5250  · have hint : ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂(volume : Measure (Torus d)) = 0 := by
5251      rw [Torus.integral_mode]; simp [hk]
5252    rw [hint, sub_zero, sum_mode_latCfg hn k]
5253    split_ifs with h
5254    · have : ((((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ)) • ((n : ℂ) ^ d) = 1 := by
5255        rw [Complex.real_smul]
5256        have : (((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) : ℂ) = (n : ℂ) ^ d := by push_cast; rfl
5257        have hn0 : ((n : ℂ) ^ d) ≠ 0 := by
5258          have : (n : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5259          exact pow_ne_zero _ this
5260        push_cast
5261        field_simp
5262      rw [this]; simp
5263    · simp

5275theorem cT_scale {s : ℝ} {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (m : Lattice d) (hm : m ≠ 0) :
5276    cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • m) =
5277      subA d s * (((n : ℝ) ^ 2) ^ (-(((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) *
5278        ∫ v in Ioi (1 : ℝ), v ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 * v)) := by
5279  set a : ℝ := ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha
5280  set b : ℝ := (n : ℝ) ^ 2 with hb
5281  have hb0 : 0 < b := by positivity
5282  have hnm : ((n : ℤ) • m) ≠ 0 := by
5283    intro h
5284    apply hm
5285    funext c
5286    have := congrFun h c
5287    simp only [Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, Pi.zero_apply] at this
5288    have hn' : (n : ℤ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5289    exact (mul_eq_zero.mp this).resolve_left hn'
5290  unfold cT
5291  rw [if_neg hnm, enorm_nsmul, mul_assoc]
5292  congr 1
5293  set g : ℝ → ℝ := fun v => v ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 * v)) with hg
5294  have hF : ∀ u ∈ Ioi (1 / b), u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(((n : ℝ) * Lattice.enorm m) ^ 2 * u)) =
5295      b ^ (-(a - 1)) * g (b * u) := by
5296    intro u hu
5297    have hu0 : 0 < u := lt_trans (by positivity) hu
5298    simp only [hg]
5299    rw [Real.mul_rpow hb0.le hu0.le, ← mul_assoc, ← mul_assoc, ← Real.rpow_add hb0]
5300    · have : b ^ (-(a - 1) + (a - 1)) = 1 := by simp
5301      rw [this, one_mul]
5302      congr 2
5303      rw [mul_pow, hb]; ring
5304  rw [setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioi hF, integral_const_mul,
5305    integral_comp_mul_left_Ioi g (1 / b) hb0, mul_one_div_cancel hb0.ne']
5306  rw [smul_eq_mul, ← mul_assoc]
5307  have : b ^ (-(a - 1)) * b⁻¹ = b ^ (-a) := by
5308    rw [← Real.rpow_neg_one, ← Real.rpow_add hb0]; congr 1; ring
5309  rw [this]

5312theorem cT_lattice_le {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n)
5313    (m : Lattice d) (hm : m ≠ 0) :
5314    cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • m) ≤
5315      subA d s * halfInt (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) * (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d) * Real.exp (-(1 / 2) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) := by
5316  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by linarith
5317  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd
5318  rw [cT_scale hn m hm]
5319  have h1 := intT_le ha (ε := 1) one_pos m hm
5320  have hb : (((n : ℝ) ^ 2) ^ (-(((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) = (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d) := by
5321    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul (by positivity)]
5322    congr 1; push_cast; ring
5323  rw [hb]
5324  have hn0 : 0 ≤ (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d) := by positivity
5325  have h2 : -(1 / 2 : ℝ) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 = -((1:ℝ) / 2) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 := by ring
5326  calc subA d s * (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d) * ∫ v in Ioi (1 : ℝ), v ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) *
5327        Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 * v))
5328      ≤ subA d s * (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d) * (Real.exp (-((1:ℝ) / 2) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) *
5329          halfInt (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)) := by
5330        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (mul_nonneg hA hn0)
5331    _ = _ := by rw [h2]; ring

5334theorem esq_lattice_bound {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
5335    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n →
5336      ∑' k : Lattice d, cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) k * (if (∀ c, (n : ℤ) ∣ k c) then 1 else 0) ≤
5337        C * (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d) := by
5338  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd
5339  have hH := halfInt_nonneg (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)
5340  set S0 : ℝ := ∑' m : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(1 / 2) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) with hS0
5341  have hS0s : Summable fun m : Lattice d => Real.exp (-(1 / 2) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) :=
5342    summable_gauss_lattice d (1 / 2) (by norm_num)
5343  have hS0n : 0 ≤ S0 := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
5344  refine ⟨subA d s * halfInt (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) * S0, by positivity, fun n hn => ?_⟩
5345  set F : Lattice d → ℝ := fun k => cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) k * (if (∀ c, (n : ℤ) ∣ k c) then 1 else 0)
5346    with hF
5347  have hε : 0 < 1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2 := by positivity
5348  have hn' : (n : ℤ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5349  have hinj : Function.Injective (fun m : Lattice d => (n : ℤ) • m) := by
5350    intro m m' h
5351    funext c
5352    have := congrFun h c
5353    simp only [Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul] at this
5354    exact mul_left_cancel₀ hn' this
5355  have hsupp : Function.support F ⊆ Set.range (fun m : Lattice d => (n : ℤ) • m) := by
5356    intro k hk
5357    have hdiv : ∀ c, (n : ℤ) ∣ k c := by
5358      by_contra h
5359      apply hk
5360      simp only [hF, h, if_false, mul_zero]
5361    refine ⟨fun c => k c / n, ?_⟩
5362    funext c
5363    simp only [Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
5364    exact Int.mul_ediv_cancel' (hdiv c)
5365  rw [← hinj.tsum_eq hsupp]
5366  have hFm : ∀ m : Lattice d, F ((n : ℤ) • m) = cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • m) := by
5367    intro m
5368    have : ∀ c, (n : ℤ) ∣ ((n : ℤ) • m) c := fun c => ⟨m c, rfl⟩
5369    simp [hF, this]
5370  simp_rw [hFm]
5371  set M : Lattice d → ℝ := fun m => subA d s * halfInt (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) * (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d) *
5372    Real.exp (-(1 / 2) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) with hM
5373  have hMs : Summable M := hS0s.mul_left _
5374  have hle : ∀ m : Lattice d, cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • m) ≤ M m := by
5375    intro m
5376    by_cases hm : m = 0
5377    · subst hm
5378      have : cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • (0 : Lattice d)) = 0 := by
5379        simp [cT]
5380      rw [this]
5381      simp only [hM]
5382      positivity
5383    · exact cT_lattice_le hd hs0 hsd hn m hm
5384  have hnn : ∀ m : Lattice d, 0 ≤ cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • m) :=
5385    fun m => cT_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε _
5386  have hsum : Summable fun m : Lattice d => cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • m) :=
5387    Summable.of_nonneg_of_le hnn hle hMs
5388  calc ∑' m : Lattice d, cT d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) ((n : ℤ) • m) ≤ ∑' m, M m :=
5389        hsum.tsum_le_tsum hle hMs
5390    _ = _ := by
5391        simp only [hM]
5392        rw [tsum_mul_left, hS0]
5393        ring

5396theorem psi_le_gauss (y : Euc d) (hy : ∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1 / 2) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) (hu1 : u ≤ 1) :
5397    psiFn (Torus.mk y) u ≤
5398      (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) * thetaB) ^ d * Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u)) * ‖y‖ ^ 2) := by
5399  have h1 := heatK_boxPt_le (d := d) hu hu1 (y := fun i => y i) hy
5400  have h2 : heatK d u (boxPt (fun i => y i)) = 1 + psiFn (boxPt (fun i => y i)) u := by
5401    unfold heatK; exact psi_eq _ hu
5402  have hmk : Torus.mk y = boxPt (fun i => y i) := rfl
5403  rw [hmk]
5404  have h3 : ∏ i, Gt u (y i) = (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) * thetaB) ^ d *
5405      Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u)) * ‖y‖ ^ 2) := by
5406    unfold Gt
5407    rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_const, Finset.prod_const,
5408      Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, ← Real.exp_sum]
5409    rw [← mul_pow]
5410    congr 2
5411    rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_sq_eq]
5412    simp only [Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
5413    rw [Finset.mul_sum]
5414    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
5415    ring
5416  rw [← h3]
5417  linarith [h1.2]

5419theorem lattice_diff_coords {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (i j : Fin d → Fin n) (c : Fin d) :
5420    ∃ t : ℤ, (n : ℝ) * (KernelGrad.centeredRep (latticePoint n i - latticePoint n j)) c = t := by
5421  have hn' : (n : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5422  set w : Torus d := latticePoint n i - latticePoint n j with hw
5423  set y0 : Euc d := WithLp.toLp 2 (fun c => ((((i c : ℕ) : ℝ) - ((j c : ℕ) : ℝ)) / n : ℝ)) with hy0
5424  have hmk : Torus.mk (KernelGrad.centeredRep w) = Torus.mk y0 := by
5425    rw [KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep]
5426    funext c
5427    simp only [hw, Pi.sub_apply, Torus.mk, hy0, latticePoint]
5428    rw [← AddCircle.coe_sub]
5429    congr 1
5430    ring
5431  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := KernelGrad.exists_lattice_of_mk_eq hmk
5432  refine ⟨((i c : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((j c : ℕ) : ℤ) + n * m c, ?_⟩
5433  have := congrArg (fun v : Euc d => v c) hm
5434  simp only [hy0, PiLp.add_apply, Lattice.toEuc] at this
5435  rw [this]
5436  push_cast
5437  field_simp

5440theorem gauss_shell_le {c v : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (hv : 1 ≤ v) :
5441    Summable (fun M : Lattice d => if M = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2 * v)) ∧
5442    ∑' M : Lattice d, (if M = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2 * v)) ≤
5443      Real.exp (-(c * v / 2)) * ∑' M : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(c / 2) * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2) := by
5444  have hS : Summable fun M : Lattice d => Real.exp (-(c / 2) * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2) :=
5445    summable_gauss_lattice d (c / 2) (by positivity)
5446  have hle : ∀ M : Lattice d, (if M = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2 * v)) ≤
5447      Real.exp (-(c * v / 2)) * Real.exp (-(c / 2) * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2) := by
5448    intro M
5449    by_cases hM : M = 0
5450    · simp [hM]
5451      positivity
5452    · simp only [hM, if_false]
5453      rw [← Real.exp_add]
5454      apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
5455      have h1 : 1 ≤ Lattice.enorm M ^ 2 := by
5456        have := one_le_enorm M hM
5457        nlinarith
5458      nlinarith [mul_nonneg hc.le (sub_nonneg.mpr h1), mul_nonneg hc.le (sub_nonneg.mpr hv),
5459        mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hc.le (sub_nonneg.mpr h1)) (sub_nonneg.mpr hv)]
5460  have hnn : ∀ M : Lattice d, 0 ≤ (if M = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2 * v)) := by
5461    intro M; split_ifs <;> positivity
5462  have hsum := Summable.of_nonneg_of_le hnn hle (hS.mul_left (Real.exp (-(c * v / 2))))
5463  refine ⟨hsum, ?_⟩
5464  calc _ ≤ ∑' M : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(c * v / 2)) * Real.exp (-(c / 2) * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2) :=
5465        hsum.tsum_le_tsum hle (hS.mul_left _)
5466    _ = _ := tsum_mul_left

5469theorem rpow_exp_bound {κ p : ℝ} (hκ : 0 < κ) (hp : 0 < p) :
5470    ∃ K0 : ℝ, 0 ≤ K0 ∧ ∀ (N u : ℝ), 0 < N → 0 < u →
5471      u ^ (-p) * Real.exp (-(κ / (N * u))) ≤ K0 * N ^ p := by
5472  refine ⟨κ ^ (-p) * (p ^ p * Real.exp (-p)), by positivity, fun N u hN hu => ?_⟩
5473  set v : ℝ := 1 / (N * u) with hv
5474  have hv0 : 0 < v := by positivity
5475  have h1 : u ^ (-p) = N ^ p * v ^ p := by
5476    have : u = (N * v)⁻¹ := by rw [hv]; field_simp
5477    rw [this, Real.inv_rpow (by positivity), Real.rpow_neg (by positivity), inv_inv,
5478      Real.mul_rpow hN.le hv0.le]
5479  have h2 := rpow_mul_exp_le hp (v := κ * v) (by positivity)
5480  have h3 : κ / (N * u) = κ * v := by rw [hv]; field_simp
5481  rw [h1, h3]
5482  rw [Real.mul_rpow hκ.le hv0.le] at h2
5483  have h4 : κ ^ (-p) * κ ^ p = 1 := by
5484    rw [← Real.rpow_add hκ]; simp
5485  have hκp : 0 < κ ^ p := by positivity
5486  calc N ^ p * v ^ p * Real.exp (-(κ * v)) = N ^ p * (κ ^ (-p) * (κ ^ p * v ^ p * Real.exp (-(κ * v)))) := by
5487        rw [← mul_assoc (κ ^ (-p)), ← mul_assoc (κ ^ (-p)), h4, one_mul]; ring
5488    _ ≤ N ^ p * (κ ^ (-p) * (p ^ p * Real.exp (-p))) := by gcongr
5489    _ = _ := by ring

5491/-- integer coordinates of the centered representative of `Λ_i - Λ_j`, scaled by `n` -/
5492def shellVec (n : ℕ) (i j : Fin (n ^ d)) : Lattice d :=
5493  fun c => round ((n : ℝ) * (KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)) c)
5494
5495theorem shellVec_spec {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (i j : Fin (n ^ d)) (c : Fin d) :
5496    (n : ℝ) * (KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)) c = (shellVec n i j c : ℝ) := by
5497  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := lattice_diff_coords hn (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm i) (finFunctionFinEquiv.symm j) c
5498  have : (n : ℝ) * (KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)) c = (t : ℝ) := ht
5499  rw [shellVec, this, round_intCast]
5500
5501theorem shellVec_norm {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (i j : Fin (n ^ d)) :
5502    ‖KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)‖ = Lattice.enorm (shellVec n i j) / n := by
5503  have hn' : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5504  have h : KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = ((n : ℝ)⁻¹) • Lattice.toEuc (shellVec n i j) := by
5505    ext c
5506    have := shellVec_spec hn i j c
5507    simp only [PiLp.smul_apply, Lattice.toEuc, smul_eq_mul]
5508    rw [← this]
5509    field_simp
5510  rw [h, norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr hn')]
5511  unfold Lattice.enorm
5512  field_simp
5513
5514theorem shellVec_injective {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (i : Fin (n ^ d)) :
5515    Function.Injective (shellVec n i) := by
5516  intro j j' h
5517  have hn' : (n : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5518  have hc : KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) =
5519      KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j') := by
5520    ext c
5521    have h1 := shellVec_spec hn i j c
5522    have h2 := shellVec_spec hn i j' c
5523    rw [h] at h1
5524    exact mul_left_cancel₀ hn' (h1.trans h2.symm)
5525  have := congrArg Torus.mk hc
5526  rw [KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep, KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep] at this
5527  have h3 : latCfg n j = latCfg n j' := by
5528    have := sub_right_injective this
5529    exact this
5530  exact latCfg_injective hn h3
5531
5532theorem shellVec_ne_zero {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {i j : Fin (n ^ d)} (hij : i ≠ j) :
5533    shellVec n i j ≠ 0 := by
5534  intro h0
5535  have hn' : (n : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hn.ne'
5536  have hc : KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = 0 := by
5537    ext c
5538    have h1 := shellVec_spec hn i j c
5539    rw [h0] at h1
5540    simp only [Pi.zero_apply, Int.cast_zero] at h1
5541    have := (mul_eq_zero.mp h1).resolve_left hn'
5542    simpa using this
5543  have h2 := congrArg Torus.mk hc
5544  rw [KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep] at h2
5545  have h3 : latCfg n i = latCfg n j := by
5546    have : Torus.mk (0 : Euc d) = 0 := by funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
5547    rw [this] at h2
5548    exact sub_eq_zero.mp h2
5549  exact hij (latCfg_injective hn h3)

5551theorem sqrt_div_pow {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) (d : ℕ) :
5552    (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u)) ^ d = (Real.sqrt Real.pi) ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) := by
5553  have h1 : Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) = Real.sqrt Real.pi * u ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 2) := by
5554    rw [Real.sqrt_div Real.pi_pos.le, Real.sqrt_eq_rpow u, div_eq_mul_inv, ← Real.rpow_neg hu.le]
5555    congr 2; ring
5556  have h2 : (u ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / 2)) ^ d = u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) := by
5557    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hu.le]
5558    congr 1; ring
5559  rw [h1, mul_pow, h2]

5561open CoercAux KernelAux Set in
5562theorem row_pointwise {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
5563    ∃ K : ℝ, 0 ≤ K ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ i : Fin (n ^ d), ∀ u : ℝ, 0 < u → u ≤ 1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2 →
5564      ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
5565        u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u ≤ K * (n : ℝ) ^ (s + 2) := by
5566  set c : ℝ := Real.pi ^ 2 / 2 with hc
5567  have hc0 : 0 < c := by positivity
5568  set p : ℝ := s / 2 + 1 with hp
5569  have hp0 : 0 < p := by positivity
5570  obtain ⟨K0, hK0, hK⟩ := rpow_exp_bound (κ := c / 2) (p := p) (by positivity) hp0
5571  set Sc : ℝ := ∑' M : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(c / 2) * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2) with hSc
5572  have hSc0 : 0 ≤ Sc := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
5573  have hB0 : 0 ≤ (Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d :=
5574    pow_nonneg (mul_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _) thetaB_nonneg) _
5575  refine ⟨(Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d * Sc * K0, by positivity, fun n hn i u hu hu1 => ?_⟩
5576  have hn' : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5577  have hu1' : u ≤ 1 := by
5578    refine hu1.trans ?_
5579    have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (n : ℝ) ^ 2 := by
5580      have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5581      nlinarith
5582    rw [div_le_one (by positivity)]; exact this
5583  set v : ℝ := 1 / ((n : ℝ) ^ 2 * u) with hv
5584  have hv1 : 1 ≤ v := by
5585    rw [hv, le_div_iff₀ (by positivity)]
5586    rw [le_div_iff₀ (by positivity)] at hu1
5587    linarith
5588  -- pointwise bound for each term
5589  set f : Lattice d → ℝ := fun M => Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm M ^ 2 * v) with hf
5590  have hterm : ∀ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
5591      u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u ≤
5592        (Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d * u ^ (-p) * (if shellVec n i j = 0 then 0 else f (shellVec n i j)) := by
5593    intro j hj
5594    have hij : i ≠ j := fun h => (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj) h.symm
5595    have hM0 : shellVec n i j ≠ 0 := shellVec_ne_zero hn hij
5596    rw [if_neg hM0]
5597    set y := KernelGrad.centeredRep (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) with hy
5598    have hbox := KernelGrad.abs_centeredRep_le (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)
5599    have hmk : Torus.mk y = latCfg n i - latCfg n j := KernelGrad.mk_centeredRep _
5600    have h1 := psi_le_gauss y hbox hu hu1'
5601    rw [hmk, mul_pow, sqrt_div_pow hu] at h1
5602    have hnorm : ‖y‖ = Lattice.enorm (shellVec n i j) / n := shellVec_norm hn i j
5603    have h2 : -(Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u)) * ‖y‖ ^ 2 = -c * Lattice.enorm (shellVec n i j) ^ 2 * v := by
5604      rw [hnorm, hc, hv]; field_simp
5605    rw [h2] at h1
5606    have hu0 : 0 < u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu _
5607    have hpow : u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) = u ^ (-p) := by
5608      rw [← Real.rpow_add hu, hp]; congr 1; ring
5609    calc u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u
5610        ≤ u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) * thetaB ^ d *
5611            Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm (shellVec n i j) ^ 2 * v)) :=
5612          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 hu0.le
5613      _ = (u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2)) * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * thetaB ^ d) *
5614            Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm (shellVec n i j) ^ 2 * v) := by ring
5615      _ = _ := by rw [hpow, mul_pow]; simp only [hf]; ring
5616  refine (Finset.sum_le_sum hterm).trans ?_
5617  rw [← Finset.mul_sum]
5618  obtain ⟨hgs, hgle⟩ := gauss_shell_le (d := d) hc0 hv1
5619  set g : Lattice d → ℝ := fun M => if M = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else f M with hg
5620  have hsumg : ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, (if shellVec n i j = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else f (shellVec n i j)) ≤
5621      ∑' M : Lattice d, g M := by
5622    have e : ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, (if shellVec n i j = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else f (shellVec n i j)) =
5623        ∑ M ∈ (Finset.univ.erase i).image (shellVec n i), g M := by
5624      rw [Finset.sum_image (fun x _ y _ h => shellVec_injective hn i h)]
5625    rw [e]
5626    refine hgs.sum_le_tsum _ (fun M _ => ?_)
5627    simp only [hg]; split_ifs <;> positivity
5628  have hgle' : ∑' M : Lattice d, g M ≤ Real.exp (-(c * v / 2)) * Sc := hgle
5629  have hup : 0 ≤ (Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d * u ^ (-p) := by
5630    have := Real.rpow_nonneg hu.le (-p)
5631    positivity
5632  have hkey := hK ((n : ℝ) ^ 2) u (by positivity) hu
5633  have hexp : c * v / 2 = c / 2 / ((n : ℝ) ^ 2 * u) := by rw [hv]; field_simp
5634  rw [hexp] at hgle'
5635  have hNp : (((n : ℝ) ^ 2) ^ p) = (n : ℝ) ^ (s + 2) := by
5636    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hn'.le, hp]
5637    congr 1; push_cast; ring
5638  rw [hNp] at hkey
5639  calc (Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d * u ^ (-p) * ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
5640        (if shellVec n i j = 0 then (0 : ℝ) else f (shellVec n i j))
5641      ≤ (Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d * u ^ (-p) * (Real.exp (-(c / 2 / ((n : ℝ) ^ 2 * u))) * Sc) :=
5642        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hsumg.trans hgle') hup
5643    _ = (Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d * Sc * (u ^ (-p) * Real.exp (-(c / 2 / ((n : ℝ) ^ 2 * u)))) := by ring
5644    _ ≤ (Real.sqrt Real.pi * thetaB) ^ d * Sc * (K0 * (n : ℝ) ^ (s + 2)) := by gcongr
5645    _ = _ := by ring

5648theorem nearF_row_le {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
5649    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ i : Fin (n ^ d),
5650      ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, nearF d s (1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2) (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) ≤
5651        C * (n : ℝ) ^ s := by
5652  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := row_pointwise (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
5653  have hA := subA_nonneg_one (d := d) (by omega) hs0 hsd
5654  refine ⟨subA d s * K, mul_nonneg hA hK0, fun n hn i => ?_⟩
5655  have hn' : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5656  set ε : ℝ := 1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2 with hε
5657  have hε0 : 0 < ε := by positivity
5658  have hdist : ∀ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, latCfg n i - latCfg n j ≠ 0 := by
5659    intro j hj h
5660    have hij : i ≠ j := fun h => (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj) h.symm
5661    exact hij (latCfg_injective hn (sub_eq_zero.mp h))
5662  have hnear : ∀ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, nearF d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) =
5663      subA d s * ∫ u in Ioc 0 ε, u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u := by
5664    intro j hj
5665    have hz := hdist j hj
5666    have := periodicRiesz_decomp (d := d) (s := s) (by omega) hs0 hsd hε0 _ hz
5667    simp only [nearF, hz, if_false]
5668    linarith
5669  have hint : ∀ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, IntegrableOn
5670      (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u) (Ioc 0 ε) := by
5671    intro j hj
5672    exact (integrableOn_psi hsd _ (hdist j hj)).mono_set (fun u hu => mem_Ioi.mpr hu.1)
5673  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hnear, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← integral_finset_sum _ hint]
5674  have hsumint : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
5675      u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u) (Ioc 0 ε) :=
5676    integrable_finset_sum _ hint
5677  have hle : ∫ u in Ioc 0 ε, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
5678      u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u ≤
5679        ∫ u in Ioc 0 ε, K * (n : ℝ) ^ (s + 2) := by
5680    refine setIntegral_mono_on hsumint (integrableOn_const (by simp)) measurableSet_Ioc (fun u hu => ?_)
5681    exact hK n hn i u hu.1 hu.2
5682  rw [setIntegral_const, Real.volume_real_Ioc_of_le hε0.le] at hle
5683  have hv : (ε - 0) * (K * (n : ℝ) ^ (s + 2)) = K * (n : ℝ) ^ s := by
5684    rw [hε, sub_zero, Real.rpow_add hn', Real.rpow_two]
5685    field_simp
5686  rw [smul_eq_mul, hv] at hle
5687  calc subA d s * ∫ u in Ioc 0 ε, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
5688        u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) u
5689      ≤ subA d s * (K * (n : ℝ) ^ s) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hle hA
5690    _ = _ := by ring

5693theorem psi_zero_ge {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
5694    Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) ≤ 1 + psiFn (0 : Torus d) u := by
5695  have h := psi_eq (0 : Torus d) hu
5696  have hb : (boxPt (0 : Fin d → ℝ) : Torus d) = 0 := by
5697    funext i; simp [boxPt]
5698  have h2 := theta_prod (0 : Fin d → ℝ) hu
5699  rw [hb] at h2
5700  rw [h2] at h
5701  simp only [Pi.zero_apply] at h
5702  rw [Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin] at h
5703  rw [← h]
5704  set S := ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u)) with hS
5705  have hsum : Summable fun n : ℤ => Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u)) :=
5706    (summable_exp_sq (Real.pi ^ 2 / u) (by positivity)).congr (fun n => by congr 1; ring)
5707  have hS1 : 1 ≤ S := by
5708    have := hsum.le_tsum 0 (fun j _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le)
5709    rw [hS]
5710    simpa using this
5711  rw [← sqrt_div_pow_eq d hu]
5712  calc Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) ^ d = (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) * 1) ^ d := by rw [mul_one]
5713    _ ≤ (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) * S) ^ d :=
5714        pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS1 (Real.sqrt_nonneg _)) d

5717theorem gT_zero_ge_zero (hd1 : 1 ≤ d) :
5718    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1 → 1 / 2 * Real.log (1 / ε) - C ≤ gT d 0 ε 0 := by
5719  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd1
5720  have hsd : (0 : ℝ) < d := hd0
5721  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd1 le_rfl hsd
5722  have hAP := subA_zero_mul (d := d) hd1
5723  set a : ℝ := ((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2 with ha_def
5724  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
5725  refine ⟨subA d 0 / a, fun ε hε hε1 => ?_⟩
5726  rw [gT_eq_integral hd1 le_rfl hsd hε 0]
5727  set f : ℝ → ℝ := fun u => u ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (0 : Torus d) u with hf
5728  have hcont : ContinuousOn f (Ioi 0) :=
5729    ContinuousOn.mul (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt ht)) (psi_continuousOn _)
5730  have hint1 : IntegrableOn f (Ioc ε 1) := by
5731    have : ContinuousOn f (Icc ε 1) := hcont.mono (fun u hu => lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1)
5732    exact this.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Ioc_subset_Icc_self
5733  have hG : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => psiLargeC d * (Real.exp (-u) * u ^ (a - 1))) (Ioi 0) :=
5734    (Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul (psiLargeC d)
5735  have hf0 : ∀ u : ℝ, 0 < u → 0 ≤ f u := fun u hu =>
5736    mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu _).le (psi_zero_nonneg hu)
5737  have hint2 : IntegrableOn f (Ioi 1) := by
5738    refine Integrable.mono' (hG.mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one)) ?_ ?_
5739    · exact (hcont.mono (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one)).aestronglyMeasurable measurableSet_Ioi
5740    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u hu => ?_)
5741      have hu1 : (1:ℝ) < u := hu
5742      have hu0 : 0 < u := by linarith
5743      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (hf0 u hu0)]
5744      have := psi_large (0 : Torus d) hu1.le
5745      have h2 := (abs_le.mp this).2
5746      have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
5747      rw [hf]
5748      unfold psiLargeC
5749      calc u ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (0 : Torus d) u
5750          ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * ((Real.exp 1 * ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) *
5751              Real.exp (-u)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 h3.le
5752        _ = _ := by ring
5753  have hsplit : ∫ u in Ioi ε, f u = (∫ u in Ioc ε 1, f u) + ∫ u in Ioi 1, f u := by
5754    rw [← Ioc_union_Ioi_eq_Ioi hε1, setIntegral_union Ioc_disjoint_Ioi_same measurableSet_Ioi hint1 hint2]
5755  have h2nn : 0 ≤ ∫ u in Ioi 1, f u :=
5756    setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioi fun u hu => hf0 u (lt_trans one_pos hu)
5757  have hlowint : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u⁻¹ - u ^ (a - 1)) (Ioc ε 1) := by
5758    have : ContinuousOn (fun u : ℝ => Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u⁻¹ - u ^ (a - 1)) (Icc ε 1) := by
5759      refine ContinuousOn.sub (continuousOn_const.mul ?_) ?_
5760      · exact continuousOn_inv₀.mono (fun u hu => ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1))
5761      · exact (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε ht.1)))
5762    exact this.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Ioc_subset_Icc_self
5763  have hlow : ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u⁻¹ - u ^ (a - 1)) ≤ ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, f u := by
5764    refine setIntegral_mono_on hlowint hint1 measurableSet_Ioc (fun u hu => ?_)
5765    have hu0 : 0 < u := lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1.le
5766    have h1 := psi_zero_ge (d := d) hu0
5767    have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
5768    have h5 : u ^ (a - 1) * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) = u⁻¹ := by
5769      rw [← Real.rpow_add hu0, ← Real.rpow_neg_one]
5770      congr 1; rw [ha_def]; ring
5771    rw [hf]
5772    have h6 : u ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) - 1) ≤
5773        u ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (0 : Torus d) u :=
5774      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) h3.le
5775    have h7 : u ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) - 1) =
5776        Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u⁻¹ - u ^ (a - 1) := by
5777      rw [← h5]; ring
5778    simp only
5779    linarith
5780  have hcalc : ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u⁻¹ - u ^ (a - 1)) =
5781      Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * Real.log (1 / ε) - (1 - ε ^ a) / a := by
5782    rw [← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hε1, intervalIntegral.integral_sub, intervalIntegral.integral_const_mul,
5783      integral_inv_of_pos hε one_pos, integral_rpow (Or.inl (by linarith))]
5784    · simp
5785    · exact (intervalIntegral.intervalIntegrable_inv (f := fun u : ℝ => u) (fun u hu => by
5786        rw [Set.uIcc_of_le hε1] at hu; exact ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1))
5787        continuousOn_id).const_mul _
5788    · refine ContinuousOn.intervalIntegrable ?_
5789      rw [Set.uIcc_of_le hε1]
5790      exact continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε ht.1))
5791  rw [hsplit]
5792  have hP : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d := by positivity
5793  have hlogε : 0 ≤ ε := hε.le
5794  have hεa : 0 ≤ ε ^ a := Real.rpow_nonneg hε.le _
5795  calc 1 / 2 * Real.log (1 / ε) - subA d 0 / a
5796      = subA d 0 * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * Real.log (1 / ε) - 1 / a) := by
5797        linear_combination (-Real.log (1 / ε)) * hAP
5798    _ ≤ subA d 0 * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * Real.log (1 / ε) - (1 - ε ^ a) / a) := by
5799        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hA
5800        have : (1 - ε ^ a) / a ≤ 1 / a := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) ha.le
5801        linarith
5802    _ ≤ _ := by
5803        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hA
5804        rw [← hcalc]; linarith

5807theorem gT_zero_nonneg {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
5808    0 ≤ gT d s ε 0 := by
5809  unfold gT

5811  rw [show Torus.cosMode k (0 : Torus d) = 1 by
5812    simp [Torus.cosMode, Torus.mode, UnitAddTorus.mFourier], mul_one]

5816theorem lattice_pair_sum {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
5817    ∃ C₂ : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₂ ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n →
5818      ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) ≤
5819        ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) * (C₂ * (n : ℝ) ^ s - (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0)) := by
5820  have hd1 : 1 ≤ d := by omega
5821  obtain ⟨CE, hCE0, hCE⟩ := esq_lattice_bound (d := d) (s := s) hd1 hs0 hsd
5822  obtain ⟨CN, hCN0, hCN⟩ := nearF_row_le (d := d) (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
5823  obtain ⟨CG, hCG⟩ := gT_zero_ge_zero (d := d) hd
5824  refine ⟨CE + CN + |CG|, by positivity, fun n hn => ?_⟩
5825  have hn' : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5826  set N : ℝ := ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) with hN
5827  have hN0 : 0 < N := by positivity
5828  have hN' : N = (n : ℝ) ^ d := by rw [hN]; push_cast; rfl
5829  set ε : ℝ := 1 / (n : ℝ) ^ 2 with hε
5830  have hε0 : 0 < ε := by positivity
5831  have hε1 : ε ≤ 1 := by
5832    rw [hε, div_le_one (by positivity)]
5833    have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
5834    nlinarith
5835  have hdist : ∀ i j : Fin (n ^ d), i ≠ j → latCfg n i - latCfg n j ≠ 0 := by
5836    intro i j hij h
5837    exact hij (latCfg_injective hn (sub_eq_zero.mp h))
5838  -- row decomposition
5839  have hrow : ∀ i : Fin (n ^ d),
5840      ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) =
5841        ∑ j, gT d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) - gT d s ε 0 +
5842          ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, nearF d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) := by
5843    intro i
5844    have h1 : ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) =
5845        ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, (gT d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) +
5846          nearF d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)) := by
5847      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
5848      have hij : i ≠ j := fun h => (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj) h.symm
5849      exact periodicRiesz_eq_add (ε := ε) _ (hdist i j hij)
5850    rw [h1, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
5851    have h3 : ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, gT d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) =
5852        ∑ j, gT d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) - gT d s ε 0 := by
5853      rw [← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ i)]
5854      simp
5855    rw [h3]
5856  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun i _ => hrow i), Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
5857  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
5858  -- the three terms
5859  have hE := gT_lattice_sum (d := d) (s := s) hd1 hs0 hsd hε0 hn
5860  have hE2 := hCE n hn
5861  have hEsum : ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) ≤ N * (CE * (n : ℝ) ^ s) := by
5862    have h1 : ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) =
5863        N ^ 2 * ∑' k : Lattice d, cT d s ε k * (if (∀ c, (n : ℤ) ∣ k c) then 1 else 0) := by
5864      rw [← hE, hN]; field_simp
5865    rw [h1]
5866    have h2 : N ^ 2 * (CE * (n : ℝ) ^ (s - d)) = N * (CE * (n : ℝ) ^ s) := by
5867      rw [hN', Real.rpow_sub hn', Real.rpow_natCast]
5868      field_simp
5869    rw [← h2]
5870    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hE2 (by positivity)
5871  have hNear : ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, nearF d s ε (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) ≤
5872      N * (CN * (n : ℝ) ^ s) := by
5873    calc _ ≤ ∑ _i : Fin (n ^ d), CN * (n : ℝ) ^ s := Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => hCN n hn i
5874      _ = _ := by rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul, hN]
5875  have hG0 : (if s = 0 then Real.log n - CG else 0) ≤ gT d s ε 0 := by
5876    split_ifs with hs
5877    · subst hs
5878      have := hCG ε hε0 hε1
5879      have h2 : 1 / 2 * Real.log (1 / ε) = Real.log n := by
5880        rw [hε, one_div_one_div, Real.log_pow]; push_cast; ring
5881      linarith
5882    · exact gT_zero_nonneg hd1 hs0 hsd hε0
5883  have hnpos : 0 < (n : ℝ) ^ s := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hn' _
5884  have hCG' : (if s = 0 then CG else 0) ≤ |CG| := by split_ifs; exact le_abs_self _; exact abs_nonneg _
5885  have hmain : -(gT d s ε 0) ≤ |CG| * (n : ℝ) ^ s - (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0) := by
5886    by_cases hs : s = 0
5887    · subst hs
5888      simp only [ite_true, Real.rpow_zero, mul_one] at hG0 ⊢
5889      linarith [le_abs_self CG]
5890    · simp only [hs, if_false] at hG0 ⊢
5891      have := mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg CG) hnpos.le
5892      linarith
5893  have hm2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hmain hN0.le
5894  rw [← hN]
5895  nlinarith [hEsum, hNear, hm2]
5896
5897/-- exact cancellation of the first-order term on the lattice -/
5898theorem first_order_zero {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (G : Torus d → Euc d) (hodd : ∀ x, G (-x) = -G x)
5899    (b : Fin (n ^ d) → Euc d) :
5900    ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, inner ℝ (G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)) (b i - b j) = 0 := by
5901  -- the force on each particle vanishes
5902  have hV : ∀ i : Fin (n ^ d), ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = 0 := by
5903    intro i
5904    choose τ hτ using fun j => latCfg_reflect hn i j
5905    refine Finset.sum_involution (fun j _ => τ j) ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
5906    · intro j hj
5907      have : latCfg n i - latCfg n (τ j) = -(latCfg n i - latCfg n j) := by
5908        rw [hτ j]; abel
5909      rw [this, hodd]; abel
5910    · intro j hj hne hjj
5911      apply hne
5912      have h1 : latCfg n (τ j) = latCfg n j := by rw [hjj]
5913      rw [hτ j] at h1
5914      have h2 : (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = -(latCfg n i - latCfg n j) := by
5915        have : (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) + (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = 0 := by
5916          calc (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) + (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)
5917              = (latCfg n i + latCfg n i - latCfg n j) - latCfg n j := by abel
5918            _ = 0 := by rw [h1]; simp
5919        exact eq_neg_of_add_eq_zero_left this
5920      have h3 : G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = -G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) := by
5921        conv_lhs => rw [h2]
5922        exact hodd _
5923      have h4 : (2 : ℝ) • G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = 0 := by
5924        rw [two_smul]; nth_rewrite 1 [h3]; exact neg_add_cancel _
5925      exact (smul_eq_zero.mp h4).resolve_left (by norm_num)
5926    · intro j hj
5927      have hji : j ≠ i := Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj
5928      rw [Finset.mem_erase]
5929      refine ⟨fun h => hji ?_, Finset.mem_univ _⟩
5930      have h1 : latCfg n (τ j) = latCfg n i := by rw [h]
5931      rw [hτ j] at h1
5932      apply latCfg_injective hn
5933      have : latCfg n j = latCfg n i := by
5934        have h5 := h1
5935        rw [sub_eq_iff_eq_add] at h5
5936        exact (add_left_cancel h5).symm
5937      exact this
5938    · intro j hj
5939      apply latCfg_injective hn
5940      rw [hτ (τ j), hτ j]
5941      abel
5942  have hV' : ∀ j : Fin (n ^ d), ∑ i ∈ Finset.univ.erase j, G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = 0 := by
5943    intro j
5944    have : ∀ i : Fin (n ^ d), G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) = -G (latCfg n j - latCfg n i) := by
5945      intro i
5946      rw [← hodd]; congr 1; abel
5947    simp_rw [this]
5948    rw [Finset.sum_neg_distrib, hV j, neg_zero]
5949  simp_rw [inner_sub_right, Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
5950  have hA : ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, inner ℝ (G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)) (b i) = 0 := by
5951    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i _ => ?_
5952    rw [← sum_inner, hV i, inner_zero_left]
5953  have hB : ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, inner ℝ (G (latCfg n i - latCfg n j)) (b j) = 0 := by
5954    rw [Finset.sum_comm' (t' := Finset.univ) (s' := fun j => Finset.univ.erase j)]
5955    · refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun j _ => ?_
5956      rw [← sum_inner, hV' j, inner_zero_left]
5957    · intro x y
5958      simp [Finset.mem_erase, ne_comm]
5959  rw [hA, hB]; simp

5962    perturbedLattice n κ a j = latCfg n j + Torus.mk (κ⁻¹ • a (latCfg n j)) := rfl

5965    perturbedLattice n κ a i - perturbedLattice n κ a j =
5966      (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) + Torus.mk (κ⁻¹ • (a (latCfg n i) - a (latCfg n j))) := by
5967  rw [perturbed_apply, perturbed_apply, smul_sub, KernelGrad.mk_sub]

5970theorem rho_sq_rpow {z : Torus d} (hz : z ≠ 0) {s : ℝ} :
5971    rho z ^ 2 * rho z ^ (-(s + 2)) = rho z ^ (-s) := by
5972  have h := rho_pos hz
5973  rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_add h]
5974  congr 1; push_cast; ring

5976theorem perturb_pair_sum {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
5977    {a : Torus d → Euc d} (ha : Torus.IsSmooth a) :
5978    ∃ κ₀ C₁ : ℝ, 0 < κ₀ ∧ 0 ≤ C₁ ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∀ κ : ℝ, κ₀ ≤ κ →
5979      ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
5980          periodicRiesz d s (perturbedLattice n κ a i - perturbedLattice n κ a j) ≤
5981        ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) +
5982          C₁ * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ 2 := by
5983  obtain ⟨Cτ, hCτ0, hCτ⟩ := taylor_torus (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
5984  obtain ⟨L, hL0, hL⟩ := lipschitz_a ha
5985  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := rpow_neg_le_add (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
5986  obtain ⟨C₂, hC₂0, hC₂⟩ := lattice_pair_sum (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
5987  set CK : ℝ := K + (d : ℝ) ^ 2 with hCK
5988  have hCK0 : 0 ≤ CK := by positivity
5989  refine ⟨max (2 * L) 1, Cτ * L ^ 2 * (C₂ + CK), lt_of_lt_of_le one_pos (le_max_right _ _), by positivity,
5990    fun n hn κ hκ => ?_⟩
5991  have hκ1 : 1 ≤ κ := (le_max_right _ _).trans hκ
5992  have hκ0 : 0 < κ := by linarith
5993  have hκL : L ≤ κ / 2 := by
5994    have := (le_max_left (2 * L) 1).trans hκ
5995    linarith
5996  have hκi : κ⁻¹ * L ≤ 1 / 2 := by
5997    rw [inv_mul_le_iff₀ hκ0]; linarith
5998  have hκi0 : 0 ≤ κ⁻¹ := inv_nonneg.mpr hκ0.le
5999  set N : ℝ := ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) with hN
6000  have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by
6001    rw [hN]; exact_mod_cast Nat.one_le_pow _ _ hn
6002  have hn' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
6003  -- pair bound
6004  have hpair : ∀ i j : Fin (n ^ d), i ≠ j →
6005      periodicRiesz d s (perturbedLattice n κ a i - perturbedLattice n κ a j) ≤
6006        periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) +
6007          κ⁻¹ * inner ℝ (periodicRieszGrad d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j))
6008            (a (latCfg n i) - a (latCfg n j)) +
6009          Cτ * L ^ 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * (periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) + CK) := by
6010    intro i j hij
6011    have hz : latCfg n i - latCfg n j ≠ 0 := fun h => hij (latCfg_injective hn (sub_eq_zero.mp h))
6012    set z := latCfg n i - latCfg n j with hzdef
6013    set δ : Euc d := κ⁻¹ • (a (latCfg n i) - a (latCfg n j)) with hδ
6014    have hρ := rho_pos hz
6015    have hδn : ‖δ‖ ≤ κ⁻¹ * L * rho z := by
6016      rw [hδ, norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg hκi0, mul_assoc]
6017      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hL _ _) hκi0
6018    have hδ2 : ‖δ‖ ≤ rho z / 2 := by
6019      refine hδn.trans ?_
6020      nlinarith
6021    have hT := hCτ z δ hz hδ2
6022    rw [← perturbed_sub] at hT
6023    have hT' := (abs_le.mp hT).2
6024    have hin : inner ℝ (periodicRieszGrad d s z) δ =
6025        κ⁻¹ * inner ℝ (periodicRieszGrad d s z) (a (latCfg n i) - a (latCfg n j)) := by
6026      rw [hδ, inner_smul_right]
6027    have hρd : rho z ^ 2 ≤ (d : ℝ) ^ 2 := by
6028      have := rho_le z
6029      have := rho_nonneg z
6030      nlinarith
6031    have hρK := hK z hz
6032    have hδsq : ‖δ‖ ^ 2 ≤ (κ⁻¹ * L) ^ 2 * rho z ^ 2 := by
6033      calc ‖δ‖ ^ 2 ≤ (κ⁻¹ * L * rho z) ^ 2 :=
6034            pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) hδn 2
6035        _ = _ := by ring
6036    have hfac : 0 ≤ rho z ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1 := by
6037      have := Real.rpow_nonneg hρ.le (-(s + 2)); linarith
6038    have hrem : Cτ * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 * (rho z ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1) ≤
6039        Cτ * L ^ 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * (periodicRiesz d s z + CK) := by
6040      calc Cτ * ‖δ‖ ^ 2 * (rho z ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1)
6041          ≤ Cτ * ((κ⁻¹ * L) ^ 2 * rho z ^ 2) * (rho z ^ (-(s + 2)) + 1) := by gcongr
6042        _ = Cτ * L ^ 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * (rho z ^ 2 * rho z ^ (-(s + 2)) + rho z ^ 2) := by ring
6043        _ = Cτ * L ^ 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * (rho z ^ (-s) + rho z ^ 2) := by rw [rho_sq_rpow hz]
6044        _ ≤ Cτ * L ^ 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * (periodicRiesz d s z + CK) :=
6045            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by rw [hCK]; linarith) (by positivity)
6046    rw [hin] at hT'
6047    linarith
6048  -- summation
6049  have hodd : ∀ x : Torus d, periodicRieszGrad d s (-x) = -periodicRieszGrad d s x :=
6050    fun x => KernelGrad.periodicRieszGrad_neg_of_lt hsd x
6051  have h0 := first_order_zero hn (periodicRieszGrad d s) hodd (fun i => a (latCfg n i))
6052  set SZ : ℝ := ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) with hSZ
6053  have hsum : ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
6054        periodicRiesz d s (perturbedLattice n κ a i - perturbedLattice n κ a j) ≤
6055      ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
6056        (periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) +
6057          κ⁻¹ * inner ℝ (periodicRieszGrad d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j))
6058            (a (latCfg n i) - a (latCfg n j)) +
6059          Cτ * L ^ 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * (periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n i - latCfg n j) + CK)) := by
6060    refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => Finset.sum_le_sum fun j hj => ?_
6061    exact hpair i j (fun h => (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj) h.symm)
6062  refine hsum.trans ?_
6063  simp only [Finset.sum_add_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum]
6064  rw [h0, mul_zero, add_zero]
6065  have e : ∑ x, ∑ x_1 ∈ Finset.univ.erase x, periodicRiesz d s (latCfg n x - latCfg n x_1) = SZ := rfl
6066  rw [e]
6067  have hcard : ∑ x : Fin (n ^ d), ∑ _y ∈ Finset.univ.erase x, CK ≤ N ^ 2 * CK := by
6068    calc ∑ x : Fin (n ^ d), ∑ _y ∈ Finset.univ.erase x, CK ≤ ∑ x : Fin (n ^ d), ∑ _y : Fin (n ^ d), CK :=
6069          Finset.sum_le_sum fun x _ => Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg (Finset.erase_subset _ _)
6070            (fun _ _ _ => hCK0)
6071      _ = N ^ 2 * CK := by
6072          simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
6073          rw [hN]; ring
6074  have hlog : 0 ≤ (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0) := by
6075    split_ifs
6076    · exact Real.log_nonneg hn'
6077    · exact le_rfl
6078  have hns : (n : ℝ) ^ s ≤ N := by
6079    have := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hn' hsd.le
6080    rw [Real.rpow_natCast] at this
6081    rw [hN]; push_cast; exact this
6082  have hSZ2 : SZ ≤ C₂ * N ^ 2 := by
6083    refine (hC₂ n hn).trans ?_
6084    rw [← hN]
6085    have hN0 : 0 ≤ N := by linarith
6086    calc N * (C₂ * (n : ℝ) ^ s - (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0)) ≤ N * (C₂ * (n : ℝ) ^ s) := by
6087          gcongr; linarith
6088      _ ≤ N * (C₂ * N) := by gcongr
6089      _ = C₂ * N ^ 2 := by ring
6090  have hfac : 0 ≤ Cτ * L ^ 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 := by positivity
6091  have h1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (add_le_add hSZ2 hcard) hfac
6092  nlinarith [h1]
6093
6094theorem rieszEnergy_eq_pairs {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {N : ℕ}
6095    (X : Config d N) :
6096    rieszEnergy d s X volume = (1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2)) *
6097      ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (X i - X j) := by
6098  haveI : (volume : Measure (Torus d)).IsNegInvariant :=
6099    MeasureTheory.Measure.pi.isNegInvariant (fun _ : Fin d => (volume : Measure UnitAddCircle))
6100  have h0 : ∀ x : Torus d, ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) = 0 := by
6101    intro x
6102    rw [integral_sub_left_eq_self (fun z => periodicRiesz d s z) (volume : Measure (Torus d)) x]
6103    rcases Nat.eq_or_lt_of_le hd with hd1 | hd2
6104    · subst hd1
6105      exact (integral_periodicRiesz_d1 hs0 (by simpa using hsd)).2
6106    · exact (integral_periodicRiesz hd2 hs0 hsd).2
6107  unfold rieszEnergy modulatedEnergyKernel modulatedEnergy
6108  simp [h0]
