6110theorem perturbedLattice_energy_aux {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (C₀ : ℝ)
6111    {a : Torus d → Euc d} (ha : Torus.IsSmooth a) :
6112    ∃ κ₀ C : ℝ, 0 < κ₀ ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 2 ≤ n → ∀ κ : ℝ, κ₀ ≤ κ →
6113      rieszEnergy d s (perturbedLattice n κ a) volume + oN d s C₀ (n ^ d) ≤
6114        C * (κ⁻¹ ^ 2 + ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) := by
6115  obtain ⟨κ₀, C₁, hκ₀, hC₁0, hC₁⟩ := perturb_pair_sum (s := s) hd hs0 hsd ha
6116  obtain ⟨C₂, hC₂0, hC₂⟩ := lattice_pair_sum (s := s) hd hs0 hsd
6117  refine ⟨κ₀, C₁ / 2 + C₂ / 2 + |C₀|, hκ₀, fun n hn κ hκ => ?_⟩
6118  have hn0 : 0 < n := by omega
6119  have hn' : (0 : ℝ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn0
6120  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
6121  set N : ℝ := ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) with hN
6122  have hN' : N = (n : ℝ) ^ d := by rw [hN]; push_cast; rfl
6123  have hN0 : 0 < N := by positivity
6124  rw [rieszEnergy_eq_pairs hd hs0 hsd]
6125  have h1 := hC₁ n hn0 κ hκ
6126  have h2 := hC₂ n hn0
6127  have hκ0 : 0 ≤ κ⁻¹ ^ 2 := by positivity
6128  -- the pair sum
6129  set SY := ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
6130    periodicRiesz d s (perturbedLattice n κ a i - perturbedLattice n κ a j) with hSY
6131  have hSY2 : SY ≤ N * (C₂ * (n : ℝ) ^ s - (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0)) + C₁ * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * N ^ 2 :=
6132    h1.trans (by linarith [h2])
6133  have hpow : N ^ (s / d - 1) = (n : ℝ) ^ s / N := by
6134    rw [Real.rpow_sub hN0, Real.rpow_one, hN', ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hn'.le]
6135    congr 2
6136    field_simp
6137  have hF : 1 / (2 * N ^ 2) * SY ≤ C₂ / 2 * N ^ (s / d - 1) - (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0) / (2 * N) +
6138      C₁ / 2 * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 := by
6139    have : 1 / (2 * N ^ 2) * SY ≤ 1 / (2 * N ^ 2) * (N * (C₂ * (n : ℝ) ^ s -
6140        (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0)) + C₁ * κ⁻¹ ^ 2 * N ^ 2) :=
6141      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hSY2 (by positivity)
6142    refine this.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
6143    rw [hpow]
6144    field_simp
6145  have hoN : oN d s C₀ (n ^ d) = C₀ * N ^ (s / d - 1) + (if s = 0 then Real.log n / (2 * N) else 0) := by
6146    unfold oN
6147    congr 1
6148    split_ifs with hs
6149    · rw [← hN]
6150      have : Real.log N = d * Real.log n := by rw [hN', Real.log_pow]
6151      rw [this]
6152      field_simp
6153    · rfl
6154  rw [hoN]
6155  have hite : (if s = 0 then Real.log n / (2 * N) else 0) = (if s = 0 then Real.log n else 0) / (2 * N) := by
6156    split_ifs <;> simp
6157  rw [hite]
6158  have hP : 0 ≤ N ^ (s / d - 1) := Real.rpow_nonneg hN0.le _
6159  nlinarith [mul_nonneg hC₁0 hP, mul_nonneg hC₂0 hκ0, mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg C₀) hκ0,
6160    mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.mpr (le_abs_self C₀)) hP]
6161
6162end LatticeEnergyAux

6164/-- Example A.39 (source lines 11608-11612), energy level (external: Serfaty, *Lectures on
6165Coulomb and Riesz gases*, Ch. 4-5; Sandier-Serfaty 2015; Petrache-Serfaty 2017; only the
6166order of magnitude is used): for `a ∈ C^∞` and `κ_N` large enough that `z ↦ z + κ⁻¹a(z)` is a
6167bi-Lipschitz perturbation, `F_N(Y_N, 1) + o_N ≤ C(κ_N^{-2} + N^{s/d-1})`. -/
6168theorem perturbedLattice_energy {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (C₀ : ℝ)
6169    (a : Torus d → Euc d) (ha : Torus.IsSmooth a) :
6170    ∃ κ₀ C : ℝ, 0 < κ₀ ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, 2 ≤ n → ∀ κ : ℝ, κ₀ ≤ κ →
6171      rieszEnergy d s (perturbedLattice n κ a) volume + oN d s C₀ (n ^ d) ≤
6172        C * (κ⁻¹ ^ 2 + ((n ^ d : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) := by
6173  exact LatticeEnergyAux.perturbedLattice_energy_aux hd hs0 hsd C₀ ha

6175/-! ### `H:eq:balanced-weights` (source lines 636-646) -/

6177namespace InitialDataAux

6179lemma scale_eq (N : ℕ) (hN : 1 ≤ N) (κ invβ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ) (hinvβ : 0 ≤ invβ) :
6180    Ascale N κ invβ = (if invβ = 0 then 1 else min 1 (κ * Real.sqrt (invβ / N))⁻¹) ∧
6181    qScale N κ invβ = min ((κ * Real.sqrt (invβ / N)) ^ 2) 1 := by
6182  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
6183  by_cases h : invβ = 0
6184  · subst h
6185    simp [Ascale, qScale]
6186  · have hpos : 0 < invβ := lt_of_le_of_ne hinvβ (Ne.symm h)
6187    have hr : 0 < Real.sqrt (invβ / N) := Real.sqrt_pos.2 (div_pos hpos hNpos)
6188    have hsq : Real.sqrt (N / invβ) = (Real.sqrt (invβ / N))⁻¹ := by
6189      rw [← Real.sqrt_inv, inv_div]
6190    have hsig : sigmaScale N κ invβ = min κ (Real.sqrt (invβ / N))⁻¹ := by
6191      simp [sigmaScale, h, hsq]
6192    constructor
6193    · simp only [Ascale, h, ite_false, hsig]
6194      rw [div_eq_inv_mul, mul_min_of_nonneg _ _ (inv_nonneg.2 hκ.le), inv_mul_cancel₀ hκ.ne',
6195        mul_inv, mul_comm]
6196    · simp only [qScale, h, ite_false, hsig]
6197      have hsq2 : (Real.sqrt (invβ / N)) ^ 2 = invβ / N := Real.sq_sqrt (div_pos hpos hNpos).le
6198      have : min κ (Real.sqrt (invβ / N))⁻¹ ^ 2 = min (κ ^ 2) ((Real.sqrt (invβ / N))⁻¹ ^ 2) := by
6199        rcases le_total κ (Real.sqrt (invβ / N))⁻¹ with h1 | h1
6200        · rw [min_eq_left h1, min_eq_left (pow_le_pow_left₀ hκ.le h1 2)]
6201        · rw [min_eq_right h1, min_eq_right (pow_le_pow_left₀ (inv_nonneg.2 hr.le) h1 2)]
6202      have ht : 0 < invβ / N := div_pos hpos hNpos
6203      rw [this, inv_pow, hsq2, mul_pow, hsq2, mul_div_assoc, min_mul_of_nonneg _ _ ht.le,
6204        inv_mul_cancel₀ ht.ne', mul_comm]
6205
6206
6207lemma A_eq_of_pos (N : ℕ) (hN : 1 ≤ N) (κ invβ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ) (hinvβ : 0 ≤ invβ)
6208    (hr : 0 < κ * Real.sqrt (invβ / N)) :
6209    Ascale N κ invβ = min 1 (κ * Real.sqrt (invβ / N))⁻¹ := by
6210  rw [(scale_eq N hN κ invβ hκ hinvβ).1]
6211  have : invβ ≠ 0 := by
6212    rintro rfl
6213    simp at hr
6214  simp [this]
6215
6216lemma A_eq_one (N : ℕ) (hN : 1 ≤ N) (κ invβ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ) (hinvβ : 0 ≤ invβ)
6217    (hr : κ * Real.sqrt (invβ / N) < 1) : Ascale N κ invβ = 1 := by
6218  rw [(scale_eq N hN κ invβ hκ hinvβ).1]
6219  by_cases h : invβ = 0
6220  · simp [h]
6221  · have hpos : 0 < invβ := lt_of_le_of_ne hinvβ (Ne.symm h)
6222    have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
6223    have h0 : 0 < κ * Real.sqrt (invβ / N) := mul_pos hκ (Real.sqrt_pos.2 (div_pos hpos hNpos))
6224    simp only [h, ite_false]
6225    exact min_eq_left (one_le_inv_iff₀.2 ⟨h0, hr.le⟩)
6226
6227end InitialDataAux

6229open InitialDataAux
6230/-- `H:eq:balanced-weights` (source lines 640-645): if `κ_N / √(Nβ_N) → c ∈ (0, ∞)`
6231(written `κ_N √(β_N⁻¹/N) → c`), then `A_N → min{1, c⁻¹}` and `q_N → min{c², 1}`. -/
6232theorem balanced_weights (κ invβ : ℕ → ℝ) (hκ : ∀ N, 0 < κ N) (hinvβ : ∀ N, 0 ≤ invβ N)
6233    (c : ℝ) (hc : 0 < c)
6234    (hratio : Tendsto (fun N => κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N)) atTop (𝓝 c)) :
6235    Tendsto (fun N => Ascale N (κ N) (invβ N)) atTop (𝓝 (min 1 c⁻¹)) ∧
6236      Tendsto (fun N => qScale N (κ N) (invβ N)) atTop (𝓝 (min (c ^ 2) 1)) := by
6237  have hev : ∀ᶠ N : ℕ in atTop, 1 ≤ N ∧ 0 < κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N) :=
6238    (eventually_ge_atTop 1).and (hratio.eventually (lt_mem_nhds hc))
6239  constructor
6240  · have h1 : Tendsto (fun N => min 1 (κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N))⁻¹) atTop (𝓝 (min 1 c⁻¹)) :=
6241      (tendsto_const_nhds.min (hratio.inv₀ hc.ne'))
6242    refine h1.congr' ?_
6243    filter_upwards [hev] with N hN
6244    exact (A_eq_of_pos N hN.1 _ _ (hκ N) (hinvβ N) hN.2).symm
6245  · have h1 : Tendsto (fun N => min ((κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N)) ^ 2) 1) atTop
6246        (𝓝 (min (c ^ 2) 1)) := (hratio.pow 2).min tendsto_const_nhds
6247    refine h1.congr' ?_
6248    filter_upwards [hev] with N hN
6249    exact ((scale_eq N hN.1 _ _ (hκ N) (hinvβ N)).2).symm
6250
6251/-- Source lines 638-640: if `κ_N / √(Nβ_N) → 0` then `A_N → 1` and `q_N → 0` (including
6252`β_N = ∞`, `invβ = 0`). -/
6253theorem balanced_weights_zero (κ invβ : ℕ → ℝ) (hκ : ∀ N, 0 < κ N) (hinvβ : ∀ N, 0 ≤ invβ N)
6254    (hratio : Tendsto (fun N => κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N)) atTop (𝓝 0)) :
6255    Tendsto (fun N => Ascale N (κ N) (invβ N)) atTop (𝓝 1) ∧
6256      Tendsto (fun N => qScale N (κ N) (invβ N)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
6257  have hev : ∀ᶠ N : ℕ in atTop, 1 ≤ N ∧ κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N) < 1 :=
6258    (eventually_ge_atTop 1).and (hratio.eventually (gt_mem_nhds one_pos))
6259  constructor
6260  · refine tendsto_const_nhds.congr' ?_
6261    filter_upwards [hev] with N hN
6262    exact (A_eq_one N hN.1 _ _ (hκ N) (hinvβ N) hN.2).symm
6263  · have h1 : Tendsto (fun N => min ((κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N)) ^ 2) 1) atTop
6264        (𝓝 (min ((0:ℝ) ^ 2) 1)) := (hratio.pow 2).min tendsto_const_nhds
6265    simp only [ne_eq, OfNat.ofNat_ne_zero, not_false_eq_true, zero_pow, zero_le_one, min_eq_left] at h1
6266    refine h1.congr' ?_
6267    filter_upwards [hev] with N hN
6268    exact ((scale_eq N hN.1 _ _ (hκ N) (hinvβ N)).2).symm
6269
6270/-- Source line 646: if `κ_N / √(Nβ_N) → ∞` then `A_N → 0` and `q_N → 1`. -/
6271theorem balanced_weights_infty (κ invβ : ℕ → ℝ) (hκ : ∀ N, 0 < κ N) (hinvβ : ∀ N, 0 ≤ invβ N)
6272    (hratio : Tendsto (fun N => κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N)) atTop atTop) :
6273    Tendsto (fun N => Ascale N (κ N) (invβ N)) atTop (𝓝 0) ∧
6274      Tendsto (fun N => qScale N (κ N) (invβ N)) atTop (𝓝 1) := by
6275  have hev : ∀ᶠ N : ℕ in atTop, 1 ≤ N ∧ 1 < κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N) :=
6276    (eventually_ge_atTop 1).and (hratio.eventually (eventually_gt_atTop 1))
6277  constructor
6278  · have h1 : Tendsto (fun N => min 1 (κ N * Real.sqrt (invβ N / N))⁻¹) atTop (𝓝 (min 1 0)) :=
6279      tendsto_const_nhds.min (tendsto_inv_atTop_zero.comp hratio)
6280    rw [min_eq_right zero_le_one] at h1
6281    refine h1.congr' ?_
6282    filter_upwards [hev] with N hN
6283    exact (A_eq_of_pos N hN.1 _ _ (hκ N) (hinvβ N) (one_pos.trans hN.2)).symm
6284  · refine tendsto_const_nhds.congr' ?_
6285    filter_upwards [hev] with N hN
6286    rw [(scale_eq N hN.1 _ _ (hκ N) (hinvβ N)).2]
6287    exact (min_eq_right (by nlinarith [hN.2])).symm
6288
6289end HRS
